## 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流 [[Back]](#toc)

关注连续视觉语言模型、连续视觉 token、视觉 VAE/ViT latent、flow matching 与多模态理解。

### [1] [Post-Hoc Sparse Coding of Latent Communication Between Vision-Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10198)
*Di Wu, Xiaohui Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流

Importance: 值得关注 (71.18) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型智能体间潜空间通信的可压缩性。通过在冻结的 Vision Wormhole 激活上拟合后验稀疏自编码器（SAE），发现其通信通道存在大量冗余：仅用 k=4 个活跃系数即可将传输字节减少 128 倍，且下游任务精度几乎不变（49.85%→49.77%）。4096 元素字典仅使用 50 个特征，任务级活跃集平均 Jaccard 相似度达 0.906，表明潜空间通信具有强后验可压缩性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间通信允许异构视觉语言模型智能体交换连续表征而无需序列化为文本，但 Vision Wormhole 将每条消息都作为固定大小的稠密张量传输，无论内容如何。作者观察到固定容量稠密张量的有效信息密度并不固定，部分消息可能只使用可用表征自由度的一小部分，因此通信通道可能大幅可压缩。

Method: 在冻结的 Vision Wormhole 激活上拟合后验稀疏自编码器（post-hoc sparse autoencoder），测量重建质量、下游效用、特征复用和 token 级干预。采用 uint16 索引/float16 值稀疏载荷，每 token 使用 k=4 个活跃系数，在九个推理基准上评估。

Result: 相对原始 float32 传输，稀疏载荷将传输字节减少 128 倍；单次运行评估中七个非 AIME 任务平均准确率从 49.85% 变为 49.77%。拟合的 4096 元素字典仅使用 50 个特征，任务级活跃集平均成对 Jaccard 相似度为 0.906。

Conclusion: 测量结果确立了相对原始传输的强后验可压缩性，但尚未隔离稀疏编码相对于位置选择、降低精度、低秩结构或 SAE 优化效应的增量贡献。结果激励了匹配载荷比较以及载荷随每条消息实际使用信息自适应的通信机制。

Classification reason: 论文研究视觉语言模型智能体之间通过连续潜空间（latent space）进行通信，并对 Vision Wormhole 的连续激活进行稀疏编码压缩。这属于连续视觉语言模型与视觉潜空间表征的范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 continuous_vlm_latent_flow（连续 VLM 与视觉潜空间流）。

Importance reason: Directly targets continuous VLM latent communication compressibility, a core priority subtopic with strong efficiency implications. Signals: continuous VLM, latent communication, sparse autoencoder, 128x compression, model efficiency

Abstract: Latent-space communication allows heterogeneous vision-language model agents to exchange continuous representations without serializing visual and reasoning states into text. Vision Wormhole realizes this approach by translating visual features into a universal latent representation that can be consumed by another model, but every message is transported as a dense tensor of the same size regardless of its content. A fixed-capacity dense tensor therefore need not have a fixed effective information density: some messages may use only a small fraction of the available representational degrees of freedom. This observation suggests that the communication channel may be substantially compressible. We study its redundancy by fitting a post-hoc sparse autoencoder to frozen Vision Wormhole activations and measuring reconstruction, downstream utility, feature reuse, and token-level interventions across nine reasoning benchmarks. Relative to the original float32 transport, a uint16-index/float16-value sparse payload with k=4 active coefficients per token reduces the transmitted bytes by 128x. In a single-run evaluation, the seven-task non-AIME mean accuracy changes from 49.85% to 49.77%. The fitted 4096-element dictionary uses only 50 features, and task-level active sets have a mean pairwise Jaccard similarity of 0.906. These measurements establish strong post-hoc compressibility relative to the original transport, but do not yet isolate the incremental contribution of sparse coding from position selection, reduced precision, low-rank structure, or SAE optimization effects. The results motivate matched-payload comparisons and communication mechanisms whose payload adapts to the information used by each message.

</details>

### [2] [Continuous Interaction Diffusion: A Diffusion-Native Runtime for Asynchronous Tool-Augmented Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10438)
*Yuhang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 高优先级 (93.28) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Continuous Interaction Diffusion (CID)，一种扩散原生的模型-运行时架构，将外部工具交互集成到扩散语言模型的迭代去噪过程中。CID 分离只读事实通道、由 Typed Cognitive Tensor 表示的思维通道和显示通道，允许信息需求在文本/JSON 调用完全序列化之前出现，从而在去噪继续的同时启动外部读取，并将返回结果投影到演化的思维状态中，可修正早期认知与显示区域。论文形式化描述了架构、运行时与训练目标，并定义了任务质量与端到端效率的评测协议，但本文聚焦只读工具且不做经验性能声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型天然适配工具调用（发出调用、等待结果、继续生成），但扩散语言模型通过并行反复精炼输出的多个部分进行推理，这种"停止-恢复"的交互模式会强制在推理稳定前做出工具决策、延迟有用观察、引入冗余精炼与工具执行，损害任务准确性与推理效率。

Method: CID 将工具交互集成进迭代去噪过程，分离三个通道：模型只读的事实通道、由 Typed Cognitive Tensor 表示的思维通道、以及显示通道。信息需求可在调用完全序列化前出现，使感知绑定能启动外部读取而无需中断去噪；返回结果被投影到演化的思维状态中，可修正早期认知与显示区域；持久绑定复用静态结果避免重复外部执行，并在需要时刷新变化源。论文形式化架构、运行时与训练目标，并定义评测协议。

Result: 本文为概念性/架构性论文，聚焦只读工具，未提供经验性能声明。主要贡献在于形式化 CID 架构、运行时与训练目标，并定义任务质量与端到端效率的评测协议。

Conclusion: CID 旨在更早暴露证据、将工具延迟与模型计算重叠、减少重复外部工作，并在新证据到达后保留有用计算，为扩散语言模型提供更自然的工具增强推理范式。本文为系列工作的第一篇，聚焦只读工具，后续将补充经验验证。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Continuous Interaction Diffusion (CID)，一种扩散原生（diffusion-native）的模型-运行时架构，将工具交互集成到迭代去噪过程中，属于扩散语言模型（dLLM）在连续潜空间中的建模与推理。这与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"连续语言模型与语言流"高度相关，该子主题涵盖扩散语言模型与连续 latent 语言建模。

Importance reason: Novel diffusion-native runtime for tool-augmented reasoning directly advances continuous language model interaction, though it lacks empirical validation. Signals: continuous language model, diffusion language model, tool-augmented reasoning, asynchronous interaction, runtime architecture

Abstract: Large language models increasingly rely on external tools to access up-to-date information, perform computation, and interact with the outside world. For autoregressive models, tool use naturally fits the generation process: the model emits a tool call, waits for the result, and then continues generating. Diffusion language models (dLLMs), however, reason by repeatedly refining many parts of their output in parallel, making this stop-and-resume interaction pattern unnecessarily restrictive. It can force tool decisions before the model's reasoning has stabilized, delay useful observations until a discrete call finishes, and introduce redundant refinement and tool execution, potentially hurting both task accuracy and inference efficiency. We introduce Continuous Interaction Diffusion (CID), a diffusion-native model--runtime architecture that integrates tool interaction into iterative denoising. CID separates a model-read-only fact channel, a thought channel represented by a Typed Cognitive Tensor, and a display channel. Information needs can emerge before a textual or JSON call is fully serialized, allowing perceptual bindings to launch external reads while denoising continues. Returned results are projected into the evolving thought state and can revise earlier cognition and display regions. Persistent bindings reuse static results without repeated external execution and refresh changing sources when needed. CID is designed to expose evidence earlier, overlap tool latency with model computation, reduce duplicate external work, and preserve useful computation after new evidence arrives. We formalize the architecture, runtime, and training objectives, and define an evaluation protocol for task quality and end-to-end efficiency. This first paper focuses on read-only tools and makes no empirical performance claims.

</details>

### [3] [Whole-Body Planning for Humanoids Navigating Confined Spaces via Self-Collision Avoidance References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10220)
*Carlos Gonzalez, Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一个三阶段全身规划框架，直接在运动学可达的刚体体积上构建运动学路径规划，结合可微碰撞规避与可达性约束，生成体积感知引导以驱动全阶轨迹优化器，并训练残差强化学习策略实现鲁棒在线执行，在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在高度受限环境中导航需要同时处理密集环境障碍、复杂自碰撞约束和多接触动态可行性。传统轨迹优化器在受限空间中常陷入局部最小值，因为基于粒子抽象的样条难以覆盖大碰撞空间。

Method: 提出三阶段全身规划框架：(1) 直接在运动学可达的刚体体积上构建运动学路径规划；(2) 将可微碰撞规避集成到可达性约束公式中，合成体积感知引导；(3) 用这些引导驱动全阶轨迹优化器，并将优化轨迹作为参考训练残差强化学习策略用于在线执行。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上，跨三个基准测试台验证，超过 NIST 应急响应标准，达到受限空间比 Cr<1.5。框架在 12-18 秒任务中生成可行轨迹，具有复杂脚和手接触，标准基线失败；学习策略在物理仿真中大量域随机化下成功跟踪这些计划。

Conclusion: 通过将体积感知的碰撞规避与可达性约束结合进全身规划，能够可靠地引导全阶轨迹优化器在受限空间中生成可行轨迹，并可通过残差强化学习实现鲁棒在线执行。

Classification reason: 论文面向人形机器人在受限空间中的全身运动规划与自碰撞规避，属于具身智能中的动作规划与控制，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Solid robotics planning work, but it is outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generative models and video/world-model generation. Signals: humanoid whole-body planning, self-collision avoidance, trajectory optimization, residual RL, confined-space navigation

Abstract: Humanoid locomotion in highly confined environments requires navigating dense environmental obstacles and complex self-collision bounds while maintaining multi-contact dynamic feasibility. Traditional trajectory optimizers frequently struggle in these restricted spaces, as navigating the large collision space with splines on particle abstractions is insufficient and leads to poor local minima. To address this, we propose a three-stage whole-body planning framework that formulates kinematic path planning directly over kinematically reachable rigid-body volumes. By integrating differentiable collision avoidance into a reachability-constrained formulation, our framework synthesizes volume-informed guides that reliably guide a full-order trajectory optimizer over long horizons. We show that these optimized plans serve as high-quality references to train a residual reinforcement learning policy for robust online execution. We validate our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid across three benchmark testbeds exceeding NIST emergency response standards, achieving restricted confinement ratios ($C_r < 1.5$). Our framework generates feasible trajectories across 12-to-18-second tasks with complex foot and hand contacts where standard baselines fail, while the learned policy successfully tracks these plans under extensive domain randomization in physics simulation.

</details>


### [4] [FACT: Failure-Aware Causal Training for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10232)
*Quanquan Peng, Yutong Liang, Rui Yan, Nicklas Hansen, Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LG, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (90.8) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FACT 是一种因果世界-动作模型，通过动作条件化的未来视频与任务进度预测，将失败轨迹纳入训练，使世界模型能预测坏动作的后果，减少成功偏置的未来幻觉，并在仿真与真实双臂操作任务上超越多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）主要基于成功演示训练，世界模型几乎没有理由预测坏动作的后果，导致在失败动作下产生成功偏置的未来幻觉，限制了动作生成的鲁棒性。

Method: FACT 构建因果世界-动作模型，以执行的动作作为条件，预测未来视频和任务进度。该动作条件化接口允许失败轨迹监督动作后果，将坏动作转化为有效的未来目标而非丢弃。失败感知训练使进度预测器同时感知成功与失败的动作结果，推理时可选择用于对采样动作候选进行评分。

Result: 在仿真与真实双臂操作任务上的大量实验表明，FACT 优于多种现有基线，随着失败数据纳入训练性能持续提升，并显著减少坏动作下的成功偏置未来幻觉。

Conclusion: 将失败轨迹纳入世界-动作模型训练能够有效提升动作生成的鲁棒性，动作条件化的因果接口是让世界模型学习失败后果的关键设计。

Classification reason: 论文提出因果世界-动作模型（World-Action Model），将未来视频预测与动作生成结合，用于机器人双臂操作任务，属于具身智能中的世界模型与规划控制方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model。

Importance reason: Directly advances world-action models with failure-aware causal training, aligning with the user's world model and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: world model, neural simulator, failure-aware training, action-conditioned video prediction, bimanual manipulation

Abstract: Recent world-action models (WAMs) show that co-training policies with future prediction can provide physical priors for action generation. Building on the future-prediction ability of video models, many WAMs generate future videos and recover actions with inverse-dynamics models, or use these predicted videos as goal conditions for action generation. In both cases, the world model is trained mostly on successful demonstrations and has little reason to predict the consequences of bad actions. We introduce FACT, a causal World-Action Model that predicts future video and task progress conditioned on the executed action. This action-conditioned interface allows failure rollouts to supervise action consequences, turning bad actions into valid future targets rather than being discarded. Failure-aware training makes the progress predictor aware of both successful and failed action outcomes, which can optionally be used to score sampled action candidates at inference. Extensive experiments on simulation and real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show that FACT outperforms many existing baselines, improves as failure data are incorporated into training, and reduces success-biased future hallucination under bad actions. See more details at

</details>


### [5] [Lost in Reconstruction: Aligning Action Representations with Language in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10484)
*Li Wenjie, Yash Jangir, Ignacy Stepka, Yash Agarwal, Marion Kipsang, Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.RO, cs.CL

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (62.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 SALT（Semantically ALigned action Tokenizer），在 VQ-VAE 风格的动作 tokenizer 上增加辅助目标，要求冻结的视觉语言模型从量化动作潜变量中恢复指令，从而保留动作轨迹中的语言接地信息，显著提升语言条件控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作动词不仅描述动作的物理结果，还描述动作的执行方式。然而 VLA 模型中的动作表征通常仅在原始动作空间用 L1/L2 损失优化重建，数值接近并不代表语言上有意义的区分。重建-only 的离散 tokenization 会系统性侵蚀动作轨迹中的动词接地信息。

Method: SALT 在 VQ-VAE 风格 tokenizer 基础上增加辅助目标：要求冻结的视觉语言模型从量化后的动作潜变量中恢复 episode 指令，从而强制动作表征保留语言语义信息。训练后的策略使用 SALT 的语义对齐动作 token。

Result: 在 SimplerEnv 中，SALT 训练的策略平均成功率 71.9%，远高于重建-only VQ-VAE tokenizer 的 42.7% 和 FAST 的 31.2%。SALT 还发展出动词特化的 code，同时保持重建保真度。

Conclusion: 机器人动作轨迹提供了语言接地的来源，在动作表征中保留这种结构可以显著改善语言条件控制。SALT 证明了语义对齐的动作 tokenizer 的价值。

Classification reason: 论文研究视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）中的动作表征与语言对齐，属于机器人具身智能与语言条件控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Strong robotics/world-model contribution with language-aligned action tokenization, but only partially overlaps the user's continuous-multimodal and video-generation focus. Signals: world model, vision-language-action, action tokenizer, language grounding, VQ-VAE

Abstract: Action verbs describe not only the physical outcomes of actions, but also how those actions are performed. Yet action representations in vision-language-action models (VLAs) are typically optimized for reconstruction under L1/L2 losses in raw action space, where numerical proximity need not reflect linguistically meaningful distinctions. On BridgeV2, we show that action trajectories contain verb-grounding information beyond visual state changes, and that reconstruction-only discrete tokenization systematically erodes this information. To address this problem, we introduce SALT, a Semantically ALigned action Tokenizer that augments a VQ-VAE-style tokenizer with an auxiliary objective requiring a frozen vision-language model to recover the episode instruction from quantized action latents. Policies trained with SALT achieve 71.9% average success in SimplerEnv, compared with 42.7% for a reconstruction-only VQ-VAE tokenizer and 31.2% for FAST. SALT also develops verb-specialized codes while maintaining reconstruction fidelity. These results show that robot action trajectories provide a source of language grounding and that preserving this structure in action representations can substantially improve language-conditioned control.

</details>

Abstract: Embodied mobile manipulation requires language, visual observations, three-dimensional scene structure, and action feasibility to be aligned before execution. We study open-vocabulary target grounding with few-shot manipulation in local household workspaces and present an embodied multimodal grounding framework that integrates active multi-view Semantic 3D Gaussian Splatting (Semantic-3DGS), reachability-aware base positioning, and a diffusion-based vision-language-action policy. A task-driven local Semantic-3DGS serves as a shared interface across active sensing, language-conditioned 3D localization, obstacle-aware scene reasoning, base preparation, and semantic conditioning of the action model. To preserve pretrained action priors, the 3D semantic cues are injected only into the late action-expert blocks. In expanded 50-trial real-robot evaluations against representative vision-language-action (VLA) approaches, the full system achieves 60% long-horizon success compared with 40% for PointVLA and 28% for DexVLA, and reaches 74% success in heavily cluttered manipulation compared with 52% for the single-view variant and 46% for PointVLA. It also maintains 75% success under a 75 cm height shift and eliminates photo-induced false grasps. These results indicate that explicit, refreshable 3D semantic grounding can improve robustness under clutter, occlusion, viewpoint variation, and embodiment constraints.

*Zhijie Wu, Kento Kawaharazuka, Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Gated VLA-Cache，一种轻量级、免训练的 KV-Cache 复用扩展方法，通过监测解码时 top-2 动作 token 的 logit 边际作为神经内省置信信号，在置信度下降时触发缓存失效与完整重算，在 LIBERO 基准上恢复盲缓存损失的精度同时保留大部分计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在实时控制中会为相邻帧中几乎不变的视觉 token 重复计算 KV 表示，造成大量计算浪费。现有 VLA-Cache 通过视觉相似性启发式复用 KV 状态，但其策略仅依赖观测空间启发式，未考虑模型自身的不确定性，在缓存有害时缺乏可靠性保障。

Method: 提出 Gated VLA-Cache，在视觉相似性缓存基础上增加神经内省门控：监控解码过程中 top-2 预测动作 token 之间的 logit 边际作为零成本的置信信号，当边际低于阈值时使缓存失效并触发完整重算。该方法为训练无关的轻量扩展，可应用于 OpenVLA 与 OpenVLA-OFT。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 基准套件上以 OpenVLA 和 OpenVLA-OFT 评估，Gated VLA-Cache 在盲缓存有害时提升可靠性。在 LIBERO-Goal 和 LIBERO-Long 上恢复超过 100% 的丢失精度，同时保留 80% 的计算节省。

Conclusion: 通过引入模型自身的解码置信度作为缓存失效信号，Gated VLA-Cache 在保持大部分计算效率的同时显著提升了 VLA 模型 KV-Cache 复用的可靠性，为实时具身控制中的高效推理提供了更稳健的方案。

Classification reason: 论文研究 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型在机器人实时控制中的 KV-Cache 复用优化，属于具身智能与机器人控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 (embodied_world_model)。同时 VLA 模型本身属于多模态理解与生成方向，但主要贡献聚焦于具身控制效率，故以 world_model 为主方向。

Importance reason: Efficient KV-cache reuse for VLA models is relevant to model efficiency, but the robotics/action focus and lack of continuous multimodal or video generation alignment reduce priority. Signals: KV-cache reuse, efficient inference, vision-language-action, training-free, logit margin confidence

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action(VLA) models map camera images and language instructions directly to motor commands through a single autoregressive transformer. In real-time control, they still spend substantial compute recomputing key-value(KV) representations for visual tokens that barely change across neighboring frames. Recent work such as VLA-Cache reduces that cost by reusing KV states for visually static patches, but its policy relies only on observation-space heuristics and does not account for the model's own uncertainty. We propose Gated VLA-Cache, a lightweight, training-free extension that augments visual-similarity caching with neural introspection. The method monitors the logit margin between the top two predicted action tokens, a zero-cost confidence signal available during decoding. When the margin drops below a threshold, the cache is invalidated and a full recompute is triggered. Evaluated on four LIBERO benchmark suites with both OpenVLA and OpenVLA-OFT, Gated VLA-Cache improves reliability when blind caching hurts. On LIBERO-Goal and LIBERO-Long, it recovers over 100% of the lost accuracy while retaining 80% of the compute savings.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型（WAM）几乎只预测 RGB 潜变量，仅针对像素重建训练，缺乏操作任务所需的 3D 几何与物体语义的显式信号。作者希望在不增加新传感器、新预训练或推理延迟的前提下，为 WAM 引入 3D 几何与物体中心语义监督。

Method: 作者发现冻结的视频生成 VAE 在编码 RGB 的同时也能近乎无损地编码 3D pointmap（无需 pointmap 专门训练）。基于此，Flex-π 将所有视觉信号（RGB、3D pointmap、DINO 语义）投影到共享潜空间，并在 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干中与动作联合去噪。通过逐流 dropout 与跨模态强制，单一训练好的 checkpoint 可在任意流子集上运行，从快速的动作-only 模式到完整联合生成模式。

Result: Flex-π 在真实世界双臂灵巧操作任务上（分布内与分布外）比最强基线快 2-7 倍，同时推理速度超过 π0.5，展现出极高的示范效率和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明冻结的视频生成 VAE 潜空间可同时承载 3D 几何与语义信息，为世界-动作模型提供"免费午餐"式的多模态监督；多流联合去噪与计算灵活性设计使单一模型能按需切换不同计算模式，兼顾性能与效率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Flex-π，一个面向真实世界双臂灵巧操作任务的 6B 世界-动作模型（WAM），在共享潜空间中联合去噪 RGB、3D 几何与 DINO 语义及动作，属于具身智能世界模型与规划控制方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model。同时其利用冻结视频生成 VAE 编码 3D pointmap 与连续潜空间联合去噪，与连续多模态方向相关。

Importance reason: 该工作将冻结视频生成VAE用于3D pointmap编码，实现多流世界-动作模型的高效联合生成，与用户世界模型和连续多模态方向高度契合。 Signals: world model, neural simulator, continuous multimodal representation, flow matching, efficient inference

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) predict the future to act better, but nearly all of them predict only RGB latents, trained purely for pixel reconstruction, with no explicit signal for the 3D geometry or object semantics manipulation needs. We find a surprising free lunch: the same frozen video-generation VAE that encodes RGB also encodes 3D pointmaps almost losslessly, with no pointmap-specific training at all. This lets us supervise Flex-$\pi$, a 6B-parameter WAM, on 3D geometry and object-centric DINO semantics alongside RGB, at no cost in new sensors, new pre-training, or inference latency. Every visual signal is projected into this shared latent space and denoised jointly with actions inside a Mixture-of-Transformers backbone; per-stream dropout with cross-modality forcing then lets a single trained checkpoint run on any subset of these streams, from a fast action-only mode to full joint generation. The result is a policy that is exceptionally demonstration-efficient and generalizes well, beating the strongest baselines by up to 2-7$\times$ on dexterous, precise, real-world bimanual manipulation tasks both in and out of distribution, all while running faster than $\pi_{0.5}$. Our project website:

*Haiyu Wu, Randall Balestriero, Morgan Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: 本文提出VIScore指标，用于诊断潜空间世界模型的规划相关质量。通过对比SIGReg和VISReg两种正则化方法，发现VISReg能提升OOD规划成功率，并据此提出覆盖编码器、预测器和规划器三个层面的Veracity-Influence-Sobriety评分，其Spearman相关超过0.75，优于straightness、physical-state probing和empowerment等现有指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜空间世界模型的规划能力与潜空间性质之间缺乏明确联系。现有评测指标仅关注编码后的潜空间，无法解释规划成功率的变化，需要一种能覆盖编码器、预测器和规划器全链路的诊断指标。

Method: 首先对比SIGReg与VISReg两种正则化损失，发现VISReg通过更灵活的中心/尺度/形状正则权重控制和大batch size提升OOD规划成功率。基于此提出VIScore指标，从三个维度量化规划质量：Veracity（预测器在给定编码特征下的可达性）、Influence（预测器的容量）、Sobriety（基于搜索的规划器的幻觉程度），综合覆盖编码器、预测器和规划器。

Result: VIScore在seen和unseen模型及数据集上均取得超过0.75的Spearman相关系数，优于straightness、physical-state probing和empowerment等对比指标。在所有测试场景中，VIScore是唯一校准误差低于常数拟合的指标。

Conclusion: VIScore能够更好地解释潜空间世界模型的规划成功率，强调了编码器、预测器和规划器三个层面在规划成功中的重要性，可为未来世界模型的设计与诊断提供参考。

Classification reason: 论文研究潜空间世界模型中的规划质量诊断与评测指标（VIScore），属于世界模型方向。虽然更偏向评测，但核心对象是潜空间世界模型的规划能力，与"具身世界模型与规划控制"这一CANONICAL子主题最相关，因为其关注世界模型中的规划与预测器能力。

Importance reason: Focuses on latent world model planning diagnostics, relevant to world models but not directly on continuous/multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image priorities. Signals: world model, latent space regularization, planning success, OOD generalization, VIScore metric

Abstract: Regulating the latent space to an isotropic Gaussian distribution provides a stable and information-maximized landscape for world model planning. However, the latent space property and successful planning remain disconnected. We first study this by comparing SIGReg and VISReg, two regularization loss functions with the same distribution target but different properties. Compared with SIGReg, VISReg has more flexibility in controlling the weights of center, scale, and shape regularization, and a larger batch size brings a finer distribution approximation. We find that the former, despite being beneficial in self-supervised learning (SSL), does not help the planning, whereas the latter improves the planning success on out-of-domain (OOD) datasets. This motivates a deep understanding of the factors that correlate with the success rate. Unlike the previous metrics focusing on the encoded latent only, we propose the Veracity-Influence-Sobriety score (VIScore), a metric that quantifies the reachability and capacity of a predictor given the encoded feature, and the hallucination of the searching-based planner. Compared with straightness, physical-state probing, and empowerment, we show that, with the measurement covering encoder, predictor, and planner, VIScore explains the success rate better than the others, as reflected by a strong Spearman correlation. Specifically, VIScore consistently achieves a Spearman correlation over 0.75 on both seen and unseen models and datasets on the cross-task success rate pool. Moreover, VIScore is the only metric that has a calibration error below the constant fit across all testing scenarios, showcasing the importance of these three aspects in planning success. We hope this metric can help future studies on world model design and diagnosis.

</details>

*Sachin Sunil Kelkar, Tanmay Dokania, Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

TL;DR: 本文提出一种风险感知的 kinodynamic 运动规划方法，用于行星环境下的安全导航。方法分两步：先用采样规划器 AO-RRT 生成动态可行、风险感知且渐近成本最优的轨迹，再用序列凸规划（SCP）将运动规划转化为非线性优化问题求解。通过 CVaR 量化风险，在仿真与硬件实验中风险降低超过 97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间探索中，机器人运动规划面临环境交互未知的问题，如轮式机器人的地形力学学习会引入不确定性，导致高风险运动计划甚至任务失败；感知系统带来的不确定性进一步加剧安全运动规划的难度。

Method: 两步方法：第一步使用采样规划器 AO-RRT 生成动态可行、风险感知且渐近成本最优的轨迹；第二步将运动规划形式化为非线性优化问题，用序列凸规划（SCP）求解，以 AO-RRT 轨迹作为初始解。风险通过条件风险价值（CVaR）量化。

Result: 在仿真与硬件实验中，该方法在轨迹上实现了超过约 97% 的风险降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的风险感知 kinodynamic 运动规划方法能有效降低不确定性下的运动风险，为行星环境中的安全自主导航提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 论文面向行星环境下的机器人运动规划，在不确定性下进行风险感知的 kinodynamic 运动规划，属于具身智能/机器人规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on planetary rover motion planning, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and generative video/world models. Signals: risk-aware planning, kinodynamic motion planning, CVaR, planetary navigation, AO-RRT

Abstract: For autonomous space exploration, robotic agents need to perform motion planning in which environmental interactions may be unknown. Learning these interactions, such as terrain mechanics for wheeled robots, can introduce uncertainties that lead to risky motion plans and potentially hazardous operations or mission failures. Moreover, uncertainties induced by perception-based systems can exacerbate the problem of safe motion planning. In this letter, we address the problem of performing cost-optimal kinodynamic motion planning with risk awareness. We approach this in two steps. First, a sampling-based planner (AO-RRT) generates a dynamically feasible, risk-aware, and asymptotically cost-optimal trajectory. Second, we formulate motion planning as a nonlinear optimization problem and solve it using sequential convex programming (SCP), using the AO-RRT trajectory as an initial solution. By quantifying risk using conditional value-at-risk (CVaR), we demonstrate a reduction in risk by over $\sim$97\% across trajectories in simulation and hardware experiments.

</details>


### [11] [Surgical WAM: A World-Action Model for Data-Efficient Surgical Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11204)
*Wenrui Bao, Tianyun Jiang, Zhiben Chen, Ser-Nam Lim, Peter D. Peng, Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (78.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Surgical WAM 是一个基于 Cosmos Policy 构建的手术世界-动作模型，先通过无动作视频预训练学习手术视觉动力学，再在固定数量的动作标注演示上微调，部署时作为闭环滚动时域控制器执行动作块并重规划。在四个模拟手术操作任务上，视频预训练将平均成功率从 63.5% 提升到 77.8%，在接触密集和双臂协调任务上提升最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人操作策略学习受限于动作标注演示的稀缺性：遥操作手术机器人（如 dVRK）的同步运动学轨迹采集成本高昂，而内镜视频相对廉价且丰富。现有手术世界模型主要将视频用于仿真或策略评估，很少将学到的动力学转化为闭环控制。作者希望回答：在固定动作标注预算下，无动作视频预训练能否提升闭环手术操作性能。

Method: Surgical WAM 是一个统一生成式模型，基于 Cosmos Policy 构建，联合预测未来的内镜观测和可执行的手术机器人动作块。训练分两阶段：先从无动作视频学习手术视觉动力学，再在固定动作标注预算上微调。部署时作为闭环滚动时域控制器，执行每个预测动作块的前缀并基于新观测重新规划。

Result: 在四个模拟手术操作任务上，视频预训练将平均成功率从 63.5% 提升到 77.8%，其中 PegTransfer 任务绝对提升 20 个百分点，接触密集和双臂协调任务提升最大。

Conclusion: 无动作视频提供了可迁移的视觉动力学先验，有助于在有限动作监督下学习手术机器人控制，将数据高效视频预训练作为扩展手术机器人学习的实用路径。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Surgical WAM，一个面向手术机器人操作的世界-动作模型，联合预测内镜观测与机器人动作块，用于闭环控制。这属于具身智能/机器人世界模型与规划控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] embodied_world_model（具身世界模型与规划控制）。同时其基于 Cosmos Policy 的生成式建模与动作条件视频预测也涉及多模态理解与生成方向。

Importance reason: World-model pretraining for data-efficient robot control is solid but outside the user's core multimodal generation focus. Signals: world model, video pretraining, action chunks, surgical robotics, data-efficient learning

Abstract: Learning reliable surgical manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of action-labeled demonstrations: teleoperated surgical robot (e.g., dVRK) trajectories with synchronized kinematics are costly to collect, while surgical tasks demand precise contact handling, long-horizon reasoning, and bimanual coordination. Endoscopic video is comparatively inexpensive and abundant relative to synchronized video--kinematics trajectories, and a natural way to exploit it is to learn world models of surgical scenes. However, existing surgical world models use video primarily for simulation or policy evaluation, and rarely translate the learned dynamics into closed-loop control. This gap raises our central question: under a fixed budget of action-labeled demonstrations, does action-free video pretraining improve closed-loop surgical manipulation? To answer it, we introduce the Surgical World-Action Model (Surgical WAM), a unified generative model built on Cosmos Policy that jointly predicts future endoscopic observations and executable surgical robot action chunks. Surgical WAM first learns surgical visual dynamics from action-free video and is then fine-tuned on the fixed action-labeled budget; at deployment, it acts as a closed-loop, receding-horizon controller that executes a short prefix of each predicted action chunk and replans from the resulting observation. On a suite of four simulated surgical manipulation tasks, video pretraining improves the average success rate from 63.5% to 77.8%, including an absolute gain of 20 percentage points on PegTransfer, with the largest improvements on contact-rich and bimanual tasks. These results demonstrate that action-free video provides transferable visual dynamics priors for learning surgical robot control with limited action supervision, positioning data-efficient video pretraining as a practical path toward scaling up surgical robot learning.

</details>

Abstract: Advanced air mobility operations hold the potential to enhance and expand regional transportation of both people and goods in populated areas. However, hazardous flight conditions arising from interactions between wind and the built environment remain a significant challenge for aerial vehicles in urban settings. This work proposes a novel framework towards safe flight planning of aerial vehicles in windy urban environments. A learning-based surrogate model is trained to rapidly predict flow fields from readily available information such as building geometry and incident wind. This surrogate prediction is used to calculate a volumetric flight challenge scalar field based on critical flow parameters and proximity to structures. A safe, flow-informed flight trajectory is then identified through a cost-minimizing pathfinder. The complete system is demonstrated experimentally through flight tests of a micro aerial vehicle through a model urban geometry placed in a large fan-array wind tunnel. Comparing this approach to trajectories generated without knowledge of the wind field, we find the flow-informed approach reduces undesired vehicle displacement and improves flight stability. This work is among the first practical demonstrations of safe, wind-aware methodologies for advanced air mobility in urban environments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶强化学习常受数据效率与模型偏差之间的权衡限制。世界模型虽能减少对昂贵环境交互的依赖，但基于学习动力学的策略优化对预测误差敏感，需要更鲁棒且样本高效的训练框架。

Method: 提出 Dreamer-SAC 框架，将循环状态空间世界模型与 off-policy 软演员-评论家（SAC）算法集成，直接在潜空间中训练策略。结合真实交互与短视界生成轨迹，采用 n-step 目标估计与多目标监督（涵盖驾驶效率与安全性）。

Result: 在自动驾驶场景中，Dreamer-SAC 一致优于 DreamerV3、SAC、PPO 等代表性强化学习基线，并以显著更少的真实环境交互获得更优性能。实验发现 rollout 视界与策略性能呈倒 U 型关系，短视界潜空间 rollout 在额外训练信号与累积模型偏差间取得最佳平衡；n-step 目标估计比一步 TD 目标在利用预测经验进行价值学习上更有效。

Conclusion: Dreamer-SAC 通过 off-policy 潜空间学习与短视界 rollout 结合，有效缓解了世界模型偏差问题，实现了自动驾驶场景下的样本高效强化学习，并揭示了 rollout 视界与 n-step 目标估计对性能的关键影响。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Dreamer-SAC 框架，将循环状态空间世界模型与 off-policy 软演员-评论家算法结合，用于自动驾驶场景的样本高效强化学习。这属于面向自动驾驶/具身智能的规划控制世界模型，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Applies world-model RL to autonomous driving, but lacks direct novelty in continuous latent modeling or generation, and the driving domain is outside the user's core focus. Signals: world model, off-policy RL, latent space policy, sample-efficient, autonomous driving

Abstract: Sample-efficient reinforcement learning for autonomous driving is often limited by the trade-off between data efficiency and model bias. While world models reduce the reliance on costly environment interactions, policy optimization over learned dynamics remains sensitive to prediction errors. This paper proposes the Dreamer-SAC framework, which integrates a recurrent state-space world model with an off-policy soft actor-critic algorithm trained directly in latent space. The framework uses a combination of real interactions and short-horizon generated trajectories with n-step target estimation and multi-objective supervision. Evaluated in autonomous driving scenarios with objectives encompassing driving efficiency and safety, the proposed framework consistently outperforms representative reinforcement learning baselines, including DreamerV3, SAC, and PPO, while achieving improved performance with substantially fewer real environment interactions. Experiments reveal an inverted-U relationship between rollout horizon and policy performance, where short-horizon latent rollouts achieve the best trade-off between additional training signals and accumulated model bias. Furthermore, n-step target estimation demonstrates more effectiveness over one-step temporal-difference targets in exploiting predicted experience for value learning.

</details>


### [14] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team, XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

TL;DR: XCoT-VLA 提出用紧凑的可执行思维链（XCoT）token 替代冗长的自然语言 CoT，用于自动驾驶的 Vision-Language-Action 建模。通过 Reason-Action 监督自动构建 XCoT 序列，并采用确定性 token 函数路由（Reason FFN 处理 XCoT、Control FFN 处理轨迹查询）进行 flow-matching 轨迹生成，同时引入 XCPO 策略优化扩展，显著降低推理开销并提升轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型能连接场景理解、语义推理与轨迹生成，但冗长的自然语言思维链（CoT）不适合实时控制：它是开放式的、解码成本高、且难以作为面向动作的表征进行优化。

Method: 提出 XCoT-VLA：用从自动构建的 Reason-Action 监督中学到的紧凑可执行 XCoT token 替代描述性推理。记录轨迹提供动作证据，场景上下文提供因果语义。预测的 XCoT 序列保持在上下文中，通过共享多模态自注意力条件化固定轨迹查询。采用确定性 token 函数路由：Reason FFN 处理 XCoT token，Control FFN 处理轨迹查询，用于 flow-matching 轨迹生成。另提出 XCPO（XCoT 策略优化）作为同一可执行 token 空间中的可选精化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布集上纵向 ADE 从 1.645 降至 1.323，变道场景中横向 FDE 从 1.616 降至 0.648。仅用 2-6 个可执行 XCoT token 表示驾驶导向推理，大幅降低自回归推理开销，保持在实时规划预算内。

Conclusion: 结果表明驾驶导向推理可以做到紧凑、可执行，并直接与轨迹生成相连，为自动驾驶 VLA 模型提供了一种高效且可优化的推理表征方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 XCoT-VLA，面向自动驾驶的 Vision-Language-Action 模型，将可执行思维链 token 与轨迹生成（flow matching）结合，属于具身智能/自动驾驶的动作规划与控制范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: 将连续思维链与flow-matching轨迹生成结合，直接契合用户对连续多模态模型和世界模型的高优先级关注。 Signals: executable chain-of-thought, flow-matching trajectory generation, vision-language-action driving, world model, efficient inference

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>

<details>

Result: 发布 HUI360 开放集 1M 预处理标注及原始全景 360° 图像（按需提供，符合 GDPR），并建立首个跨数据集交互意图预测评测基线。

Conclusion: HUI360 提供了迄今最大规模、最多样化的野外人机交互意图预测数据集与标注流水线，为评估和提升交互预测模型的泛化能力奠定基础，并推动具身智能体的主动社会行为研究。

Classification reason: 论文关注移动机器人在真实环境中的人机交互意图预测（human-robot interaction anticipation），属于具身智能感知与交互预测范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Signals: egocentric dataset, human-robot interaction anticipation, 360-degree video, world model (weak), no autoregressive/diffusion generation

Abstract: As robots increasingly operate in human-populated environments, anticipating human intentions is essential for enabling proactive and socially aware behavior. Automatic anticipation of human-robot interactions is thus emerging as a crucial perception challenge for embodied agents. To this end, we introduce HUI360, the largest dataset for human-robot interaction anticipation in the wild and its set of baselines. The dataset was collected from a mobile robot, in the wild, over multiple days within a 3-month period, and in several environments, capturing natural, spontaneous behaviors from both passersby and users, and encompassing a diverse range of individuals. This variety enables evaluating and improving the generalization capabilities of interaction anticipation models. We designed a pipeline and share code for automatic interaction annotation in arbitrary 360-degree equirectangular videos, along with interfaces for manual refinement. Using this pipeline, we release the HUI360 open set of 1M pre-processed annotations, including detailed 2D poses, facial keypoints, and segmentation masks, obtained using state-of-the-art computer vision methods and manually curated to ensure high-quality tracking and interaction annotation. Additionally, we release the raw panoptic 360-degree images captured from the robot's egocentric viewpoint (on demand, for research purpose only in compliance with GDPR). Finally, we establish benchmark baselines for interaction anticipation, including the first cross-dataset evaluations for this task: to this end, we also release 6M annotations for another existing in-the-wild outdoor dataset collected from a mobile robot (SSUP-HRI). Dataset and code can be found at .

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents a self-supervised representation learning framework for understanding 3D skeleton-based human motion in soccer, using future motion prediction as the learning objective. Since human motion is inherently uncertain, accounting for multiple plausible futures is essential for capturing the underlying motion dynamics and learning effective representations. To this end, we introduce a conditioning module for motion prediction that models a probabilistic distribution over discretized future motions in 3D Euclidean space, learning multimodality with explicit supervision from future trajectories. Experiments on large-scale soccer player tracking data show that our approach substantially improves motion prediction accuracy. Moreover, the learned representations effectively transfer to multiple soccer downstream applications, demonstrating strong cross-task generalization.

</details>

### [17] [GESTO: Human-Centric Spatio-Temporal Memory for Reasoning in Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10886)
*Ermanno Bartoli, Buwei He, Dennis Rotondi, Sebastian Koch, Federico Tombari, Kai O. Arras, Patric Jensfelt, Yixi Cai, Iolanda Leite*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 空间 3D/4D 世界模型

Importance: 普通相关 (64.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GESTO 是一种面向动态人类环境的时空记忆系统，将持久 4D 场景图与两层层次结构（原子人-物交互与目标驱动事件）耦合，从 RGB-D 流自动提取带时间戳的交互、将其锚定到持久场景实体并分组为事件，通过关系感知的工具调用智能体实现活动中心的时空推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类环境中需要记忆不仅记录物体存在与位置，还要记录人们如何随时间使用物体以及个体交互如何组合成目标导向的活动。现有 4D 场景图保留了物体和地点历史但缺少活动结构，而活动表示要么未锚定在持久 3D 场景中，要么依赖外部提供的事件边界和物体关联。

Method: GESTO 将持久 4D 场景图与两层层次结构（原子人-物交互和目标驱动事件）耦合。从 RGB-D 观测流中自动提取带时间戳的交互，将其锚定到持久场景实体，分组为事件，并利用事件上下文细化不确定的物体关联。使用关系感知的工具调用智能体查询记忆，进行活动中心的时空推理。

Result: 在标准类别上取得 0.71、0.75 和 0.70 的分数，接近使用真实事件和物体锚定的方法，同时大幅优于移除这些输入后的同一推理框架。在 Space2Event 和 Event2Space 查询上分别达到 0.73 和 0.75。消融实验表明层次事件结构和上下文感知的锚定细化提供了互补收益。

Conclusion: GESTO 验证了活动锚定的层次记忆对动态人类环境中回顾性推理的有效性，层次事件结构与上下文感知的锚定细化互补，支持活动中心的时空记忆范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 GESTO，一种将持久 4D 场景图与原子人-物交互及目标驱动事件耦合的时空记忆系统，用于动态人类环境中的活动中心时空推理。这属于世界模型方向下的空间 3D/4D 世界模型（spatial_3d_4d_world_model）范畴，因为其核心是 4D 场景图与动态场景中的空间记忆建模。

Importance reason: Solid robotics memory/reasoning work, but only partially overlaps your continuous-world-model focus and lacks generative or continuous-representation novelty. Signals: 4D scene graph, human-object interaction, event hierarchy, spatio-temporal reasoning, RGB-D grounding

Abstract: Robots operating in human environments need memories that capture not only what objects exist and where, but also how people use them over time and how individual interactions compose into goal-directed activities. Existing 4D scene graphs preserve object and place histories but omit activity structure, whereas activity representations are either not grounded in persistent 3D scenes or rely on externally provided event boundaries and object associations. We present GESTO (Grounded Event and Spatio-Temporal memOry), a spatio-temporal memory that couples a persistent 4D scene graph with a two-level hierarchy of atomic human--object interactions and goal-driven events. From an RGB-D observation stream, GESTO automatically extracts timestamped interactions, grounds them to persistent scene entities, groups them into events, and uses event context to refine uncertain object associations. A relation-aware tool-calling agent queries the resulting memory for activity-centric spatio-temporal reasoning. We evaluate GESTO on the reproducible text, binary, and time categories of an existing benchmark, together with 40 new Space2Event and Event2Space queries. GESTO achieves scores of 0.71, 0.75, and 0.70 on the standard categories, approaching a method supplied with ground-truth event and object grounding, while substantially outperforming the same reasoning framework when these inputs are removed. It further achieves 0.73 and 0.75 on Space2Event and Event2Space queries. Ablations show that hierarchical event structure and context-aware grounding refinement provide complementary benefits, supporting activity-grounded hierarchical memory for retrospective reasoning in dynamic human environments.

</details>


### [18] [GS-CPE: Unified 6-Degree-of-Freedom Camera Pose Estimation via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10938)
*Huaiyuan Weng, Chul Min Yeum, Su-Min Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 空间 3D/4D 世界模型

Importance: 普通相关 (60.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GS-CPE 提出一个基于 3D Gaussian Splatting 的粗到细 6-DoF 相机位姿估计框架，先通过检索引导的几何位姿估计获得粗位姿，再通过多尺度可见性感知的 RGB 重投影优化进行位姿精化，在室内外基准上取得 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉定位在场景坐标回归和直接相机位姿回归方面虽有进展，但同时在鲁棒泛化与高精度之间取得平衡仍具挑战。现有方法难以兼顾几何精度与对新场景的泛化能力。

Method: GS-CPE 采用粗到细两阶段框架：首先在 3DGS 场景表示上通过检索引导的几何位姿估计获得粗位姿；随后通过最小化可见性感知的掩码 RGB 重投影目标，在多尺度优化框架中进行位姿精化，并采用自适应重渲染策略。

Result: 在 7Scenes、Cambridge Landmarks、FAST-LIVO2 数据集及自定义数据集上的大量实验表明，GS-CPE 在精度和泛化能力上均一致优于现有方法，达到 SOTA 性能。

Conclusion: 基于 3DGS 场景表示的统一粗到细位姿估计框架能够同时实现高精度与良好泛化，验证了 3D 高斯泼溅作为场景世界模型在视觉定位任务中的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文使用 3D Gaussian Splatting 场景表示进行相机位姿估计，属于基于 3D 场景/神经场表示的空间世界建模，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 spatial_3d_4d_world_model。

Importance reason: Solid 3DGS-based pose estimation work, but it is a niche CV task with limited direct connection to the user's core continuous multimodal and video generation priorities. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, 6-DoF camera pose estimation, coarse-to-fine refinement, visual localization, world model (spatial 3D)

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in visual localization, from scene coordinate regression to direct camera pose regression, achieving both robust generalization and high accuracy remain challenging. This study introduces GS-CPE (Gaussian Splatting based Camera Pose Estimation), a coarse-to-fine framework for 6-DoF camera pose estimation that unifies geometry-based coarse pose estimation with robust 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) warping based pose refinement. GS-CPE first estimates a coarse pose via retrieval-guided geometric pose estimation on a 3DGS scene representation, then refines it by minimizing a visibility aware masked RGB warping objective in a multi-scale optimization framework, with adaptive re-rendering. Extensive experiments on indoor and outdoor benchmarks including 7Scenes, Cambridge Landmarks, FAST-LIVO2 datasets, and a custom dataset demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, consistently outperforming in both accuracy and generalization.

</details>

### [19] [4D-WAM: 4D Consistent World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10107)
*Jiacheng Fu, Yibo Yuan, Meng Tian, Yue Li, Jiangtong Zhu, Jianhua Han, Yueyi Zhang, Jianwu Fang, Jianru Xue, Hang Xu, Zhiwei Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (91.36) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 4D-WAM 提出利用几何基础模型提供训练时监督，为自动驾驶世界-动作模型（WAM）引入4D一致性损失，并设计面向决策的时序采样策略，使模型在无额外推理开销下生成物理一致的4D场景演化并提升轨迹规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界-动作模型（WAM）仅用2D视频数据训练，无法理解底层4D驾驶场景结构，导致生成的未来预测视觉上合理但4D不一致，从而误导下游规划。

Method: 将WAM预测的未来帧输入几何基础模型，利用其4D感知响应定义4D一致性损失，在训练时监督模型理解、表示和预测物理一致的4D场景。同时识别WAM中的"早期决策"现象，提出面向决策的时序采样策略，强调在高噪声早期阶段（驾驶决策主要形成阶段）的监督。

Result: 在NAVSIM-v1和NAVSIM-v2基准上达到最先进性能，有效建模4D一致的场景演化并提升轨迹规划。

Conclusion: 通过训练时几何监督与决策导向采样，4D-WAM在不增加推理成本的前提下实现了4D一致的场景建模，验证了将几何先验注入世界模型训练的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文面向自动驾驶场景的未来场景演化与轨迹规划联合建模，属于世界模型方向。其核心是视频世界模型（World-Action Model）在4D一致性上的改进，与[CANONICAL]视频世界模型子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances video world models for autonomous driving with 4D consistency, aligning with the user's world model priority. Signals: world model, video world model, autonomous driving, 4D consistency, trajectory planning

Abstract: Emerging World-Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated promising performance in autonomous driving by jointly modeling future driving scene evolution and trajectory planning. However, existing WAMs are typically trained with video data, which is only 2D projections of the underlying 4D driving scene. Consequently, WAMs fail to understand and capture the structure of 4D scenes and thus generate visually plausible yet 4D inconsistent future predictions that mislead downstream planning. To alleviate this issue, we present 4D-WAM, a model that leverages geometric foundation models for training-time supervision to enable 4D consistent world modeling. Specifically, we feed WAM-predicted future frames into a geometric foundation model, and use 4D-aware responses to define a 4D consistency loss. This loss encourages the model to understand, represent, and predict physically consistent 4D scenes during training, without additional inference cost. Moreover, we identify an early-decision phenomenon in WAMs and propose a decision-oriented timestep sampling strategy that emphasizes supervision at early, high-noise stages, where driving decisions are primarily formed. By propagating 4D supervision to this critical decision-formation phase, the proposed strategy further improves trajectory planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that 4D-WAM effectively models 4D consistent scene evolution and achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging NAVSIM-v1 and NAVSIM-v2 benchmarks.

</details>


### [20] [Stream Forcing: Constructing Unified Training Trajectory for Robust Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10439)
*Yueting Zhu, Yuehao Song, Kaicheng Zhang, Bao Tang, Shaoyu Chen, Qian Zhang, Wenyu Liu, Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (94.22) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: 提出 Stream Forcing，一种统一的流式视频生成训练框架，通过将视频扩散采样重构为帧索引的随机过程，构建从独立采样到推理一致采样的连续训练轨迹，并引入联合校准与时间相关采样算法，解决训练-推理不一致问题，显著提升生成质量与长时序外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频扩散模型存在根本的训练-推理不匹配：推理遵循专门的去噪顺序，而先进训练策略通常需要多样化的噪声水平配置。这种训练充分性与推理一致性之间的权衡限制了流式视频生成的质量。

Method: 将视频扩散采样重构为帧索引的噪声水平随机过程，在该随机过程空间中构建连续训练轨迹，使采样调度从独立采样渐进演化为推理一致采样；引入联合校准算法保证轨迹平滑性，以及时间相关采样算法增强跨帧相关性。

Result: 在 UCF-101 基准上 FVD 提升 36.6%；零样本外推到长时序视频生成时 FVD 提升 27.9%。

Conclusion: Stream Forcing 有效平衡了训练充分性与推理效率，显著提升流式视频生成质量，并具备稳健的长时序视频生成外推能力，为流式视频世界建模提供了统一训练框架。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦流式视频生成（streaming video generation），强调其作为世界建模的潜力，涉及未来帧在线顺序推断与长时序视频生成，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型（video_world_model）高度契合。

Importance reason: Directly advances streaming video world models with a novel unified training trajectory, strongly matching the user's top-priority video world model and continuous/diffusion video generation interests. Signals: video world model, streaming video generation, diffusion video generation, train-inference mismatch, long-horizon extrapolation

Abstract: Streaming video generation holds strong potential for world modeling, where future frames must be inferred online sequentially to form a continuous video stream. However, streaming video diffusion models introduce a fundamental train-inference mismatch: inference follows a specialized denoising order, whereas advanced training strategies typically require diverse noise-level configurations. To address this trade-off between train-inference consistency and training coverage, we reformulate the video diffusion sampling as a frame-indexed stochastic process over noise levels. Within this stochastic process space, we construct a continuous training trajectory along which the sampling schedule progressively evolves from independent sampling to inference-consistent sampling. We further introduce a joint calibration algorithm and a temporal correlative sampling algorithm to ensure trajectory smoothness and cross-frame correlation. Building on these designs, we propose Stream Forcing, a unified training framework for streaming video generation that balances training sufficiency and inference efficiency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Stream Forcing significantly improves generation quality with a 36.6% FVD improvement on the UCF-101 benchmark. Furthermore, our method facilitates robust zero-shot extrapolation to long-horizon video generation with a 27.9% FVD improvement on the UCF-101 benchmark.

</details>


### [21] [The Evaluation Protocol Determines the Result: An Independent Reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10145)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (82.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了 LeWorldModel 在 TwoRoom 环境上的结果，发现评估协议（目标偏移、步数预算、目标构造方式）对结果影响巨大，作者声称的 87% 目标达成率在不同协议下可从 84% 跌至 8%。同时发现一步预测精度无法预测长时程规划成功率，且 batch normalization 会掩盖训练损失的真实情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWorldModel 声称在 TwoRoom 诊断环境上达到约 87% 的目标达成率，但作者发布的配置文件中缺少决定结果的关键约定。作者希望验证该结果的可复现性，并揭示评估协议对世界模型评测结果的巨大影响。

Method: 作者用约 25 美元租用算力独立重实现 LeWorldModel，并在单台笔记本 CPU 上完成全部评测。通过对比作者发布 checkpoint 与自身复现模型，系统性地改变评估协议（目标偏移、步数预算、目标构造方式、帧跳过、动作编码器宽度、图像归一化、动作 z-score）来测量其对结果的影响。

Result: 复现模型在仓库评估目标偏移下达到 94.0%，作者 checkpoint 在同一协议下为 84.0%；位置探针 Pearson r=0.9988（报告值 0.996）。但仅按发布配置复现的模型预测器无法收敛。改变目标构造方式使 checkpoint 从 84% 跌至 8%；一步预测精度无法排序长时程规划成功率；batch normalization 使验证损失虚高最多 300 倍。

Conclusion: LeWorldModel 的核心结果高度依赖未公开的评估协议约定，其可复现性存疑。研究揭示世界模型评测中评估协议的重要性，以及一步预测精度与长时程规划能力之间的脱节，为世界模型评测方法提出警示。

Classification reason: 论文对 LeWorldModel 这一潜空间世界模型进行独立复现，关注其预测损失、反坍缩正则化与评估协议对结果的影响，属于世界模型方向。映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 video_world_model（视频世界模型），因为该子主题涵盖生成式世界模型、未来预测与动作条件建模，LeWorldModel 作为潜空间世界模型与其合理相关。

Importance reason: A rigorous reproducibility study exposing evaluation-protocol fragility in world models, but its narrow TwoRoom scope limits direct value for your continuous-multimodal and generation priorities. Signals: world model evaluation protocol, reproducibility study, goal offset sensitivity, batch normalization pitfall, prediction vs planning decoupling

Abstract: LeWorldModel trains a latent world model with a prediction loss and a single anti-collapse regulariser, and reports approximately 87% of goals reached on TwoRoom, its simplest diagnostic environment. We reproduce that result by independent reimplementation on roughly $25 of rented compute, with all evaluation on one laptop CPU. We reach 94.0% at the repository's evaluation goal offset, against 84.0% for the authors' own released checkpoint measured under our protocol on identical episodes, and we reproduce the reported representation result directly (position probe Pearson r = 0.9988 against a reported 0.996). Reaching that point required four conventions that determine the outcome and appear in no released configuration file: dense action gathering across a frameskip block, a programmatically-set action-encoder width, ImageNet pixel normalisation, and action z-scoring. A reproducer following the released configurations alone obtains a model whose predictor cannot converge. The evaluation protocol is itself contested by the released material. The paper's appendix and the repository's configuration specify different goal offsets and step budgets; on the authors' own weights these yield 14.0% and 84.0%, and only the configuration's values reproduce the reported figure. On fifty identical episodes, changing nothing but how the goal is constructed moves that checkpoint from 84.0% to 8.0%. Two findings generalise. One-step prediction accuracy does not predict long-horizon planning success: across three checkpoints spanning a sevenfold range in prediction error, including the authors' own, it orders short-horizon success monotonically and fails to order long-horizon success at all. And a batch normalisation layer inflated our reported validation loss by up to a factor of 300, concealing a training loss that was flat throughout.

</details>

### [22] [TRACE-GS: On-Policy Trajectory Distillation with Privileged Geometric Conditioning for Sparse-View 3DGS Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10286)
*Linlian Jiang, Yuchen Xi, Sadman Rakib Pinon, Ruigang Yang, Yang Wang, Xinxin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (53.55) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TRACE-GS 提出一种基于在线策略轨迹蒸馏的稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复框架，利用训练时的特权几何条件（LUPI 设置）将扩散先验适配到 3DGS 恢复任务，在部署时仅保留稀疏视角学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复方法存在根本性局限：在独立加噪状态上的监督无法覆盖推理时实际到达的状态。稀疏视角下欠约束的几何从一开始就使去噪产生偏差，且偏差沿 rollout 不断累积放大。

Method: TRACE-GS 采用在线策略轨迹蒸馏：教师模型利用额外训练视角提供的更丰富几何作为条件，沿稀疏视角学生模型自身的 rollout 提供目标，在每个访问状态对齐去噪方向与跨视角响应。这种仅训练时使用的几何信息使其处于 LUPI（利用特权信息学习）设定。部署时仅保留稀疏视角学生模型，其恢复的渲染结果作为伪观测用于 3DGS 细化。

Result: TRACE-GS 是首个从特权几何中导出在线策略监督用于稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复的方法，在多个数据集和稀疏视角设置下取得一致的性能提升和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过在线策略轨迹蒸馏与特权几何条件，TRACE-GS 有效解决了扩散先验在稀疏视角 3DGS 恢复中训练-推理状态分布不匹配的问题，验证了 LUPI 设定在该任务中的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦稀疏视角 3DGS 重建与恢复，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: Solid 3DGS restoration method but outside the user's core continuous/multimodal/video-generation focus. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, sparse-view restoration, on-policy trajectory distillation, privileged geometric conditioning, LUPI

Abstract: We present TRACE-GS, an on-policy trajectory distillation framework that leverages privileged geometric conditioning at training time, thereby adapting a diffusion prior to sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) restoration. Rather than pursuing increasingly sophisticated restoration architectures, we identify a more fundamental limitation shared by existing diffusion-based approaches: supervision at independently noised states does not cover those reached during inference. In sparse-view 3DGS, under-constrained geometry biases denoising from the outset, and the resulting deviations compound along the rollout. TRACE-GS instead performs on-policy trajectory distillation: a teacher conditioned on richer geometry from additional training views supplies targets along the sparse-view student's own rollout, aligning denoising directions and cross-view responses at each visited state. This training-only geometry places TRACE-GS in the learning using privileged information (LUPI) setting. At deployment, only the sparse-view student is retained, and its restored renderings serve as pseudo-observations for 3DGS refinement. To the best of our knowledge, TRACE-GS is the first to derive on-policy supervision from privileged geometry for sparse-view 3DGS restoration, achieving consistent gains and strong generalization across datasets and sparse-view settings.

</details>


### [23] [CasDeblurGS: Cascaded 2D-to-3D Multi-View Consistency for 3D Gaussian Splatting from Two Blurry Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10345)
*Haeyun Choi, Minhyuk Jang, I-Gil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (51.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CasDeblurGS 提出一种级联框架，仅从两张运动模糊图像（已知内参、无位姿、无辅助清晰图、无逐场景优化）重建一致的 3D 高斯泼溅场景，通过从局部 2D 对应到全局 3D 引导的渐进式恢复实现高质量新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由视角 3D 场景媒体对沉浸式应用日益重要，但实际采集常面临视角稀疏与运动模糊。现有模糊感知神经渲染方法通常需要大量多视角冗余、精确相机位姿或昂贵的逐场景优化，难以满足仅有两张模糊图像的严苛实际场景。

Method: 提出级联框架 CasDeblurGS：Stage 1 通过遮挡感知的对应过滤构建局部可靠的 2D 对应引导；Stage 2 将中间恢复结果聚合为无位姿的临时 3D 高斯表示，其输入视角重渲染提供密集全局引导用于最终恢复，从而获得更一致的 3D 表示与更高质量的新视角合成。

Result: 在真实与合成 Deblur-NeRF 场景上均优于强基线，PSNR 分别提升 1.19 dB 和 2.11 dB。渐进消融、跨视角对应可视化与相机重投影分析进一步验证了渲染质量与多视角几何一致性的提升。

Conclusion: CasDeblurGS 证明在仅有两张模糊图像、无位姿与无逐场景优化的极端稀疏设置下，通过级联的 2D 到 3D 多视角一致性恢复能够实现连贯的 3D 重建与高质量新视角合成。

Classification reason: 论文提出 CasDeblurGS，从两张模糊图像重建 3D 场景并生成新视角，属于 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）稀疏视角重建与神经渲染方向，与 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）高度匹配。

Importance reason: 该论文聚焦于从两张模糊图像重建3D场景，属于3D重建方向，与用户优先关注的连续多模态模型、视频/图像生成及世界模型等主题关联较弱。 Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, 运动模糊图像重建, 稀疏视角3D重建, 无位姿重建, 新视角合成

Abstract: Free-viewpoint 3D scene media is increasingly important for immersive applications, yet practical capture often suffers from severe view sparsity and motion blur. Although neural rendering has advanced sparse-view synthesis, existing blur-aware methods typically require substantial multi-view redundancy, accurate camera poses, or costly per-scene optimization. We address a stringent yet practical setting: reconstructing a coherent 3D scene from only two motion-blurred images with known intrinsics, without input-view poses, auxiliary sharp images, or per-scene test-time optimization. To this end, we propose CasDeblurGS, a cascaded framework that progressively recovers reliable cross-view information from local 2D correspondences to global 3D guidance. Stage 1 constructs locally reliable guidance through occlusion-aware correspondence filtering, while Stage 2 aggregates the intermediate restorations into a provisional pose-free 3D Gaussian representation whose input-view re-renders provide dense global guidance for final restoration. The resulting views enable a more coherent 3D representation and higher-quality novel-view synthesis. Experiments on real-world and synthetic Deblur-NeRF scenes show consistent gains over strong baselines, improving PSNR by 1.19 dB and 2.11 dB, respectively. Progressive ablations, cross-view correspondence visualization, and camera reprojection analysis further demonstrate improvements in both rendering quality and multi-view geometric consistency.

</details>


### [24] [Gaussian Sculpting: End-to-End Controllable Surface Reconstruction via Field Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10602)
*Ke Jiaxin, Juncheng Liu, Yi Wang, Zhouhui Lian, Bin Liu, Shengfa Wang, Xiangjia He*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.GR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Method: 将高斯原语锚定在演化的可微表面上，使其引导 SDF 优化而非仅在后期提取表面。设计双层训练策略：外层循环优化 SDF 表示的几何，内层循环在几何固定下更新高斯参数。对高斯参数施加约束以保证与底层表面一致，并引入基于八叉树划分的多分辨率细分方案以保留细节并降低内存消耗。

Conclusion: 通过将高斯锚定到可微表面并采用双层优化策略，实现了端到端可控的高质量表面重建，兼顾了几何精度与外观保真度。

Importance reason: Solid 3D reconstruction contribution but outside the user's configured priority directions and subtopics, with no matching boost keywords. Signals: 3D surface reconstruction, Gaussian Splatting, SDF optimization, bi-level training, multi-resolution subdivision

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has recently enabled real-time novel view synthesis with impressive quality. However, it struggles to recover accurate surfaces under limited viewpoints and due to the inherent irregularity of Gaussian primitives. The resulting geometric errors are notoriously difficult to correct manually. To address these issues, we propose Gaussian Sculpting, a fully differentiable end-to-end framework for high-quality surface reconstruction. Our key insight is to anchor Gaussians onto an evolving differentiable surface, allowing them to guide signed distance field (SDF) optimization instead of extracting the surface only during post-processing. To enable stable gradient isolation during joint optimization, we design a bi-level training strategy in which the outer loop optimizes the geometry represented by the SDF, while the inner loop updates the Gaussians with the geometry fixed. We further impose constraints on Gaussian parameters to ensure consistency with the underlying surface, thereby improving both geometric and appearance fidelity during optimization. In addition, we introduce a multi-resolution subdivision scheme based on octree-like partitioning to preserve fine details while reducing memory consumption. Experiments on object-level scenes demonstrate that our method effectively removes redundant surfaces, recovers missing structures caused by limited viewpoints, and achieves strong reconstruction quality even at relatively low resolutions.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在约 1/20 的高斯数量下仍能大幅保留视觉质量，相比此前降低基元数量的方法，质量更好且更鲁棒，同时提供高度紧凑、可高效渲染的表征。

Conclusion: 该流水线作为后处理模块与 backbone 无关，能利用现有前馈方法的优势，实现紧凑且高效的 3D 高斯表征，为 3D 场景重建提供灵活的质量-效率权衡。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于 3D 高斯泼溅（3DGS）的紧凑化重建与渲染，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D Gaussian compression rather than the user's core continuous/multimodal generation or autoregressive video/image priorities. Signals: 3D Gaussian splatting, feed-forward reconstruction, saliency-guided merging, compact representation, backbone-agnostic post-processing

Abstract: 3D scene reconstruction, modeling, and rendering are highly relevant for numerous tasks, and 3D Gaussian splatting has become a standard choice in this context. Its feed-forward variants provide fast reconstruction from sparse input views but often produce per-pixel primitives, leading to highly redundant and thus inefficient representations. We present a structure-aware merging pipeline that takes per-pixel primitives from any feed-forward method and consolidates them into a compact, content-adaptive Gaussian set while largely retaining visual quality at just $\frac{1}{20}^\text{th}$ of the Gaussians of a per-pixel method. We group spatially coherent Gaussians of similar appearance into variable-size clusters via adaptive superpixel segmentation guided by a saliency map, which allocates fine segments to textured regions and coarse segments to homogeneous areas. We compress each cluster into a compact latent representation through a learned encoder, then match and consolidate representations across views based on geometric overlap and feature similarity via a learned merger. A level-of-detail decoder then produces the final Gaussians at a controllable resolution, enabling a flexible quality-efficiency trade-off at inference. As a post-processing module, the pipeline is backbone-agnostic, leveraging the strengths of existing feed-forward methods. This leads to better and more robust quality than achieved by previous approaches that target a reduction in primitive count, while providing a highly compact representation, that can be rendered efficiently.

</details>

Conclusion: PolyLayout 通过分层混合框架有效弥合了学术研究与零售应用之间的差距，在感知合理性与几何合规性之间取得良好平衡，并支持非矩形房间布局生成。

Classification reason: 论文提出 PolyLayout，一个面向零售场景的 3D 房间布局生成框架，属于 3D 场景生成范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 three_d_generation（3D 生成与重建）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D room layout generation, which is outside the user's core continuous multimodal, autoregressive generation, and world model priorities. Signals: 3D layout generation, VLM-guided, hierarchical generation, non-rectangular rooms, retail application

Abstract: Generating physically plausible 3D room layouts is essential for home furnishing retail, enabling customers to visualize products in their own homes and confidently make purchasing decisions. However, a gap exists between academic research and real-world application: existing solutions primarily focus on algorithmic strategies for furniture placement, largely neglecting the non-rectangular geometries and strict door/window constraints prevalent in real homes. To bridge the gap, we introduce a hybrid, hierarchical framework tailored for retail, specifically designed to support scalable spatial planning applications. Our system decouples generation into three stages: (1) functional furniture clustering and fine-grained intra-zone placement; (2) macro-routing guided by a vision-language model (VLM) to anchor both these clustered zones and any remaining standalone furniture within diverse polygonal boundaries; and (3) rule-based optimization for collision-free micro-arrangements that respect architectural constraints. We evaluate our system on production-scale catalogs and a representative set of irregular real-world topologies. Our results show that our approach attains the highest perceptual plausibility while maintaining good geometric compliance at relatively low latency, and extends to irregular boundaries that existing methods do not natively support.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出 LGS 框架，通过干预引导的密度控制策略（Gaussian Densify Policy）学习剪枝/添加分数来优化前馈式驾驶场景 3D 高斯重建的结构，并引入跨时间点查询聚合特征以提升属性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LiDAR 的前馈式高斯重建方法保持初始点与高斯原语的对应关系，将初始化原语集视为最终表示，无法像优化式 3DGS 那样在训练中累积梯度来决定场景表示如何稠密化；同时共享稀疏骨干仅隐式融合不同时间戳的观测，缺乏对单个原语的跨时间证据显式聚合。

Method: 提出 Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS) 框架：(1) 关键观察是剪枝或添加干预引起的局部梯度响应变化可揭示相应结构调整是否有利于重建，据此设计 Gaussian Densify Policy，学习包含 Prune 和 Addition 分数的 Densify Map，在推理时直接调整高斯结构；(2) 开发紧凑的 Cross-Time Point Query，显式检索并聚合其他时间戳高斯原语的邻近特征，用于可靠的属性预测。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Dataset 和 PandaSet 上的大量实验表明，LGS 在驾驶场景重建任务上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过干预引导的密度控制策略和跨时间点特征聚合，LGS 同时增强了高斯结构与原语属性，为前馈式驾驶场景重建提供了更优的表示学习方案。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于前馈式驾驶场景 3D 高斯重建，涉及 3DGS 结构优化（密度控制）与跨时间点特征聚合，属于 3D 生成与重建方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] three_d_generation。

Importance reason: Focuses on driving-scene 3D reconstruction, which is outside the user's configured priority directions and subtopics. Signals: 3D Gaussian reconstruction, driving scene, feed-forward reconstruction, intervention-guided density control, cross-time aggregation

Abstract: Feed-forward Gaussian reconstruction has recently emerged as an efficient approach for driving scene reconstruction. However, prevailing LiDAR-based methods preserve the initial correspondence between observed points and Gaussian primitives, treating the initialized primitive set as the final representation. Unlike optimization-based 3DGS, these methods cannot accumulate gradients during training to determine how the scenes representation should be densified. Meanwhile, the shared sparse backbone only fuses observations from different timestamps implicitly, without explicitly aggregating cross-time evidence for individual primitives. In this paper, we present Learning Gaussian Structure (LGS), a framework that enhances both Gaussian structure and primitive attributes. Our key observation is that changes in local gradient responses induced by a prune or add intervention reveal whether the corresponding structural adjustment benefits reconstruction. Based on this observation, our Gaussian Densify Policy learns a Densify Map comprising Prune and Addition Scores from controlled interventions, and directly adjusts the Gaussian structure during inference. We further develop a compact Cross-Time Point Query that explicitly retrieves and aggregates neighboring features from Gaussian primitives at other timestamps for reliable attribute prediction. Extensive experiments on the Waymo Open Dataset and PandaSet demonstrate that LGS consistently outperforms existing methods.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Latent-to-4D，一种直接 latent-to-4D 生成方法，绕过 RGB 视频重建，将视频扩散模型的去噪 latent 与预训练 4D 解码器的 token 网格对齐，并通过帧级与全局时空注意力细化，实现跨多个视频 DiT 的通用 4D 场景生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 4D 生成方法要么用独立 4D 模型重建生成的 RGB 视频（存在分布不匹配与误差传播），要么将 4D 预测绑定到特定视频生成器（更换生成器需重新训练）。作者希望利用共享 VAE 的视频模型最终去噪 latent 作为可复用的 4D 预测接口，实现与具体生成器解耦的通用 4D 生成。

Method: Latent-to-4D 方法：将视频扩散模型的去噪 latent 与预训练 4D 解码器的 token 网格对齐，绕过 RGB 重建；通过帧级与全局时空注意力对 latent 进行细化，直接预测显式 4D 表示。仅用约 1K 个重建片段训练，单个 checkpoint 可在同一 VAE 家族内的多个视频扩散 Transformer 上无需修改地迁移。

Result: 在 Text4D-200 和 I4D-200 基准上，Latent-to-4D 相比同 latent 的 Wan+4RC 级联方法，在投影式 DINO-F1 上分别提升 2.88-3.45 和 5.81 分；人类评估在几何质量、时间稳定性和整体质量上也更受偏好。

Conclusion: 视频模型的去噪 latent 可作为可复用的 4D 预测接口，直接 latent-to-4D 生成能有效避免 RGB 重建带来的分布不匹配与误差传播，同时实现跨生成器的通用性与迁移能力。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Latent-to-4D，直接从视频模型的去噪 latent 生成动态 4D 场景，属于 4D 动态场景生成方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] dynamic_4d_generation（4D 与动态场景生成）。同时涉及视频先验与未来预测，与 world_model 方向下的 video_world_model 也有一定关联。

Importance reason: Novel latent-to-4D interface bypassing RGB reconstruction, but tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: 4D generation, video diffusion latent, VAE-family transfer, spatiotemporal attention, bypasses RGB

Abstract: 4D generation synthesizes dynamic 3D scenes from conditions such as text or images. Existing methods either reconstruct generated RGB videos with a separate 4D model or adapt a particular video generator to predict geometry directly. The former suffers from distribution mismatch and error propagation, whereas the latter ties 4D prediction to a specific generator and may require retraining when the generator or conditioning regime changes. We ask whether the final denoised latents of video models that share a variational autoencoder (VAE) can instead provide a reusable interface to explicit 4D prediction. Building on this insight, we introduce direct latent-to-4D generation and instantiate it as Latent-to-4D, which bypasses RGB by aligning a video latent with the token grid of a pretrained 4D decoder and refining it through frame-wise and global spatiotemporal attention. Trained on roughly 1K existing reconstruction clips, a single checkpoint transfers unchanged across multiple video diffusion transformers within the same VAE family. On Text4D-200 and I4D-200, Latent-to-4D surpasses matched same-latent Wan+4RC cascades in projection-based DINO-F1 by 2.88--3.45 and 5.81 points, respectively, while also being preferred by human raters for geometry, temporal stability, and overall quality.

</details>

Conclusion: 通过将共存作为显式优化目标而非偶然属性，可以训练出更可靠的多水印共存系统，支持内容真实性与版权的分层来源信号。

Importance reason: Watermark coexistence is a niche security topic with no direct alignment to your core continuous multimodal, autoregressive generation, or world model priorities. Signals: visual watermarking, joint optimization, video watermarking, provenance, content authenticity

Abstract: We present a method for training imperceptible visual watermarks to coexist with other such watermarks. Recent work has shown that independently trained image watermarking models can coexist with surprisingly limited interference, enabling watermark ensembling. However, this coexistence is a serendipitous property rather than an explicit optimization objective, leaving interference uncontrolled and potentially reducing decoding robustness or visual quality. We first show empirically that the same coexistence property extends to video watermarking. We then show that both image and video watermarks can be trained with a decoder-aware objective to improve coexistence. Our results suggest a practical path to signpost watermarks that indicate the presence of independently deployed provenance watermarking systems, supporting layered provenance signaling for content authenticity and rights.

</details>

Result: 在真实与合成基准上，该方法在重建保真度和时间一致性上均优于现有最先进方法，有效减少插值伪影。

Conclusion: 事件相机的高时间分辨率线索能有效增强预训练扩散模型的视频帧插值能力，适配器方案在保持模型可复用性的同时显著提升插值质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于事件相机引导的视频帧插值（Video Frame Interpolation），属于视频生成/编辑范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）合理相关，故复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Solid but niche application of event cameras to video interpolation, with limited direct alignment to your core continuous-model and autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: video frame interpolation, event camera, diffusion model, adapter-based, sparse optical flow

Abstract: Latent diffusion models have recently advanced video frame interpolation by synthesizing intermediate frames between input images. However, handling large temporal gaps and complex motion remains challenging, often resulting in motion blur, structural distortions, and temporal inconsistencies. Event cameras provide high-temporal-resolution motion cues that are well suited for bridging these gaps and improving interpolation quality. To exploit this advantage without training an event-assisted model from scratch, we propose an adapter-based framework that incorporates event-derived cues into a pre-trained image-to-video diffusion model with minimal architectural changes. Specifically, our method leverages Image Warped Events (IWEs) and bidirectional sparse optical flow to provide spatially and temporally aligned guidance during generation. By injecting these event-guided structural and motion cues into the diffusion process, our approach reduces interpolation artifacts and improves both reconstruction fidelity and temporal coherence. Experimental results on real and synthetic benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is at .

</details>
