### [31] [SafeCA: Safe Cross-Attention Localization and Regulation for Text-to-Video Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10933)
*Siyuan Liang, Yupeng Qiu, Junfeng Fang, Rong-Cheng Tu, Jiaxing Huang, Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (70.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SafeCA 是一种面向文生视频（T2V）模型的架构级越狱防御方法。论文首次在交叉注意力特征空间中发现干净样本与越狱样本之间存在累积分离效应和线性可分性递增趋势，据此提出通过注意力稳定性分析定位防御区域、能量归一化注意力掩码、轻量语义空间适配器以及反向传播抑制恶意 token 的防御机制，可将主流 T2V 模型的越狱成功率降低约 20%，且几乎不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文生视频模型在实际部署中易受越狱攻击，产生有害或不恰当内容。现有防御方法主要依赖输入过滤或重建，不仅计算延迟高，还容易扭曲语义。作者希望提出一种低开销、保持语义一致性的特征级防御方案。

Method: SafeCA 首先在交叉注意力特征空间系统分析干净与越狱样本的差异，发现累积分离效应与线性可分性递增趋势。防御流程包括：(1) 通过单次推理中干净提示的交叉注意力特征进行注意力稳定性分析，定位关键防御区域与数值；(2) 采用能量归一化的注意力掩码缓解异常激活；(3) 引入轻量语义空间适配器重定向异常语义流；(4) 通过反向传播特征异常信号到输入提示词，检测并抑制潜在恶意 token。

Result: 在主流 T2V 模型上，SafeCA 将越狱成功率降低约 20%，推理开销几乎为零（仅 +0.1s），并保持良好的文本-视频语义一致性。

Conclusion: SafeCA 提供了一种架构级、可部署的 T2V 生成模型保护范式，在保持语义一致性的同时有效防御越狱攻击，且计算开销极低，适合商业模型部署。

Classification reason: 论文针对文生视频模型的越狱攻击防御，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。虽然该子主题在 multimodal_generation 方向下为非 canonical 条目，但语义完全匹配（越狱防御、对抗攻击），且 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 canonical multimodal_safety_attack 也高度相关。选择 multimodal_generation 下的 multimodal_safety_attack 作为主方向，因为论文聚焦于 T2V 生成模型的防御。

Importance reason: Focuses on T2V jailbreak defense, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive/diffusion video generation. Signals: T2V jailbreak defense, cross-attention feature analysis, architecture-level defense, low inference overhead, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Text-to-Video (T2V) generative models are vulnerable to jailbreak attacks in real-world deployment, leading them to produce harmful or inappropriate content. Existing defense approaches mainly rely on input filtering or reconstruction, which not only incur high computational latency but also tend to distort semantics. To address these issues, we experimentally and systematically analyze the differences between clean and jailbreak samples in the cross-attention feature space, revealing for the first time a cumulative separation effect and a progressively increasing trend of linear separability between the two during the diffusion process. Based on this insight, we propose SafeCA, a feature-level defense mechanism for safe cross-attention localization and regularization. Firstly, we identify key defensive regions and values through attention stability analysis using cross-attention features collected from clean prompts within a single inference. Secondly, SafeCA mitigates anomalous activations via attention masking with energy normalization and introduces a lightweight semantic-space adapter to redirect abnormal semantic flows. Furthermore, we detect and suppress potentially malicious tokens by back-propagating feature anomaly signals to the input cue words, thereby enhancing the deployability of the defense in commercial models. Experimental results show that SafeCA reduces the jailbreak success rate by about 20% on mainstream T2V models, adds almost no inference overhead (+0.1s), and maintains good text-video semantic consistency. Overall, SafeCA provides an architecture-level, deployable protection paradigm for T2V generation models.

</details>


### [32] [MarkNull: Model-Agnostic Watermark Removal in AI-Generated Images via On-Manifold Latent Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10166)
*Jie Cao, Qi Li, Zelin Zhang, Xiaodong Wu, Lingshuang Liu, Xiangman Li, Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CR

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (47.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MarkNull 提出一种模型无关的 AI 生成图像水印移除攻击方法，通过在流形潜空间中进行操作，利用噪声-潜空间对齐分数（NLAS）量化水印图像中潜表征与嵌入初始噪声之间的统计依赖，并优化目标以选择性去相关潜表征与嵌入水印，同时保持语义保真度。该方法在多种水印范式（post-hoc、微调、初始噪声）上平均将比特准确率降至 53.14%（接近随机猜测 50%），且无明显视觉退化，并成功攻破 Google SynthID-Image 系统，还提供了 MarkNull-A 摊销变体（0.50s/图像）及对应的攻击检测防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字水印是 AI 生成图像溯源与版权归属的关键技术，但现有水印移除攻击要么只对特定生成模型有效，要么以严重视觉退化为代价。作者希望提出一种模型无关、保持视觉质量的水印移除攻击方法，以揭示现有水印方案的脆弱性。

Method: MarkNull 基于关键观察：带水印图像在生成潜表征与嵌入初始噪声之间存在强统计依赖。作者引入噪声-潜空间对齐分数（NLAS）量化该依赖，并构建优化目标，选择性去相关潜表征与嵌入水印，同时保持语义保真度。此外提出 MarkNull-A，一种摊销、免优化的变体，将攻击蒸馏为单次前向传播，实现 0.50s/图像的推理速度。最后提出攻击检测机制作为防御对应物。

Result: 在 post-hoc、微调、初始噪声三类水印范式上，MarkNull 将平均比特准确率降至 53.14%，接近随机猜测（50%），且无可感知的图像退化。MarkNull-A 以 0.50s/图像的速度实现类似效果，计算开销适中。攻击成功攻破 Google SynthID-Image 系统，并有效迁移到视频水印。

Conclusion: 研究表明现有主流水印方案在模型无关的潜空间攻击下是脆弱的，凸显了设计对潜空间攻击具有鲁棒性的水印方案的必要性。同时，作者提出的攻击检测机制作为防御对应物，为水印安全研究提供了双向视角。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 AI 生成图像中数字水印的模型无关移除攻击（MarkNull），属于生成模型安全与攻防范畴。虽然 multimodal_safety_attack 在 multimodal_generation 方向下是非 canonical 子主题，但它与论文主题高度契合（对抗攻击、防御机制），且比 canonical 的 generation_alignment 等更贴切。

Importance reason: Solid watermark-removal attack work, but it targets security/attack-defense rather than the user's core generative-modeling and efficiency priorities. Signals: watermark removal, model-agnostic attack, latent manipulation, SynthID-Image, amortized variant

Abstract: Digital watermarking has emerged as a critical technique for provenance and copyright attribution in AI-generated imagery, yet its robustness against realistic, model-agnostic removal attacks remains poorly explored. Existing attacks either succeed only against specific generative models or achieve removal at the cost of severe visual degradation. In this paper, we propose MarkNull, a model-agnostic watermark removal attack via on-manifold latent manipulation. MarkNull is grounded in a key observation: watermarked images exhibit a strong statistical dependency between the generated latent representation and the embedded initial noise. To quantify this dependency, we introduce the Noise-Latent Alignment Score (NLAS) and formulate an optimization objective that selectively decorrelates the latent representation from the embedded watermark while preserving semantic fidelity. Extensive evaluations across different categories of watermarking paradigms, including post-hoc, fine-tuning-based, and initial-noise-based schemes, demonstrate that MarkNull reduces average bit accuracy to 53.14%, approaching random-guessing (50%), without perceptible image degradation. To further improve scalability, we propose MarkNull-A, an amortized, optimization-free variant that distills the attack into a single forward pass, achieving 0.50 s/image with modest computational overhead. Notably, our attacks successfully compromise Google's SynthID-Image system while preserving high visual quality and transfer effectively to video watermarking. Finally, we present an attack detection mechanism as a defensive counterpart to MarkNull and MarkNull-A, highlighting the necessity of developing watermark designs resilient to model-agnostic latent-space attacks.

</details>

*Ananya Bal, Kartik Sharma, Ethan Lai, Samyak Tiwari, Liza Dahiya, Chaitanya Chawla, Laszlo A. Jeni*

TL;DR: MAD-HOI 提出一种掩码自回归扩散模型，用于从文本生成关节式手-物体交互序列。该方法在连续潜空间中编码手与物体运动并保持解耦，通过掩码自回归 transformer 预测上下文特征，再由 flow-matching 头生成运动，支持原子/复合序列生成、条件运动补全与填充以及 EOM 终止预测，在 ARCTIC 和 GRAB 数据集上取得更优的多样性与物理合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动的 HOI 生成方法主要关注生成平滑且物理合理的轨迹，但缺乏序列级灵活性：标准扩散模型仅针对原子动作训练且需预先指定运动长度，自回归方法虽灵活但依赖离散运动码，会丢失接触敏感的运动细节。作者希望构建一个同时支持变长生成、复合运动序列、运动补全与填充、可靠终止且保持物理合理性的统一方法。

Method: MAD-HOI 首先将手与物体运动编码到连续潜空间并保持解耦以维持流级控制；随后使用掩码自回归 transformer 预测上下文特征，这些特征作为条件输入到 flow-matching 头生成运动。单一训练目标即可同时支持原子与复合关节序列生成、条件运动补全与填充，以及 EOM（End of Motion）预测。

Result: 在 ARCTIC 和 GRAB 数据集上的综合评测表明，MAD-HOI 相比其他开源基线方法能生成更多样且物理更合理的交互序列，并支持多种序列级能力（变长生成、复合序列、补全填充、EOM 终止）。

Conclusion: 通过将掩码自回归与扩散/flow-matching 结合，MAD-HOI 在保持物理合理性的同时获得了序列级灵活性，为 HOI 生成提供了一种统一且实用的范式，弥补了扩散模型与自回归方法各自的局限。

Classification reason: 该论文研究文本驱动的双手-物体交互（HOI）序列生成，属于人体动作/手部动作生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度相关，直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Combines masked autoregression with flow matching for HOI generation, but the hand-object interaction domain is outside the user's configured priority subtopics. Signals: flow matching, autoregressive generation, continuous latent space, hand-object interaction, text-to-motion

Abstract: Methods for text-based generation of hand-object interaction (HOI) sequences primarily focus on producing smooth, physically plausible trajectories. A truly utilitarian method should additionally support variable-length generation, composite motion sequences, motion completion and infilling, and reliable termination without compromising physical plausibility. Standard diffusion models for HOI generation are typically trained only for text-to-motion generation on atomic motions and require the motion length to be specified a-priori. Autoregressive (AR) methods provide greater sequence-level flexibility, but commonly depend on discrete motion codes, which can lose contact-sensitive motion detail. To address these key limitations, we present a model performing Masked Autoregression with Diffusion for HOI generation (MAD-HOI). Our method starts by encoding hand and object motions in a continuous latent space while keeping them disentangled to maintain stream-wise control. This is followed by a masked autoregressive transformer to predict context features that condition a flow-matching head. MAD-HOI is capable of motion generation for atomic and composite articulated sequences, conditioned motion completion and infilling, as well as EOM (End of Motion) prediction from a single training objective. We provide comprehensive evaluations for these capabilities and benchmark our method on the ARCTIC and GRAB datasets. Our experiments demonstrate that our method generates more diverse and physically plausible interactions compared to other open-sourced baseline methods.

</details>

*Haozhong Xiong, Yao Yu, Yu Zhou, Sidan Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DSAR双流自回归框架，显式建模布料物理的时间因果性，通过几何流传播表面位移、状态流融合历史状态，实现逼真且时序一致的可动画化人体虚拟化身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法从RGB视频创建可动画化人体化身时难以捕捉真实布料动态，产生过度平滑外观或在分布外姿态下出现严重伪影。根本原因在于现有方法忽略了布料物理的时间因果性——当前状态由先前状态通过时间演化而来，而非仅由瞬时骨骼姿态决定。缺乏对这种因果结构的显式建模，网络学到的是姿态-外观相关性而非运动演化，导致泛化能力差。

Method: 提出双流自回归框架DSAR，显式建模可观测几何信息和隐式内部状态。几何流从上一帧传播表面位移；状态流将当前特征与从记忆库检索的历史状态融合。采用运动自适应聚合处理空间变化的动态，并用自适应正则化在平滑性与灵活性之间取得平衡。

Result: 在多个挑战性数据集上的实验表明，该方法在渲染质量、时序一致性和对训练分布之外运动模式的泛化能力上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 双流时序建模能够实现逼真的布料动态，验证了显式建模布料物理时间因果性对可动画化人体化身生成的重要性。

Classification reason: 论文研究从RGB视频创建可动画化逼真人体虚拟化身，重点建模布料动态（cloth dynamics），属于数字人与动作生成范畴，映射到[CANONICAL] subtopic avatar_motion_generation。

Importance reason: Focuses on avatar cloth dynamics, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive video/image generation. Signals: autoregressive, video generation, controllable generation, world model, temporal causality

Abstract: Creating photorealistic and temporally coherent animatable human avatars from RGB videos remains challenging. Current methods struggle to capture realistic cloth dynamics, producing over-smoothed appearance or severe artifacts on out-of-distribution poses. This limitation stems from a fundamental oversight: existing approaches neglect the temporal causality inherent in cloth physics, where current states emerge from previous states through temporal evolution rather than instantaneous skeletal configurations alone. Without explicit modeling of this causal structure, networks learn pose-appearance correlations instead of motion evolution, leading to poor generalization. We introduce a dual-stream autoregressive framework that explicitly models both observable geometric information and implicit internal state. The geometric stream propagates surface displacement from the previous frame, while the state stream fuses current features with historical states retrieved from a memory bank. Motion-adaptive aggregation handles spatially-varying dynamics, and adaptive regularization balances smoothness with flexibility. Experiments on challenging datasets demonstrate significant improvements in rendering quality, temporal consistency, and generalization to motion patterns beyond training distributions, validating that dual-stream temporal modeling enables realistic cloth dynamics.

</details>

*Rajmund Nagy, Silvia Arellano García, Hendric Voss, Mihail Tsakov, Taras Kucherenko, Youngwoo Yoon, Gustav Eje Henter*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音驱动手势生成是数字人与具身交互的重要方向，但缺乏大规模、解耦的评测方法来公平比较不同系统，且现有评测难以隔离动作质量与语音对齐的混淆因素，也难以评估系统对对话者反应的响应能力。

Method: 采用解耦评测方法论分别评估动作质量与语音对齐；进行双人错配研究以隔离倾听与回应对话者的效果；新增语义手势生成任务，使用 Grounded Gestures 子集进行文本错配评测。共开展四项大规模用户研究，收集超过 23,000 票。

Result: 数据集的过滤片段在动作真实度上显著优于所有参赛系统（68-95% 成对胜率）；语音对齐研究中动捕片段达到 62% 对齐分数上限，最佳提交仅 32%；双人研究中动捕达到 65% 恰当性分数上限，但无系统显著高于随机；语义错配评测中数据集手势表达力强（79% 识别率），但最佳系统仅 8% 恰当性分数。

Conclusion: 当前语音驱动手势生成系统在动作质量、语音对齐、对话响应和语义表达方面均与真实动捕数据存在显著差距，尤其是语义手势生成几乎完全失败，表明该领域仍有巨大提升空间。评测数据与输出将公开以促进可复现研究。

Classification reason: 该论文是关于语音驱动手势生成（speech-driven gesture generation）的大规模评测挑战赛，属于数字人与动作生成范畴，映射到 CANONICAL 子主题 avatar_motion_generation。

Importance reason: A benchmark paper on speech-driven gesture generation, which is outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: gesture generation, human evaluation, benchmark, speech-driven, dyadic interaction

Abstract: This preprint presents the results of the fourth GENEA Challenge, a large-scale human evaluation of five speech-driven gesture-generation systems trained by participating teams on the Seamless Interaction dataset of dyadic conversations. As in the 2023 GENEA Challenge, we used a disentangled evaluation methodology to assess motion quality and speech alignment without confounding between the two, and performed a dyadic mismatching study to isolate the effect of listening and reacting to the interlocutor. We additionally introduce a new semantic gesture-generation task and a text-mismatching evaluation methodology using the Grounded Gestures subset of the data. In total, we ran four large-scale user studies, collecting over 23,000 votes from 869 test-takers. In the motion-realism study, the dataset's filtered segments had substantially higher motion quality than all challenge submissions (68-95% pairwise winrate). In the speech-alignment study, the motion-capture segments provided a conceptual ceiling at 62% alignment score, with the top submission significantly behind at 32% and the rest only slightly above the 0% expected of an input-independent system. In the dyadic study, motion capture again set the ceiling at 65% appropriateness score, but no submission scored substantially above chance, indicating that the systems could not yet respond to the interlocutor. Finally, the semantic mismatching evaluation found highly expressive gestures in the dataset (test-takers identified the matching transcript 79% of the time), yet almost all submissions failed to generate semantically expressive motion, with the best achieving only an 8% appropriateness score. The collected votes and outputs will be made publicly available at to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>

TL;DR: 提出 latent bridge matching (LBM) 框架，用于从平扫乳腺 DCE-MRI 合成增强期图像，通过学习平扫与增强期 VAE latent 之间的条件桥而非从高斯噪声出发，肿瘤条件化变体在 DUKE 验证集上优于 LDM 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出 latent bridge matching (LBM) 框架：与从高斯噪声出发的传统 LDM 不同，模型学习平扫与峰值增强 VAE latent 之间的条件桥；latent UNet 从中间桥状态预测到峰值增强 latent 的剩余校正，实现迭代细化并保持轨迹锚定于患者特定解剖结构。评估了两种条件化变体（平扫条件化与肿瘤条件化）。

Result: 在 91 例 DUKE 验证集上，肿瘤条件化变体优于平扫条件化，MSE 从 1.023 降至 0.940，FRD 从 7.523 降至 4.716，肿瘤 SSIM 从 0.355 提升至 0.429；肿瘤条件化 LBM 也优于 LDM 基线。

Conclusion: latent bridge matching 是一种有前景的平扫锚定虚拟对比增强方案，但需进一步验证泛化性并消除推理时对真实肿瘤掩膜的依赖。

Importance reason: Medical imaging synthesis with negative keywords and no overlap with user's priority directions. Signals: latent bridge matching, DCE-MRI synthesis, tumor-conditioned, diffusion baseline, medical imaging

Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (DCE-MRI) is central to breast cancer imaging, but gadolinium administration increases scan burden and motivates contrast-reduced alternatives, including synthetic contrast generation. We propose a latent bridge matching (LBM) framework for synthesizing peak-enhanced breast DCE-MRI from pre-contrast images in the MAMA-SYNTH challenge setting. Instead of starting from Gaussian noise as in conventional latent diffusion models (LDMs), the proposed model learns a conditional bridge between paired pre-contrast and peak-enhanced VAE latents. A latent UNet predicts the remaining correction from intermediate bridge states to the peak-enhanced latent, enabling iterative refinement while keeping the trajectory anchored to patient-specific anatomy. We evaluated two LBM conditioning variants on 91 DUKE validation cases. For the tumor-conditioned variant, tumor masks were used as conditioning inputs. Tumor-conditioning improved performance compared with pre-contrast conditioning, reducing MSE from 1.023 to 0.940 and FRD from 7.523 to 4.716, while increasing tumor SSIM from 0.355 to 0.429. The tumor-conditioned LBM also outperformed the evaluated LDM baseline on this validation cohort. These results suggest that latent bridge matching is a promising pre-contrast-anchored formulation for virtual contrast enhancement, while further work is needed to validate generalization and remove dependence on ground-truth tumor masks at inference.

</details>

Conclusion: JPEG2000 压缩是迈向可扩展 3D MRI 生成建模的实用步骤，在不降低合成质量的前提下显著降低存储与 I/O 成本。

Classification reason: 论文研究通过 JPEG2000/JPEG-LS 有损压缩降低 3D MRI 数据存储与 I/O 成本，从而在普通硬件上高效训练 3D 生成模型（Wavelet Flow Matching），属于生成模型训练效率优化，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）。

Abstract: Large-scale multi-modal MRI datasets impose substantial storage and I/O costs, limiting the training of 3D generative models on commodity infrastructure. While lossy compression is known to preserve accuracy for discriminative segmentation networks, its effect on generative models, which must learn the full data distribution rather than a decision boundary, is unexplored. We study whether standard image codecs can effectively compress semantically rich brain tumor MRI while preserving the fidelity required to train and deploy a 3D MRI generative model. Each 3D volume is compressed with JPEG2000 or a near-lossless JPEG-LS pipeline. Next, a Wavelet Flow Matching model, conditioned on BraTS image sequences (T1n, T1c, T2, T2f), is trained on compressed data, and the resulting models are evaluated on the validation set. At a 20:1 compression ratio, synthesis quality is statistically equivalent to a model trained on uncompressed data within a pre-specified margin ($\Delta$PSNR $<1$,dB, $\Delta$SSIM $<0.02$; paired TOST $p=[[p]]$): mean PSNR is 27.3,dB vs. 27.0,dB and mean SSIM is 0.95 vs. 0.96 across modalities. Our results indicate that JPEG2000 compression is a practical step toward scalable 3D MRI generative modeling without degrading synthesis quality. The codebase is available at .

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SparSTAR 是一种针对 InfinityStar 视频自回归生成框架的训练无关块稀疏注意力方法。它在每个昂贵的尺度和注意力头上对连续 key 块进行打分，保留必要的条件上下文，并通过前向稀疏路径执行所选块，实现约1.6倍端到端加速，同时保持 VBench 和重建保真度接近稠密 InfinityStar。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InfinityStar 将视觉自回归生成扩展到视频，通过图像和片段金字塔序列实现。但其变化的尺度和跨片段上下文使得后期尺度注意力计算代价高昂，且从扩散或图像 VAR 模型复用的稀疏模式在此场景下不可靠。因此需要一种针对该设置定制的稀疏注意力方法。

Method: SparSTAR 是一种训练无关的块稀疏注意力方法。在每个昂贵的尺度和注意力头上，它从当前 query 和 key 激活中对连续 key 块进行打分，保留所需的条件上下文，并通过仅前向的稀疏路径执行所选块。论文还分析了片段内跨尺度一致性、跨片段边界的模式持续性，以及复用跨越来越远尺度的掩码时的质量退化，发现重要 key 块会移动，因此在每个目标尺度重新计算块选择比复用转移掩码更可靠。

Result: 在 720p 文生视频和图像生视频任务上，SparSTAR 保留所有 token 和细化尺度，提供约1.6倍端到端加速，同时保持 VBench 和配对输出重建保真度接近稠密 InfinityStar。

Conclusion: SparSTAR 证明了针对视频自回归生成框架定制的训练无关稀疏注意力可以有效加速推理，同时保持生成质量。其关键洞察是跨尺度重新计算块选择比复用转移掩码更可靠，为后续视频自回归生成的高效化提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SparSTAR，一种针对视频自回归生成（InfinityStar）的训练无关块稀疏注意力方法，通过稀疏化注意力计算实现约1.6倍端到端加速。这属于生成模型效率优化范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 的 efficient_generation（生成模型加速与高效化）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free sparse attention acceleration for autoregressive video generation directly targets the user's high-priority efficiency and video generation interests. Signals: autoregressive video generation, efficient inference, training-free acceleration, sparse attention, video generation

Abstract: InfinityStar extends visual autoregressive generation to video through a sequence of image and clip pyramids. Its changing scale and cross-clip context, however, leave late-scale attention costly and make sparse patterns reused from diffusion or image VAR models unreliable. We introduce SparSTAR, a training-free block-sparse attention method tailored to this setting. At each expensive scale and attention head, SparSTAR scores contiguous key blocks from the current query and key activations, retains required conditioning context, and executes the selected blocks through a forward-only sparse path. We analyze cross-scale consistency within a clip, pattern persistence across clip boundaries, and quality degradation as reuse spans increasingly distant scales. Across these analyses, important key blocks shift, showing that recomputing block selection at each target scale is more reliable than reusing a transferred mask. On 720p text-to-video and image-to-video generation, SparSTAR preserves every token and refinement scale while providing about a 1.6x end-to-end speedup and maintaining VBench and paired-output reconstruction fidelity close to dense InfinityStar.

</details>

TL;DR: PCFlow 提出一种感知一致的 flow matching 图像复原框架，通过 latent consistency flow 目标实现稳定高效的少步推理，并引入 Latent Consistency Perceptual Loss 在引导速度场上施加语义约束，配合无冲突梯度投影策略平衡结构与感知一致性，以轻量卷积骨干在多种复原任务上以极低计算成本取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像复原面临失真与感知质量之间的根本权衡：最小化像素误差导致过度平滑，而优化感知真实感常引入结构偏差。现有方法通过后验采样或多阶段生成管线来平衡这一权衡，但计算开销大且架构复杂。

Method: 提出 PCFlow 统一框架，直接参数化从退化观测到干净目标的连续传输，联合优化失真与感知质量。采用 latent consistency flow 目标驱动稳定高效的少步推理；引入 Latent Consistency Perceptual Loss (LCPL) 在引导速度场上施加语义约束；针对结构与感知一致性冲突，集成无冲突梯度投影策略稳定多目标优化；采用轻量纯卷积骨干。

Result: 在多种图像复原任务上以极低计算成本取得有竞争力的性能，相比传统方法大幅降低计算开销。

Conclusion: PCFlow 通过统一 flow matching 框架有效平衡失真与感知质量，以轻量架构实现高效少步图像复原，验证了在引导速度场上施加感知约束与梯度投影策略的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 PCFlow，一种基于 flow matching 的图像复原统一框架，通过 latent consistency flow 实现少步高效推理，属于生成模型加速与高效化方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_generation 子主题。

Importance reason: Efficient flow-matching image restoration with perceptual consistency, but tangential to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and autoregressive generation. Signals: flow matching, efficient few-step inference, image restoration, perceptual consistency, lightweight backbone

Abstract: Image restoration is fundamentally constrained by the tradeoff between distortion and perception: minimizing pixel-wise error yields over-smoothed results, whereas optimizing for perceptual realism often introduces structural deviations. Recent approaches attempt to balance this tradeoff via posterior sampling or multi-stage generative pipelines, yet remain computationally expensive and architecturally complex. To overcome these limitations, we propose PCFlow (Perceptually Consistent Flow Matching), a unified framework that directly parameterizes a continuous transport from degraded observations to clean targets, jointly optimizing distortion and perceptual quality. While its latent consistency flow objective drives stable and efficient few-step inference, a Latent Consistency Perceptual Loss (LCPL) imposes semantic constraints directly on the guiding velocity field, steering the dynamics toward visually sharp data manifolds. Furthermore, recognizing the inherent conflict between structural and perceptual consistencies, we integrate a conflict-free gradient projection strategy to stabilize the multi-objective optimization landscape. Combined with lightweight, convolution-only backbone, PCFlow achieves competitive performance across diverse restoration tasks at a fraction of traditional computational costs.

</details>

### [40] [ELVAE: Evidential Learning-Based Variational Autoencoder for Uncertainty-Aware Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10398)
*Ge Wang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型对齐与奖励

TL;DR: ELVAE 是一种基于证据学习的变分自编码器，每个潜坐标由输入相关的 normal-inverse-gamma 后验控制，显式区分潜位置不确定性与其周围变异性，并将该不确定性作为生成时的控制变量：低不确定性锚点生成更可靠的样本，高不确定性锚点可用于压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 VAE 从概率潜表征生成样本，但无法区分关于潜位置的不确定性与围绕它的变异性。作者希望显式建模潜位置不确定性，并将其用于生成过程而非仅在推理后报告。

Method: 提出 ELVAE，每个潜坐标由输入相关的 normal-inverse-gamma 后验控制，形成层次结构以显式表达潜位置不确定性。目标函数为精确的证据下界（ELBO），并证明需要对完整层次进行直接正则化，因为边缘化的潜分布无法识别不确定性分解。在 MNIST 生成实验中结合冻结的外部分类器进行验证。

Result: 在 MNIST 生成实验中，潜位置不确定性清晰地区分了生成数字的语义可靠性。零位移对照实验表明大部分效果来自锚点可被可靠重新生成的能力，而较小但独立的部分来自不确定性缩放的扰动本身。效果仅在类内不确定性排序下成立，且幅度随随机种子变化。

Conclusion: 学习到的潜位置不确定性可作为不确定性感知生成的实际控制变量，将锚点可靠性与扰动导致的失败分离，支持更可靠的合成样本生成与压力测试。

Classification reason: 该论文提出基于证据学习的 VAE（ELVAE），利用潜空间位置不确定性作为生成时的控制变量，实现不确定性感知的生成（uncertainty-aware generation），属于生成模型的可控性与可靠性方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）子主题。

Importance reason: Novel uncertainty-aware VAE framing, but weak empirical scope (MNIST only) and tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: evidential learning, uncertainty-aware generation, VAE, latent uncertainty, stress testing

Abstract: Variational autoencoders generate samples from probabilistic latent representations but do not distinguish uncertainty about the latent location from variability around it. We formulate ELVAE, an evidential learning-based VAE in which each latent coordinate is governed by an input-dependent normal-inverse-gamma posterior. This hierarchy yields an explicit latent-location uncertainty that can be used during generation, not merely reported after inference: low-uncertainty anchors support more reliable synthetic samples, while high-uncertainty anchors can be deliberately exploited for stress testing. The objective is an exact evidence lower bound, and we show that direct regularization of the full hierarchy is required, since the marginalized latent law alone cannot identify the uncertainty decomposition. In an MNIST generation pilot with a frozen external classifier, this uncertainty clearly stratified the semantic reliability of generated digits. A zero-displacement control revealed that most of the effect reflects how reliably an anchor can be re-generated, while a smaller but distinct component is attributable to uncertainty-scaled perturbation itself. The effect holds only under within-class uncertainty ranking, and its magnitude varies across seeds. These findings support the learned latent-location uncertainty as a practical control variable for uncertainty-aware generation, separating anchor reliability from perturbation-induced failure.

</details>


### [41] [PEAK: Precise and Persistent Concept Erasure via k-Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10985)
*Man Jiang, Ouxiang Li, Weibao Xue, Zhenhua Tang, Yuan Wang, Shuo Wang, Yanbin Hao*

TL;DR: PEAK 提出基于 k-Sparse Autoencoders (kSAE) 的精确且持久的概念擦除框架，通过将扩散模型内部激活分解为可解释稀疏特征，定位目标概念相关特征并嵌入参数优化，实现无需推理时干预的鲁棒概念擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法难以同时实现精确性与持久性：概念相关表征定位不准确会导致语义干扰，而底层概念知识移除不彻底则易被对抗性攻击恢复。

Method: PEAK 首先在扩散去噪网络的内部激活上训练 kSAE，将稠密表征分解为可解释的稀疏特征；通过对比目标与非目标提示引发的稀疏激活，依据激活强度与频率识别紧凑的目标特定特征集；随后进行参数优化，选择性抑制目标相关激活同时保留互补的非目标激活，将概念擦除直接嵌入扩散参数。

Result: 在 I2P 基准上，NudeNet 检测从 582 降至 6，平均攻击成功率 (ASR) 从 96.52% 降至 5.63%，并在 MS-COCO 上保持近零 KID 的生成质量。

Conclusion: PEAK 通过稀疏自编码器实现特征级定位与参数级嵌入，在概念擦除的精确性与持久性之间取得良好平衡，无需推理时干预即可抵御对抗性恢复。

Classification reason: 论文研究文本到图像扩散模型的概念擦除（concept erasure），属于生成模型的可控性与对齐范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）。

Importance reason: Solid concept-erasure method for diffusion models, but it is outside your configured priority directions and subtopics. Signals: concept erasure, k-Sparse Autoencoders, diffusion model safety, parameter-level editing, adversarial robustness

Abstract: Erasing concepts from large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models has become increasingly crucial due to the growing concerns over copyright infringement, privacy violations, and offensive content. Existing approaches struggle to achieve both precise and persistent concept erasure: inaccurate localization of concept-related representations may cause unintended semantic interference, while incomplete removal of the underlying concept knowledge allows adversarial recovery. To address this dilemma, we propose PEAK, a \textbf{\textit{precise}} and \textbf{\textit{persistent}} concept erasure framework via k-Sparse Autoencoders (kSAEs). PEAK first trains a kSAE on internal activations of the diffusion denoising network to decompose dense representations into interpretable sparse features. By contrasting sparse activations induced by target and non-target prompts, PEAK identifies a compact set of target-specific features according to both activation strength and frequency. These localized features are then used for parameter optimization, where PEAK selectively suppresses target-related activations while preserving complementary non-target ones towards the original model. This feature-guided optimization embeds concept erasure directly into diffusion parameters, eliminating the need for additional inference-time intervention and facilitating effective persistence against adversarial attacks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PEAK achieves effective and robust concept erasure. On the I2P benchmark, PEAK reduces NudeNet detections from 582 to 6, lowers the average attack success rate (ASR) from 96.52\% to 5.63\%, and preserves general generation quality on MS-COCO with a near-zero KID. Our code and models are available at:

</details>


### [42] [AdvFD: Boosting Visual Generation via Adversarial Fr'echet Distance Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11205)
*Mingju Gao, Jingkai Zhou, Kun Gai, Changqian Yu, Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: AdvFD 提出对抗式 Fréchet 距离损失，通过可学习的对抗表征补充静态特征空间，缓解 Fréchet hacking 问题，提升一步生成器后训练的图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet 距离作为分布级目标用于生成器后训练时，直接优化会导致 Fréchet hacking：目标指标持续提升但视觉质量和其他特征空间的 Fréchet 对齐停滞或恶化。作者归因于现有 Fréchet 损失使用的静态预训练特征空间提供了不完整且固定的真实/生成分布差异视角。

Method: AdvFD 在 FD-Loss 的静态表征目标基础上，补充一个经过校准的对抗学习表征。该可学习表征通过对抗方式最大化真实与生成样本之间的 Fréchet 差异，而生成器在得到的自适应特征空间中最小化同一差异。为防止对抗表征通过特征放大平凡地增大目标，引入真实特征白化（real-feature whitening）来归一化其尺度和协方差几何，稳定 min-max 优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，AdvFD 在 JiT 和 pMF 两种骨干网络以及不同模型规模上，一致地提升了一步生成器后训练的性能。

Conclusion: 通过对抗学习表征补充静态特征空间，AdvFD 有效缓解了 Fréchet hacking，为生成模型后训练提供了更稳健的分布级目标。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AdvFD 损失，通过对抗学习表征来优化 Fréchet 距离目标，用于生成模型的后训练（post-training），属于生成模型的训练目标/损失函数设计与对齐优化范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 generation_alignment（生成模型对齐与奖励）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid post-training method for one-step generators, but tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal and autoregressive generation priorities. Signals: adversarial frechet distance, frechet hacking, one-step generator post-training, flow matching, diffusion

Abstract: Fréchet distance has recently emerged as an effective distribution-level objective for generator post-training, complementing the conventional sample-level diffusion and flow-matching losses. However, directly optimizing Fréchet objectives can cause Fréchet hacking. The target metrics keep improving, but visual quality and Fréchet alignment in other feature spaces may stagnate or deteriorate. We attribute this failure to the static pretrained feature spaces used by existing Fréchet losses. These feature spaces provide incomplete and fixed views of the differences between real and generated distributions. To address this limitation, we propose Adversarial Fréchet Distance (AdvFD), which complements the static representation targets in FD-Loss with a calibrated adversarially learned representation. AdvFD augments the original static Fréchet objective with a learnable representation that adversarially maximizes the Fréchet discrepancy between real and generated samples, while the generator minimizes the same discrepancy in the resulting adaptive feature space. To prevent the adversarial representation from trivially increasing the objective through feature amplification, we further introduce real-feature whitening, which normalizes its scale and covariance geometry and stabilizes the min--max optimization. Extensive experiments show that AdvFD consistently improves one-step generator post-training across both JiT and pMF backbones and across different model scales.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents $\pi$-SUB, a physics-informed framework for generating synthetic underwater benchmark datasets that bridges the synthetic-to-real gap for Underwater Image Enhancement (UIE). The proposed framework extends the classical underwater image formation model by incorporating depth-dependent downwelling irradiance, biologically resolved absorption, and environmental scattering across all ten Jerlov water types, together with independently controllable residual phenomena. Using this framework, the $\pi$-SUB dataset consists of paired synthetic underwater-reference images spanning shallow-to-deep and coastal-to-oceanic environments. Extensive simulation studies have been carried out to evaluate $\pi$-SUB along two criteria namely hyper-realism and generalizability. For hyper-realism, $\pi$-SUB attains a global Frechet Inception Distance (FID) that is 46% lower than Syrea. For generalizability, four state-of-the-art UIE architectures (FUnIE-GAN, Pix2Pix, PUIE-Net, and Phaseformer) are used for comparative evaluation of $\pi$-SUB. These models were independently trained on six datasets including one real and five synthetic datasets and tested on six real-world benchmarks datasets. Across four UIE architectures and six real benchmark datasets, $\pi$-SUB improves UIQM by 4.18% over PHISWID (next best) and 9.46% over Syrea (next best), while reducing NIQE by 48.78% and 23.98%, respectively. These results establish $\pi$-SUB as a hyper-realistic and generalizable benchmark for developing the next generation of underwater image enhancement methods. The code and dataset are available at

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Abstract: Controllable generation guided by external knowledge is a key requirement in modern generative deep learning applications, enabling the synthesis of samples with explicit constraints on semantic content, structural properties, and variability. In 3D Computed Tomography (CT), such control is essential for clinical applications, including data augmentation, privacy-preserving data sharing, and the simulation of specific anatomical or pathological scenarios. While research on conditional 3D CT generation has expanded rapidly, the diversity of existing approaches makes systematic comparison difficult and obscures fundamental design choices. In this survey, we propose a conditioning-centric taxonomy that organizes the literature along three orthogonal dimensions: the type of external knowledge (K), the knowledge integration paradigm (I), and the generative architecture (A). This factorization defines an explicit design space (K x I x A) that provides a unified perspective on prior work. Using this framework, we systematize existing methods, identify dominant trends and recurring design patterns, and highlight underexplored regions of the design space that point toward promising directions for future research.

</details>

*Jiahui Han, Yuhui Yao, Xin Wang, Jiafei Cao, Mingxuan Zhang, Danfeng Shan, Huiqi Deng, Guanchu Wang, Xia Hu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型在机器人操控任务中表现强大，但其对抗鲁棒性研究不足，物理世界中的攻击可能造成实际危害。现有攻击依赖像素空间扰动或白盒访问，产生明显伪影且难以在真实机器人系统中部署。

Method: 提出 DURA（Diffusion-based Unrestricted Robotic Attack），沿预训练扩散模型的潜空间轨迹优化，生成视觉自然的对抗补丁。支持白盒与黑盒两种攻击设置，黑盒设置仅需受害者模型预测的动作，通过优化将机器人引导至攻击者指定的目标动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实物理世界中的大量实验表明，DURA 在攻击成功率上持续优于现有方法，同时生成的补丁视觉上更自然。

Importance reason: Solid adversarial-robustness work for VLA models, but it is security-focused and outside your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model priorities. Signals: diffusion-based attack, vision-language-action model, white-box and black-box, physical-world robotics, adversarial patch

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong capabilities in controlling robots across diverse manipulation tasks. However, their adversarial robustness remains largely underexplored, and exploiting this weakness can lead to physical-world harm. Existing attacks on VLA models often rely on pixel-space perturbations or white-box access, resulting in noticeable artifacts and limited deployability in real-world robotic systems. In this work, we propose DURA, a diffusion-based unrestricted robotic attack that generates visually natural adversarial patches for VLA models. DURA supports both white-box and black-box attack settings, where the black-box setting requires only the predicted actions of the victim model. By optimizing along the latent trajectory of a pretrained diffusion model, DURA generates visually natural patches while steering the robot toward attacker-specified target actions. Extensive experiments in both simulation and the real physical world show that DURA consistently outperforms existing methods. Our findings expose a safety risk for physically deployed VLA models and call for stronger defenses.

</details>

TL;DR: TAF-MED 是一个由医生审阅的 500 个三轮医疗对话基准，用于评测 LLM 在用户声明自我治疗意图后，多轮对话中医药安全边界是否持续。对 8 个 LLM 的 4000 段对话评测发现 71.6% 含不安全回复，61.4% 首轮安全后后续崩溃，模型级崩溃率 24.4%-96.2%，自动评测与医生标注一致性达 94.3%（κ=0.895）。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未隔离 LLM 在用户明确表达自我治疗意图后，多轮追问中医药安全边界是否持续保持。首轮安全不能代表完整对话轨迹中的安全持久性，需要专门的医疗多轮安全评测基准。

Method: 构建 TAF-MED 基准，包含 500 个医生审阅的固定三轮场景，评测 8 个 LLM 共 4000 段对话。采用基于 rubric 的自动评判器将回复标注为 SAFE/LEAKY/UNSAFE，并由两位医生独立标注 400 段对话作为参考，评估不安全指导、首轮安全后崩溃率及模型排名稳定性。

Result: 71.6% 的对话包含不安全回复；61.4% 以严格安全首轮开始的对话后续崩溃为不安全；模型级崩溃率从 24.4% 到 96.2% 不等；28 对模型中有 4 对在首轮不安全率与崩溃率之间排名反转；自动标注与医生参考一致性达 94.3%（κ=0.895）。

Conclusion: 首轮安全只是对话安全持久性的不完整代理指标，评测应覆盖完整对话轨迹。TAF-MED 将开源以支持多轮医疗安全的可复现研究。

Signals: medical safety benchmark, multi-turn refusal collapse, LLM safety evaluation, out-of-scope domain, no continuous/multimodal focus

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly provide conversational health information that may influence treatment decisions, yet existing benchmarks do not isolate whether medication-safety boundaries persist across follow-ups after explicit self-treatment intent. We introduce TAF-MED, a physician-reviewed benchmark of 500 fixed three-turn scenarios, and evaluate eight LLMs across 4,000 conversations. A rubric-based automated judge labelled responses as SAFE, LEAKY, or UNSAFE, and two physicians independently annotated a model-balanced random subset of 400 conversations. We assessed unsafe guidance, collapse after a strictly SAFE initial response, and model-ranking stability. Overall, 71.6% of conversations contained an UNSAFE response, and 61.4% of those beginning with a strictly SAFE response later collapsed to UNSAFE; model-level collapse rates ranged from 24.4% to 96.2%. Four of 28 model pairs reversed order between initial unsafe and collapse rates. Automated labels achieved 94.3% agreement with the adjudicated physician reference ($\kappa = 0.895$). These findings show that first-turn safety is an incomplete proxy for conversational safety persistence and motivate evaluation across complete dialogue trajectories. We will release TAF-MED on Hugging Face to support reproducible research on multi-turn medical safety.

</details>


### [48] [SafeCap: Improving LVLM Safety with Image Captioning Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10513)
*Caoyuan Ma, Wenpu Liu, Weichu Xie, Tian Gu, Shilei Zhao, Lingxi Min, Shuai Dong, Yuqi Xu, Ji Zhao, Ziyue Wang, Wenzheng Chang, Taiqiang Wu, Yongfu Zhu, Wenqi Shao, Yinqiang Zheng*

TL;DR: SafeCap 提出一种基于自描述图像标题的强化学习框架，通过让策略模型先生成安全相关的图像描述再作答，并用冻结 LLM 判断该描述是否导向安全决策来优化，从而提升 LVLM 对越狱攻击的防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）仍易受利用视觉输入绕过语言骨干安全对齐的越狱攻击。现有方法多依赖直接拒绝监督，未能充分暴露与安全响应相关的视觉线索。

Method: SafeCap 训练一个策略模型，先生成安全相关的图像标题，再生成最终答案；该标题通过是否能使冻结 LLM 达到安全对齐决策来优化。这种标题中介的目标函数鼓励策略暴露与安全响应生成相关的视觉线索，而非仅依赖直接拒绝监督。

Result: 在五个多模态安全基准和六个视觉效用基准上，SafeCap 在 DirectCap 协议下显著提升整体安全性能，在四种模型设置下安全平均分提升 3.7-19.0 分，同时保持相当或更好的视觉效用。在匹配骨干和数据上的受控对比中，SafeCap 优于安全 SFT、DPO 和 SafeGRPO。

Conclusion: 标题中介的强化学习对多模态安全对齐是有效的，通过自描述图像标题暴露安全相关视觉线索，能比直接拒绝监督和现有对齐方法更有效地提升 LVLM 的越狱防御能力。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦 LVLM 安全对齐，通过自描述图像标题的强化学习来防御越狱攻击，属于多模态安全与攻防方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack。

Importance reason: Safety alignment via caption RL is useful but peripheral to your core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: LVLM safety, jailbreak defense, caption-mediated RL, safety alignment, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that exploit visual inputs to bypass safety alignment inherited from their language backbones. We propose SafeCap, a reinforcement-learning framework that aligns LVLMs through learned self-captioning. SafeCap trains a policy model to first generate a safety-relevant image caption and then produce a final answer; the caption is further optimized by whether it enables a frozen LLM to reach a safety-aligned decision. This caption-mediated objective encourages the policy to expose visual cues relevant to safe response generation rather than relying solely on direct refusal supervision. Across five multimodal safety benchmarks and six vision-utility benchmarks, SafeCap substantially improves aggregate safety performance under its intended DirectCap protocol, with gains of 3.7-19.0 points in safety average across four model settings while maintaining comparable or improved vision utility. Under controlled comparisons on matched backbones and data, SafeCap outperforms safety SFT, DPO, and SafeGRPO, demonstrating the effectiveness of caption-mediated reinforcement learning for multimodal safety alignment.

</details>

TL;DR: NullEdit 是一种针对 VLM+DiT 图像编辑器的隐蔽防御方法，通过重定向参考图像与指令联合形成的 VLM 表征，在保持输出自然且保留源内容的同时抑制恶意编辑，实现"无害空操作"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: NullEdit 针对参考图像与指令联合形成的 VLM 表征进行重定向，该表征在下游 DiT 骨干条件化之前被干预。利用正常编辑锚点与无编辑锚点，NullEdit 将表征重定向到无编辑方向；同时通过跨提示梯度平均将保护迁移到未见过的恶意指令上。

Result: 在 Step1X-Edit 和 Qwen-Image-Edit 上，基于 CelebA-HQ 和 VGGFace2 数据集，NullEdit 相比 SOTA 基线平均将 EditReward IF 分数降低 0.813，同时保持主体身份与源内容。

Conclusion: NullEdit 提供了一种隐蔽、无害且可迁移的图像保护方案，在抑制恶意编辑的同时保持输出自然与源内容一致，为公开图像的推理时防御提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 论文提出 NullEdit，一种针对 VLM+DiT 图像编辑器的推理时防御方法，通过重定向 VLM 表征来抑制恶意编辑指令，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM+DiT image editing defense, which is tangential to the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models and autoregressive/diffusion video generation. Signals: VLM condition redirection, inference-time defense, image editing security, diffusion transformer, no-op protection

Abstract: Modern image editors combine vision-language models (VLMs) with diffusion transformer backbones to modify a single reference image according to instructions without fine-tuning. This capability also enables unauthorized manipulation of publicly released images. Existing inference-time defenses either invalidate edits through conspicuous corruption, thereby exposing the protection, or allow them to proceed with identity or reference content drift, thereby failing to prevent the editing behavior itself. We instead target a stealthy and harmless no-op in which the requested edit is suppressed, the output remains natural and source-preserving without conspicuous artifacts or identity replacement, and harmful semantics requested by malicious instructions are absent. We propose NullEdit, which targets the VLM representation jointly formed from the reference image and instruction before it conditions the downstream DiT backbone. Using normal-edit and no-edit anchors, NullEdit redirects this representation, while cross-prompt gradient averaging transfers protection to held out instructions. Across Step1X-Edit and Qwen-Image-Edit on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2, NullEdit reduces the EditReward IF score by 0.813 on average relative to the SOTA baseline while preserving subject identity and source content.

</details>


### [50] [Once Poisoned, Arbitrarily Controlled: A Programmable Backdoor in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10959)
*Tao Lin, Gaojie Jin, Zongxin Liu, Peng Wu, Lijia Yu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CR

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可编程后门攻击方法，通过单次投毒阶段在 VLM 中植入可编程后门，使攻击者能在推理时动态选择任意未见过的目标字幕语义并合成对应隐蔽触发器，实现 any-to-any 字幕控制，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 后门攻击通常被视为静态漏洞：one-to-one 或 N-to-N 攻击在受害者训练前将触发器绑定到有限的目标集合上，这严重低估了威胁。作者旨在证明单次投毒即可植入可编程后门，允许攻击者在推理时动态选择任意目标语义。

Method: 方法包含两个组件：(1) 启发式投毒策略，向模型暴露多样化的触发器-字幕对，促使模型学习通用的"触发器即指令"规则而非记忆特定后门模式；(2) 特征空间触发器隐写方法，将任意攻击者指定的目标字幕映射为隐蔽视觉触发器，实现为范数受控扰动或非语义补丁。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在 any-to-any 字幕控制上取得高成功率，保持干净模型效用，并在多种经典后门防御下依然有效。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 VLM 后门攻击的更大威胁面：攻击者可在推理时动态选择目标语义并合成触发器，无需重新训练，突破了传统静态后门假设，对 VLM 安全防御提出了新的挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 VLM 后门攻击（backdoor attack），属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack（多模态安全与攻防）。

Importance reason: Novel programmable backdoor attack on VLMs, but falls outside the user's core research directions and priority subtopics. Signals: VLM security, backdoor attack, any-to-any caption control, trigger steganography, no retraining

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) backdoors are usually treated as static vulnerabilities: one-to-one and N-to-N attacks bind one or more triggers to a finite set of targets before victim training. This assumption substantially underestimates the threat. We show that a single poisoning phase can implant a programmable backdoor into a VLM, allowing an attacker to choose previously unseen target-caption semantics at inference time and synthesize corresponding stealthy triggers on demand. Unlike fixed-mapping attacks, the proposed any-to-any caption-control paradigm decouples post-training target selection from poisoning, enabling dynamic control of target captions without retraining the VLM. Our method has two components. First, a heuristic poisoning strategy exposes the model to diverse trigger-caption pairs, encouraging it to learn a general trigger-as-instruction rule rather than memorize a specific backdoor pattern. Second, a feature-space trigger steganography method maps any attacker-specified target caption to a stealthy visual trigger, implemented as either a norm-controlled perturbation or a non-semantic patch. Once inserted into arbitrary images, these triggers cause the poisoned VLM to generate outputs semantically aligned with the chosen target caption, even when the target was unseen during poisoning. Extensive experiments show that our attack achieves high any-to-any caption-control success rates, preserves clean model utility, and remains effective under several classical backdoor defenses.

</details>

*Huafeng Chen, Yueming Lyu, Ziyuan Chen, Wenda Tan, Chenyang Si, Liucheng Guo, Caifeng Shan*

TL;DR: PRMU 是一个面向人物中心知识遗忘的语料无关（corpus-free）多模态评测基准，评估 MLLM 在真实删除场景下移除目标人物知识同时保留相关知识与通用能力的效果，并提出 SGPE 轻量级无语料遗忘基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 机器遗忘方法通常假设可访问原始遗忘与保留语料，但在真实删除场景中这些语料往往不可得。同时，人物相关知识在 MLLM 中自然习得，删除时需在遗忘目标知识与保留相关知识和通用能力之间取得平衡，现有方法常面临遗忘-局部性权衡困境。

Method: 提出 PRMU 基准，聚焦自然习得的人物相关知识，通过多样化的文本与视觉探针（包括对抗性评测与细粒度局部性分析）评估模型能否移除目标知识同时保留相关知识。并提出 SGPE（Similarity-Gated Projection Editing），一种轻量级无语料遗忘基线，包含知识位移、受保护参数空间编辑与局部性感知多模态控制。

Result: 在代表性 MLLM 上的实验表明，现有遗忘方法在激进遗忘设置下常出现显著的局部性退化，且易受多模态知识再激活攻击；SGPE 在目标遗忘、局部性保持与通用多模态效用之间提供了有竞争力的权衡。

Conclusion: PRMU 为真实且可扩展的多模态机器遗忘研究提供了评测基础，揭示了现有方法在遗忘-局部性权衡与知识再激活方面的不足，并验证了 SGPE 作为无语料遗忘基线的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文研究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）中的人物知识遗忘（machine unlearning），属于多模态安全与可靠性范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态安全与攻防 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multimodal safety/unlearning, which is outside the user's core directions and priority subtopics. Signals: multimodal safety, knowledge unlearning, benchmark, MLLM, corpus-free

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in storing and recalling rich person-related knowledge, raising increasing concerns about reliable knowledge removal. However, existing machine unlearning approaches for MLLMs typically assume access to original forget and retain corpora, which are often unavailable in realistic deletion scenarios. To address this limitation, we introduce PRMU, a benchmark for evaluating corpus-free multimodal unlearning under realistic person-centric deletion requests. PRMU focuses on naturally acquired person-related knowledge and evaluates whether models can remove target knowledge while preserving related knowledge through diverse textual and visual probes, including adversarial evaluation and fine-grained locality analysis. To facilitate research in this setting, we further introduce Similarity-Gated Projection Editing (SGPE), a lightweight corpus-free unlearning baseline with knowledge displacement, protected parameter-space editing, and locality-aware multimodal control. Extensive experiments on representative MLLMs reveal that existing unlearning methods often suffer from unfavorable forgetting-locality trade-offs, with significant locality degradation under aggressive forgetting settings, and remain vulnerable to multimodal knowledge reactivation. Meanwhile, SGPE provides a competitive trade-off between target forgetting, locality preservation, and general multimodal utility. We hope PRMU can facilitate future research toward realistic and scalable multimodal machine unlearning. Code and dataset will be released at .

</details>


### [52] [VidForensics-M1: Meta-Detection Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Temporal Grounding for AI-Generated Video Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11201)
*Bowei Liu, Zheng Lu, Yuhan Bian, Xinchen Zhang, Xingming Shui, Yuesheng Huang, Xuhuan Li, Zihao Liu, Yifan Yang, Jun Zhou, Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (78.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VidForensics-M1 首次将元检测（meta-detection）引入 AI 生成视频取证，通过可验证的时间定位证据与证据引导的奖励再分配强化学习，实现鲁棒且可泛化的 AI 生成视频检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 检测器依赖监督微调或标签级强化学习，粗粒度监督限制了其对未见场景和新兴视频生成器的泛化能力。文本 rationale 依赖外部模型易受幻觉和语义偏差影响，需要更客观可验证的证据信号。

Method: 提出元检测范式，联合优化预测标签与支持证据。构建自动化数据管线，通过边界帧条件视频生成模型替换时间片段生成成对真假视频。引入证据引导的奖励再分配（Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution），根据证据质量在标签正确的响应间进行证据感知的信用分配。

Result: 大量实验表明 VidForensics-M1 能有效利用可验证的时间证据，实现鲁棒且可泛化的 AI 生成视频检测。

Conclusion: 可验证的时间定位证据比文本 rationale 更适合作为 AI 生成视频检测的监督信号，证据引导的奖励再分配能在保持标签监督的同时提升检测器的细粒度伪造定位能力。

Classification reason: 该论文研究 AI 生成视频的取证检测（forensics），属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_safety_attack（多模态安全与攻防）高度相关，直接复用该子主题。

Importance reason: Solid but niche forensics work; tangential to your core focus on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: video forensics, meta-detection, temporal grounding, reinforcement learning, MLLM-based detection

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation models have significantly improved the realism of synthetic videos, blurring the boundary between generated and authentic content and raising concerns about misinformation. Existing MLLM-based detectors mainly rely on supervised fine-tuning or label-level reinforcement learning, where coarse supervision limits generalization to unseen scenarios and emerging video generators. To overcome these limitations, we are the first to introduce \textbf{meta-detection} into AI-generated video detection, enabling reliable forgery detection by jointly optimizing predicted labels and supporting evidence within reinforcement learning. This paradigm requires reliable evidence signals and effective mechanisms to integrate them into label-level optimization. Textual rationales provide semantic descriptions of forgery artifacts, but their generation and verification depend on external models, making supervision vulnerable to hallucinations and semantic biases. In contrast, temporal grounding provides more objective and verifiable evidence, as manipulated intervals can be precisely controlled during forgery construction. Based on this insight, we propose an automated data construction pipeline that generates paired real-fake videos by replacing temporal segments with boundary-frame-conditioned video generation models. Furthermore, we introduce \textbf{Evidence-Guided Reward Redistribution}, which performs evidence-aware credit assignment by redistributing rewards among label-correct responses according to evidence quality. This preserves reliable label supervision while encouraging detectors to acquire fine-grained and verifiable forgery localization capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that \textbf{VidForensics-M1} effectively leverages verifiable temporal evidence to achieve robust and generalizable AI-generated video detection.

</details>

### [53] [Actionable Hallucination Detection: Translating Latent Uncertainty into Agentic Critique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10430)
*Sanidhya Vijayvargiya, Rahul Lokesh*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 高优先级 (82.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Latent Critic，一种轻量级 LoRA 适配器，与冻结基座 LLM 并行运行，通过重构 transformer 残差流将潜空间中的不确定性信号放大并转化为局部化的自然语言反馈，实现无需二次推理的实时幻觉检测与智能体自我纠正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 作为智能体部署时常出现用户规范接地失败，执行幻觉化的、非期望的动作而非表达不确定性。现有检测方法要么无法定位幻觉，要么推理延迟过高，无法提供可操作的实时纠正。

Method: Latent Critic 是一个轻量级低秩适配器（LoRA），与冻结基座 LLM 的生成过程并行运行，主动重构 transformer 的残差流，放大潜空间中的接地信号并将其转化为单序列内的局部化自然语言反馈。通过激活修补和逐层探针进行机制分析，验证其秩不变行为将预存在的不确定性几何结构重构为线性可分离的表征。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 Llama 系列模型上，Latent Critic 在隔离幻觉方面显著优于同等规模的微调外部检测器、语义熵基线和被动内部探针，达到 0.966 AUROC 和超过 80% 的定位准确率（如 ungrounded: date）。在闭环 ReAct 环境中作为低延迟护栏，能在执行前拦截幻觉并利用局部化反馈实现高效自我纠正。

Conclusion: 通过操纵潜空间重构不确定性几何结构，可以在不引入二次推理开销的情况下实现可靠、细粒度的幻觉检测，为智能体提供实时、可操作的纠正能力。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦 LLM 智能体的幻觉检测与纠正，通过潜空间操作将不确定性信号转化为可操作的批评反馈，属于幻觉缓解与可靠性提升范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态幻觉与可靠性子主题。

Importance reason: Solid hallucination-detection method with mechanistic analysis, but it targets LLM agent reliability rather than the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: hallucination detection, latent uncertainty, LoRA adapter, agent self-correction, residual stream probing

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed as AI agents frequently exhibit user specification-grounding failures, executing hallucinated, undesired actions to force a resolution rather than expressing uncertainty. Existing detection methods fail to provide actionable, real-time correction as they either do not localize the hallucinations, or incur prohibitive inference latency. We introduce the Latent Critic, a lightweight low-rank adapter (LoRA) that operates concurrently with a frozen base LLM's generation to actively restructure the transformer's residual stream---amplifying latent grounding signals and translating them into localized, natural language feedback within a single sequence. By refining the base model's native uncertainty signals, this manipulation of the latent space enables reliable, granular detection without the overhead of secondary inference loops. Mechanistic analysis via activation patching and layer-wise probing shows that this rank-invariant behavior restructures pre-existing uncertainty geometry into a linearly separable representation that transfers more reliably than base model representations alone. Using tool-calling as an instantiation of granular hallucinations, we validate the detection and downstream improvements enabled by the Latent Critic architecture across Qwen and Llama-based models. Demonstrating superior real-time efficacy, our approach significantly outperforms equivalent-scale fine-tuned external detectors, semantic entropy baselines, and passive internal probes in isolating hallucinations, achieving 0.966 AUROC and >80% accuracy in localization (e.g., ungrounded: date). When deployed in a closed-loop ReAct environment, the Critic acts as a negligible latency guardrail, intercepting hallucinations before execution to prevent undesired actions while simultaneously leveraging this specific localized feedback to enable efficient agent self-correction.

</details>


### [54] [UniProbe: A Learnable Token-Level Hallucination Detector for Large VLMs using Multi-Structural Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10835)
*Dvir Samuel, Guy Bar-Shalom, Fabrizio Frasca, Ethan Fetaya, Yftah Ziser, Gal Chechik, Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 值得关注 (78.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UniProbe 是一个轻量级、可学习的 token 级幻觉检测器，通过构建冻结 LVLM 单次前向传播的异构计算轨迹图（图像 patch、查询 token、生成 token 及其注意力关系），并用 GNN、ViT、GRU 交替处理空间、关系与序列证据，实现 SOTA 的 token 级与物体级幻觉检测，并在解码时减少高达 55% 的物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型常产生与视觉输入不符的幻觉内容，有效缓解需要 token 级定位以便针对性干预。现有检测器要么需要昂贵的全模型微调，要么依赖忽略生成过程的外部验证器，要么将内部信号简化为孤立特征和手工统计，丢弃了空间、序列和关系结构。

Method: UniProbe 从冻结 LVLM 的单次前向传播中构建一个有向图，节点为图像 patch、查询 token 和生成 token，边由注意力权重编码其关系。检测器交替使用结构感知模块处理该计算轨迹：GNN 捕获关系证据、ViT 处理 2D 视觉几何、GRU 建模响应顺序，使空间、关系和序列证据在检测过程中交互。此外还提出流式变体用于幻觉感知解码（检测并重采样幻觉 token），以及自适应策略使检测器与 LVLM 自身生成对齐。

Result: 在多种 LVLM 骨干上，UniProbe 在 token 级和物体级幻觉检测上达到 SOTA。在解码过程中，它以标准生成 1.06 倍的延迟将物体幻觉减少高达 55%。

Conclusion: UniProbe 证明通过统一建模 LVLM 内部计算轨迹的多结构信息（关系、空间、序列），可以在不微调模型的情况下实现高效且准确的 token 级幻觉检测，并可直接用于幻觉感知解码以显著降低幻觉。

Classification reason: 论文提出 UniProbe，一种用于大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的 token 级幻觉检测器，属于多模态幻觉缓解与可靠性提升方向，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_hallucination 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Solid technical work on LVLM hallucination detection, but it falls outside your configured priority directions and subtopics. Signals: hallucination detection, token-level, GNN+ViT+GRU, LVLM reliability, no autoregressive generation focus

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve impressive visual reasoning and dialogue capabilities, yet frequently hallucinate content unsupported by the visual input. Effective mitigation requires token-level localization, enabling targeted intervention without discarding the entire response. Existing detectors require expensive full-model fine-tuning, rely on external verifiers that ignore the model's generation process, or reduce internal signals to isolated features and hand-crafted statistics, discarding spatial, sequential, and relational structure. We introduce \textbf{UniProbe}, a lightweight, unified, learnable detector that models a frozen LVLM's heterogeneous computational trace from a single forward pass. UniProbe constructs a directed graph over image patches, query tokens, and generated tokens, with attention weights encoding their relations. It processes this trace with alternating structure-aware modules: a GNN for relational evidence, a ViT for 2-D visual geometry, and a GRU for response order. Interleaving them allows spatial, relational, and sequential evidence to interact throughout the detector. We further develop a streaming variant for hallucination-aware decoding, which detects and resamples hallucinated tokens during generation, and a self-adaptation strategy aligning the detector with the LVLM's own generations. Across diverse LVLM backbones, UniProbe achieves state-of-the-art token-level and object-hallucination detection. During decoding, it reduces object hallucinations by up to 55\% at $1.06\times$ the latency of standard generation.

</details>


### [55] [When Visual Signals Mislead: A Mechanistic Study of Attribute Hallucination in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11024)
*Yufei Zhang, Chenlu Zhan, Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态幻觉与可靠性

Importance: 普通相关 (61.95) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 VISOR 框架，通过空图像诊断（VSNR）将 VLM 预测分解为视觉 logit 信号与语言先验信号，发现视觉信号而非语言先验主导属性幻觉，并据此路由到校准、弃权或视觉适配等不同修复算子，在 Qwen、InternVL、LLaVA 上降低属性误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性幻觉（正确识别物体但错误描述其属性）在 VLM 中普遍存在，但机制层面理解不足。主流解释"语言先验主导"虽催生了先验抑制方法，却从未在属性层面被直接验证。作者希望从机制上诊断幻觉的真正来源，并据此设计更有效的修复方案。

Method: 提出 VISOR（Visual-Operational Remediation）统一框架，包含两部分：1) VSNR 诊断方法，通过空图像（null-image）对照将每个预测分解为视觉 logit 信号与语言先验信号；2) 路由式修复，根据诊断结果将查询分配到三种算子——校准（处理阈值放置错误）、弃权（处理训练无关的低信噪比）、针对性视觉适配（处理材料属性失败，先验抑制无法纠正）。在 10,791 个负样本上对三种 VLM 家族和三种属性类型进行验证。

Result: 在 Qwen、InternVL、LLaVA 三种 VLM 上，视觉信号强预测假阳性，而语言先验信号接近随机水平。VISOR 将属性幻觉分解为两类失败模式：颜色/状态属性的低裕度但方向正确的视觉信号，以及材料属性的低信噪比或错位视觉信号。VISOR 在不依赖先验主导假设的情况下降低了属性假阳性。

Conclusion: 属性幻觉的主要机制并非语言先验主导，而是视觉信号本身的质量问题（低裕度、低信噪比或错位）。因此先验抑制方法无法根本解决材料属性幻觉，需要针对不同失败模式采用差异化修复策略。VISOR 通过机制诊断驱动的路由修复，为 VLM 幻觉缓解提供了更精确的路径。

Classification reason: 论文研究 VLM 的属性幻觉（attribute hallucination）机制与缓解方法，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性方向，直接映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_hallucination。

Importance reason: Mechanistic study of VLM hallucination with a practical remediation framework, but only tangential to the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: VLM hallucination, mechanistic diagnosis, null-image probing, calibration/abstention, Qwen/InternVL/LLaVA

Abstract: Attribute hallucination---where vision-language models (VLMs) correctly identify an object but mischaracterize its properties---is prevalent yet mechanistically poorly understood. The dominant explanation, language-prior dominance, has motivated prior-suppression methods, but this explanation has not been directly tested at the attribute level. We present VISOR (Visual-Operational Remediation), a unified framework that couples null-image-based diagnosis with routed remediation. Its VSNR diagnostic decomposes each prediction into a visual logit signal and a language-prior signal. Across 10,791 negative-ground-truth samples from three VLM families and three attribute types, the visual signal strongly predicts false positives, whereas the language-prior signal is near chance. VISOR uses this diagnosis to separate two failure modes: low-margin but directionally correct visual signals in color/state attributes, and low-SNR or misaligned visual signals in material attributes. The same diagnosis routes each query to the appropriate operator: calibration for threshold-placement errors, abstention for training-free low-SNR handling, or targeted visual adaptation for material failures that prior suppression cannot correct. Across Qwen, InternVL, and LLaVA, VISOR reduces attribute false positives without relying on the prior-dominance assumption.

</details>

### [56] [Procedural Fairness Failures in RLHF from Preference Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10126)
*M P V S Gopinadh, Karthik Kamuju, Kummari Avinash, John Joshua, Srinivasa Raju Rudraraju*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI, cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出标准 RLHF 通过偏好平均聚合异构偏好，导致多数偏好群体主导奖励学习而少数偏好被系统性低估，构成程序公平性失败。作者提出 Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF)，在奖励学习阶段按偏好模式分离优化，在受控设置下将整体对齐准确率从 46.9% 提升至 67.9%，并将最佳与最差对齐群体间的公平差距从 15.9 个百分点降至 9.6 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RLHF 假设偏好同质，将异构的人类偏好聚合为单一奖励模型。当偏好异构时，这种聚合会导致程序公平性失败：多数偏好群体主导奖励学习，少数偏好被系统性低估。作者希望定义并解决对齐中的程序公平性问题。

Method: 作者首先将程序公平性定义为在奖励建模过程中保留不同的偏好信号。然后提出 Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF)，在奖励学习阶段将优化按偏好模式分离，避免通过偏好平均抹平少数群体的偏好信号，从而在受控环境中验证公平性失败的存在并加以缓解。

Result: 在受控、无噪声的设置下，PA-RLHF 将整体对齐准确率从 46.9% 提升至 67.9%，并将最佳与最差对齐群体之间的公平差距从 15.9 个百分点降至 9.6 个百分点。

Conclusion: 对齐中的程序公平性失败可能源于奖励学习的结构性设计选择，即使在受控、无噪声环境中也会出现。这对大语言模型和智能体系统有直接影响，因为偏置的奖励模型会在顺序决策中放大不平等。

Classification reason: 论文研究 RLHF 中偏好聚合导致的程序公平性失败，并提出 Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) 方法，属于强化学习与人类反馈对齐（RLHF/偏好学习）范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题"多模态强化学习与对齐"。

Importance reason: RLHF fairness is a useful alignment topic, but it is far outside your configured continuous-multimodal, video-generation, and world-model priorities. Signals: RLHF fairness, preference averaging, alignment, reward modeling, cs.LG/cs.AI/cs.CL

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) aggregates heterogeneous preferences into a single reward model, assuming preference homogeneity. When preferences are heterogeneous, this aggregation induces a procedural fairness failure where majority preference groups dominate reward learning while minority preferences are systematically under-represented. This work defines procedural fairness in alignment as preserving distinct preference signals during reward modeling and shows that standard RLHF violates this via preference averaging. Preference-Aware RLHF (PA-RLHF) is introduced, separating optimization across preference modes at the reward learning stage. In a controlled setting, PA-RLHF improves overall alignment accuracy from 46.9% to 67.9% and reduces the fairness gap between best and worst aligned groups from 15.9 to 9.6 percentage points. These results show that procedural fairness failures in alignment can arise from structural design choices in reward learning, even in controlled, noise-free settings, with direct implications for large language models and agentic systems, where biased reward models can compound inequities across sequential decisions.

</details>

*Yi Wei, Shuo Jiang, Huaixia Dou, Jie Zhu, Junhui Li, Lifan Guo, Feng Chen, Chi Zhang*

TL;DR: 提出双循环自进化框架，通过可验证情感奖励驱动多轮共情对话策略优化。内循环用连续情感奖励优化策略，外循环基于策略相对交互效用自适应调整训练经验分布，在 SAGE 上将 Qwen3-8B Overall 从 53.87 提升到 79.24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共情对话本质上是多轮且路径依赖的：用户逐步披露问题、情绪随时间演变、早期回应影响信任与接受度。现有方法在进化对话策略时保持训练交互分布固定，导致策略能力与训练经验不匹配，限制了长程交互中的共情能力提升。

Method: 提出双循环自进化框架：用户模拟器与验证器冻结，内循环使用连续情感奖励优化多轮策略；外循环利用相同结果估计策略相对交互效用并自适应调整经验分布。框架在每组内保持场景与交互状态恒定，优先选择组通过率接近策略能力边界的条件；分层控制器跨支持意图共享证据，不确定性引导的探索与均匀排练防止过早排除。

Result: 在 SAGE 基准上，框架将 Qwen3-8B Overall 从 53.87 提升至 79.24，并比协议匹配的均匀情感奖励强化学习高出 7.23 分。

Conclusion: 通过内外双循环协同，在固定 rollout 预算下同时进化策略与训练经验分布，显著提升多轮共情对话能力，验证了可验证情感奖励作为长程交互监督信号的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出基于可验证情感奖励的强化学习自进化框架，用于多轮共情对话策略优化，属于强化学习与对齐方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Focuses on empathetic dialogue RL, outside your core continuous multimodal/world-model/video-generation priorities. Signals: empathetic dialogue, RL with verifiable rewards, self-evolution, multi-turn policy, no continuous token/video/world-model overlap

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated conversational capabilities, yet empathetic competence remains challenging. Empathetic support is inherently multi-turn and path-dependent: users disclose concerns gradually, emotions evolve over time, and early responses shape trust and receptivity. Reinforcement learning with verifiable emotion rewards provides scalable supervision for long-horizon interactions. However, existing methods evolve the dialogue policy while keeping its training interaction distribution fixed, creating a mismatch between policy competence and training experience. We introduce a dual-loop self-evolution framework driven by verifiable emotion feedback. With the user simulator and verifier frozen, the inner loop optimizes the multi-turn policy using continuous emotion rewards, while the outer loop uses the same outcomes to estimate policy-relative interaction utility and adapt experience. To obtain estimates from sparse, stochastic rollouts, the framework holds the scenario and interaction state constant within each group and prioritizes conditions whose group pass rates lie near the policy's competence boundary. A hierarchical controller shares evidence across support intents, while uncertainty-guided exploration and uniform rehearsal prevent premature exclusion. The resulting distribution generates trajectories, closing both loops without increasing the rollout budget. On SAGE, our framework raises Qwen3-8B Overall from 53.87 to 79.24 and outperforms protocol-matched uniform emotion-reward reinforcement learning by 7.23 points.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: CARE 采用双阶段流程：第一阶段通过可扩展的 Medical-CoT 合成方法生成结构化冷启动数据用于监督微调（SFT）；第二阶段使用 GRPO 强化学习，并引入新颖的置信度感知奖励（CAR）机制，将模型置信度与诊断正确性绑定到奖励信号中，联合优化准确性与校准。

Result: 在三个医学 VQA 基准上，CARE 取得最高诊断准确率，同时获得最低的期望校准误差（ECE）与最低幻觉率。

Conclusion: CARE 通过将置信度校准纳入强化学习奖励信号，有效缓解了医学 MLLM 的置信度失校准问题，为可信赖的临床决策支持奠定了基础。

Classification reason: 论文提出 CARE 框架，使用 GRPO 强化学习与置信度感知奖励（CAR）来优化医学 MLLM 的推理准确性与校准，属于多模态强化学习与对齐方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_rl_alignment。

Importance reason: Medical VQA with confidence calibration is outside your core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models, and the medical domain is explicitly deprioritized. Signals: medical VQA, confidence calibration, GRPO, CoT reasoning, negative keyword: medical

Abstract: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning (RFT) has enabled medical Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to produce Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning for visual question answering, yet these models suffer from $\textit{confidence miscalibration}$---a systematic gap between expressed certainty and actual diagnostic accuracy that undermines clinical trust. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$, a $\textbf{C}$onfidence-$\textbf{A}$ware medical $\textbf{RE}$asoning framework that jointly optimizes accuracy and calibration through a dual-stage pipeline. First, a scalable Medical-CoT synthesis provides structured cold-start data for Supervised Fine-Tuning. Second, Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel $\textbf{Confidence-Aware Reward (CAR)}$ mechanism ties the model's confidence to diagnostic correctness within the reward signal. Across three Medical VQA benchmarks, $\textbf{CARE}$ achieves the highest diagnostic accuracy while obtaining the lowest Expected Calibration Error and Hallucination Rate, establishing a foundation for trustworthy clinical decision support. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [59] [Test-Time Self-Evolving GUI Visual Grounding via Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11191)
*Shiyu Xuan, Zechao Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI 视觉定位模型部署后参数冻结，难以适应未见界面；虽有测试时强化学习方法，但无法对失败的探索进行反思，限制了模型的自适应能力。

Method: 构建探索-评估-反思-内化的闭环：代理先预测定位坐标进行探索；引入 MLLM 反思器评估生成结果并提供推理反思；通过 Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation 将高层反思转化为密集 token 级监督（条件自教师）；设计 Contrastive Calibration 防止错误自回归前缀破坏监督信号。

Result: 在六个基准上验证有效性，相比基础模型平均准确率提升 7.4%，是首个在 GUI 视觉定位中成功利用 on-policy 自蒸馏进行测试时自适应的工作。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了部署后自适应的空白，完善了 GUI 代理的自进化能力，展示了无需人工标注即可在测试时持续改进模型的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文提出测试时自进化框架，通过 MLLM Reflector 评估探索结果并利用 On-Policy Self-Distillation 将反思知识内化到模型权重，属于多模态模型在测试时通过强化/自蒸馏进行自适应与对齐的范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态强化学习与对齐。

Importance reason: Solid test-time adaptation method for GUI grounding, but outside your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model priorities. Signals: test-time adaptation, self-distillation, GUI visual grounding, MLLM reflection, on-policy learning

Abstract: GUI Visual Grounding is a fundamental capability for GUI agents. Existing models typically freeze their parameters after deployment, limiting their ability to adapt to unseen interfaces. Although recent methods attempt to adapt models via test-time reinforcement learning, they cannot reflect upon failed exploration. To overcome this, we propose a Test-Time Self-Evolving framework that enables models to improve after deployment without human-annotated ground truth. It constructs a closed-loop of Exploration, Evaluation, Reflection, and Internalization. Specifically, the agent first explores unseen interfaces by predicting grounding coordinates for given instructions. To evaluate these explorations, we introduce an MLLM-based Reflector to assess the generated results and provide the corresponding reasoning reflections. To internalize reflection knowledge into the model weights, we propose Reflection-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, which translates high-level reasoning into dense token-level supervision via a conditioned self-teacher. Furthermore, we design a Contrastive Calibration method to prevent incorrect auto-regressive prefixes from corrupting the supervisory signals during failed explorations. Extensive experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate our framework's effectiveness, achieving an average accuracy improvement of 7.4% over the base model. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to successfully exploit on-policy self-distillation for test-time adaptation in GUI visual grounding. By filling the gap in post-deployment adaptation, our framework completes the self-evolving capability of GUI agents. The code will be released.

</details>
