### [60] [Where To Look? : Causal Tracing of Vision Encoders in VLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10758)
*Naren Kumar S, Tirth Bhatt, Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (55.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文通过因果追踪方法研究视觉语言模型（VLM）中哪些视觉 token 真正驱动模型回答，发现高因果性的视觉 token 往往位于目标区域之外，且该模式在更大模型和不同扰动设置下保持一致，表明强多模态性能并不必然意味着空间局部化的因果表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 能准确描述图像，但"什么视觉信息真正驱动了模型的回答"这一根本问题尚未得到解答。作者希望通过因果追踪揭示视觉信息在 VLM 中的流动与利用方式。

Method: 采用因果追踪（causal tracing）方法，对 VLM 中的视觉 token 进行因果性分析，考察高因果视觉 token 的空间分布；并在更大规模的 VLM 及不同扰动（corruption）设置下验证该模式；同时设计实验移除外观线索，检验模型是否仍能保留视觉结构。

Result: 发现高因果性的视觉 token 常位于目标区域之外；该模式在更大模型和不同扰动设置下保持一致；移除外观线索后视觉结构仍被利用，说明视觉线索被用于理解视觉结构。

Conclusion: 实验揭示了"看见、使用与推理视觉结构"之间的差距，并提供了一个因果框架来研究现代 VLM 中视觉信息如何被转换、保留和最终使用。

Classification reason: 论文通过因果追踪（causal tracing）研究 VLM 中视觉信息如何驱动回答，属于视觉推理与可解释性分析，映射到多模态推理与思维链这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Causal tracing of VLM vision tokens is methodologically interesting but only tangentially touches the user's core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: causal tracing, vision-language model interpretability, multimodal understanding, not generation-focused, no continuous token or world model link

Abstract: Vision-language models can describe an image with remarkable accuracy, yet a more fundamental question remains unanswered: what visual information actually drives their answers? In this work, we investigate this question through causal tracing, and we observe that highly causal vision tokens often lie outside the target region. Extending the analysis to larger vision-language models reveals a similar pattern across models and corruption settings, suggesting that strong multimodal performance does not necessarily imply spatially localized causal representations. We further investigate: can these models preserve visual structure when appearance cues are removed? and find that visual cues are exploited to understand visual structures. Together, our experiments expose a gap between seeing, using, and reasoning over visual structure, and provide a causal framework for studying how visual information is transformed, preserved, and ultimately used by modern vision-language models.

</details>


### [61] [FADE: From Passive Verification to Active Discovery in Counterfactual Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10764)
*Fufangchen Zhao, Jinhu Fu, Jiachen Lei, Jiahong Wu, Xiangxiang Chu, Danfeng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (58.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FADE 提出一个反事实视频理解训练框架，采用"证据优先"的两阶段范式：先通过证据内化的监督微调将模型预测锚定在关键视觉异常上，再用渐隐锚点强化学习逐步移除文本引导，迫使模型独立发现并解释证据。同时提出将 MCQ 数据集转换为对齐 MCQ、开放式问答和描述任务的流水线。在 DualityVidQA-test 和 IPV-Bench 上取得 SOTA，性能保持率远超 GPT-5.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实视频理解的多选题基准通过问题和候选选项无意中泄露了目标事件，将核心挑战从主动发现降级为文本引导的验证，无法真正评估模型对物理与常识规律的理解能力。

Method: 提出 FADE 框架，包含两阶段训练：(1) 证据内化的监督微调（evidence-internalized SFT），将模型预测锚定在决定性视觉异常上；(2) 渐隐锚点强化学习（fading-anchor RL），逐步移除文本引导，迫使模型独立发现并解释证据。同时提出将现有 MCQ 数据集转换为对齐 MCQ、开放式问答（OQA）和描述（captioning）任务的流水线。

Result: 以 Qwen3-VL-8B 为基线，FADE 在 DualityVidQA-test 和 IPV-Bench 三个任务上均取得 SOTA 严格配对分数，超越 GPT-5.6。从受限 MCQ 转向无约束 OQA 和描述任务时，性能保持率在 DualityVidQA-test 上达 90.4% 和 67.4%，远高于 GPT-5.6 的 48.1% 和 30.7%。

Conclusion: FADE 证明"证据优先"的两阶段训练范式能有效提升模型在无约束反事实视频理解中的主动发现与解释能力，可作为该领域未来研究的可靠基线。

Classification reason: 该论文关注反事实视频理解（counterfactual video understanding），评估模型对物理与常识规律的理解，属于多模态视觉推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）。

Importance reason: Focuses on counterfactual video reasoning with RL training, but lacks direct alignment with the user's priority on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: counterfactual video understanding, evidence-first training, fading-anchor RL, MCQ to OQA conversion, Qwen3-VL-8B

Abstract: Counterfactual video understanding evaluates whether models grasp physical and commonsense regularities. However, existing multiple-choice question (MCQ) benchmarks inadvertently leak target events through their questions and candidate options. This reduces the core challenge from active discovery to text-guided verification. In this paper, we present FADE, an effective training framework for counterfactual discovery and explanation. Our method is built on an evidence-first, two-stage training paradigm. First, evidence-internalized supervised fine-tuning grounds the model's predictions in decisive visual anomalies. Second, we apply a fading-anchor reinforcement learning strategy that progressively removes textual guidance, compelling the model to independently discover and explain evidence. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we also introduce an effective pipeline that converts existing MCQ datasets into aligned MCQ, open-ended question answering (OQA), and captioning tasks without requiring additional data curation. Our simple approach yields strong results. Using Qwen3-VL-8B as the baseline, FADE achieves state-of-the-art strict paired scores across all three tasks on DualityVidQA-test and IPV-Bench, outperforming GPT-5.6. In specific, when transitioning from constrained MCQs to unconstrained OQA and captioning, our model demonstrates remarkable robustness. Its performance retention is 90.4% and 67.4% on DualityVidQA-test-substantially higher than the 48.1% and 30.7% retained by GPT-5.6. We hope this simple framework can serve as a solid baseline for future research in unconstrained counterfactual video understanding.

</details>


### [62] [Rationale-Guided Learning for Multimodal Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10448)
*Sujung Oh, Jung Uk Kim, Sangmin Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (72.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出RGL框架，将对话中的多模态情感识别从直接输入输出映射转化为认知启发的推理任务，基于双过程理论分解为直觉、情境与整合三个推理层面，利用MLLM离线生成结构化rationale作为记忆引导训练，推理时无需MLLM开销，在IEMOCAP和MELD上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话多模态情感识别方法大多将任务视为直接的输入输出映射（多模态线索→情感标签），忽略了人类解读情感时使用的因果推理过程。作者希望引入认知启发的推理机制来提升情感识别的准确性与可解释性。

Method: 基于双过程理论，将情感推理分解为三个层面：直觉（System 1即时感知）、情境（System 2情境分析）与整合（两者综合）。利用MLLM离线生成结构化rationale，将其编码为记忆，通过对齐内部表征与类人推理模式来引导模型训练。最终模型在推理时不依赖任何MLLM开销。

Result: 在IEMOCAP和MELD基准上取得SOTA性能。进一步通过解释性实验证明，模型内部特征能有效检索到语义正确的rationale，验证了其推理能力。

Conclusion: RGL通过将多模态情感识别重构为认知推理任务并利用rationale引导训练，在提升性能的同时增强了模型的可解释性，且推理阶段无额外MLLM开销，验证了认知启发的推理范式在多模态情感理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文将多模态情感识别转化为基于双过程理论的认知推理任务，利用MLLM生成结构化rationale引导模型训练，属于多模态推理范畴，映射到[CANONICAL]多模态推理与思维链子主题。

Importance reason: 多模态情感识别与用户关注方向（连续多模态、视频/图像生成、世界模型）无直接交集，且未涉及生成或推理效率提升。 Signals: 多模态情感识别, 双过程理论, MLLM离线生成rationale, 推理时无额外开销, IEMOCAP/MELD SOTA

Abstract: Multimodal emotion recognition in conversation (MERC) requires understanding complex interactions between verbal and non-verbal cues. However, most existing approaches fundamentally treat this as a direct input-output (multimodal cues-emotion labels) mapping problem, overlooking the causal reasoning that humans use when interpreting emotions. We propose rationale-guided learning (RGL), a novel framework that transforms MERC into a cognitively-inspired reasoning task. Based on dual-process theory, we decompose emotional reasoning into three facets: Intuitive (immediate perception, System 1), Contextual (situational analysis, System 2), and Integrative (synthesis of both). We leverage an MLLM offline to generate structured rationales, which are encoded as memories to guide model training via aligning internal representations with human-like reasoning patterns. Our final model operates without any MLLM overheads at inference time. Experimental results show that RGL achieves state-of-the-art performance on the IEMOCAP and MELD benchmarks. Further, for interpretation, we demonstrate that the model's internal features effectively retrieve semantically correct rationales for unseen test samples, validating its rationale reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [63] [GeoForge: Non-Parametric Self-Evolving Agents for Earth-Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10494)
*Xin Xiao, Jiang Zhong, Junnan Zhu, Yingchao Feng, Peijin Wang, Yidan Zhang, Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MA

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoForge 是一个无需训练的、自进化的地球观测推理智能体框架，将完成的工作流轨迹转化为结构化的非参数执行状态，通过三种互补记忆（工作流图记忆、动作级经验、技能SOP）检索任务条件先验来引导工具执行，并在任务后通过安全门控蒸馏将轨迹转化为可复用知识，从而在不更新骨干LLM的情况下提升规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测（EO）智能体需要构建科学有效的工具工作流并基于当前地理空间证据得出结论，但EO工作流受传感语义、产品依赖、时空兼容性和参数要求等多重约束。现有智能体常为每个查询搜索广阔的操作空间，而近期自进化系统未能将异构EO轨迹组织成跨决策层级的可复用知识。

Method: GeoForge 采用训练无关的自进化框架：根据传感上下文约束操作空间，从三种互补记忆中检索任务条件先验——工作流图记忆（Workflow Graph Memory）捕获全局操作顺序、动作级经验（Action-Level Experiences）提供局部修正、技能SOP（Adapted Skill Standard Operating Procedure）保留程序与数据约束。检索到的先验引导工具执行，最终答案仍以当前观测为基础。任务完成后，通过安全门控蒸馏过程将接地轨迹转化为可复用的执行知识供未来检索。

Result: 在多个地理空间基准上的实验表明，GeoForge 在多种LLM骨干上一致提升了任务准确率和工具使用轨迹质量，同时大幅减少了大多数LLM的工具规划与推理错误。

Conclusion: GeoForge 通过执行-蒸馏-复用循环，在不更新骨干LLM的情况下有效提升地球观测推理的规划能力，验证了将异构轨迹组织为结构化非参数知识对自进化智能体的价值。

Classification reason: 论文提出 GeoForge，一个面向地球观测（EO）推理的自进化智能体框架，通过工作流图记忆、动作级经验与技能SOP检索来引导工具执行与规划，属于多模态/地理空间推理与智能体规划范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）。

Importance reason: Focuses on Earth-observation agent workflows, which is outside the user's core continuous multimodal, video generation, and world model priorities. Signals: earth observation, agent framework, self-evolving, non-parametric memory, tool-use planning

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) agents construct scientifically valid tool workflows and ground their conclusions in current geospatial evidence. This is challenging because EO workflows are constrained by sensing semantics, product dependencies, spatial and temporal compatibility, and parameter requirements. Existing agents often search a broad operation space for each query, while recent self-evolving systems do not fully organize heterogeneous EO trajectories into reusable knowledge across different decision levels. To solve this problem, we present GeoForge, a training-free, self-evolving framework that transforms completed trajectories into a structured nonparametric execution state. GeoForge constrains the operation space according to the sensing context, then retrieves a task-conditioned prior from three complementary memories. Workflow Graph Memory captures global operation order, Action-Level Experiences provide local corrections, and the Adapted Skill Standard Operating Procedure preserves procedural and data constraints. The retrieved prior guides tool execution, while current observations remain the basis of the final answer. After each task, a safety-gated distillation process converts grounded trajectories into reusable execution knowledge for future retrieval. This execution, distillation, and reuse loop improves planning without updating the backbone LLM. Experiments on multiple geospatial benchmarks demonstrate that GeoForge consistently improves both task accuracy and tool-use trajectory quality across diverse LLM backbones, while substantially reducing tool-planning and reasoning errors for most LLMs.

</details>


### [64] [MIRA: Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10827)
*Shengzhi Wang, Jun Yang, Kai Wu, Xiaozhong Ji, Yiwen Ye, Ziyang Chen, Mingliang Xiong, Wen Fang, Mingqing Liu, Mengyuan Xu, Miaoxuan Shan, Caiyan Liu, Bin He, Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MIRA 是一个医学视觉诊断框架，通过工具增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索数据引擎和强化学习驱动的反思性原理演化，实现自主证据搜索与反思性验证，在九个医学视觉推理基准上平均得分 64.73，较 Qwen3-VL-8B 提升 7.44 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉智能体可以调用工具检查图像并检索外部知识，但无差别地使用工具可能引入噪声或误导性证据。可靠的诊断不仅需要获取额外观察，还需要验证工具操作是否必要以及所得证据是否支持当前假设。

Method: MIRA 采用两阶段训练策略：(1) 工具增强的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）数据引擎探索多样诊断假设，联合验证视觉定位准确性与语义一致性以构建 SFT 轨迹；(2) 强化学习通过在线反思性原理演化改进决策，将失败案例蒸馏为候选原理，仅保留能提升留出集 rollout 奖励的原理。

Result: 在九个医学视觉推理基准上平均得分 64.73，较 Qwen3-VL-8B 骨干提升 7.44 分；有用工具使用判断从 56.2% 提升至 73.8%，有害判断从 8.9% 降至 1.6%。定性分析显示 MIRA 能重新检查证据、纠正过早结论并调整工具使用策略。

Conclusion: MIRA 验证了在医学视觉诊断中，通过反思性验证与选择性工具使用可以显著提升诊断可靠性与推理质量，减少有害工具调用，为医学视觉智能体的自主推理提供了有效范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出医学视觉智能体 MIRA，通过工具调用与反思性验证进行自主诊断推理，属于多模态视觉推理与智能体决策范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态推理与思维链（multimodal_reasoning_cot）。

Importance reason: Medical agentic diagnosis with MCTS and RL is methodologically solid but falls outside your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: medical diagnosis (negative keyword), agentic tool use, MCTS data engine, RL principle evolution, multimodal understanding

Abstract: Medical visual agents can use tools to inspect images and retrieve external knowledge, but indiscriminate tool use may introduce noisy or misleading evidence. Reliable diagnosis therefore requires not only acquiring additional observations, but also verifying whether tool actions are necessary and whether the resulting evidence supports the current hypothesis. We introduce MIRA (Medical Image Reflection for Agentic Diagnosis), a medical visual diagnostic framework for autonomous evidence search and reflective verification. MIRA dynamically invokes image-processing operations, including zooming, grounding, pointing, rotation, and measurement, as well as web search, while evaluating the relevance and consistency of the acquired evidence. We develop MIRA through a two-stage training strategy. First, a tool-augmented Monte Carlo Tree Search data engine explores diverse diagnostic hypotheses and jointly verifies visual grounding accuracy and semantic consistency to construct supervised fine-tuning trajectories. Second, reinforcement learning further improves decision-making through online reflective principle evolution: failure cases are distilled into candidate principles, and only principles that improve held-out rollout rewards are retained. Across nine medical visual reasoning benchmarks, MIRA achieves an average score of 64.73, improving its Qwen3-VL-8B backbone by 7.44 points. It also increases useful tool-use judgments from 56.2% to 73.8% and reduces harmful judgments from 8.9% to 1.6%. Qualitative analyses show that MIRA can re-examine evidence, correct premature conclusions, and adapt its tool-use strategy. Project page:

</details>


### [65] [Evidence-Grounded Trustworthy Multimodal Reasoning and Evaluation Benchmark in Complex Urban Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10954)
*Zhaoyang Wei, Bowen Jiang, Xumeng Han, Jiashu Li, Xuehui Yu, Yuling Liu, Guorong Li, Zhenjun Han, Jianbin Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 值得关注 (76.88) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 论文提出 AD2-Bench 基准与 EGVOR 框架，针对复杂城市场景下 MLLM 推理可靠性下降的问题，通过分层视觉诊断（Chain of Evidence）揭示推理失败源于证据获取不足，并提出证据原子（Evidence Atoms）结构化空间-语义三元组来显式生成证据，配合分层课程强化学习训练，显著提升恶劣条件下的推理稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在良性场景表现优异，但在复杂城市场景的恶劣条件下认知可靠性显著下降，模型常依赖隐式推理而缺乏充分视觉证据，导致感知与推理脱节。同时，现有面向结果的评测基准只评估最终预测，无法诊断底层推理过程中的失败环节。

Method: 提出 AD2-Bench 基准，引入分层视觉诊断框架将推理分解为结构化 Chain of Evidence (CoE)。从概率视角将推理失败归因于两类：空间模糊性（无法区分目标与背景杂波导致定位错误）和语义不确定性（退化视觉特征导致理解错误）。在此基础上提出 Evidence-grounded Visual Reasoning (EGVOR)，用显式生成的 Evidence Atoms（结构化空间-语义三元组）替代隐式推理，并通过从反思性监督构建到强化学习的分层课程训练，显式奖励降低推理方差。

Result: 大量实验表明 EGVOR 在恶劣条件下显著提升了推理稳定性，相比基线模型在复杂城市场景中表现出更鲁棒的多模态认知能力，验证了证据获取对可靠推理的关键作用。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的多模态推理从根本上依赖于准确的证据获取。通过显式生成结构化证据原子并配合分层课程强化学习训练，可以有效缓解空间模糊性与语义不确定性导致的推理失败，为可信多模态认知提供更稳健的框架。

Classification reason: 论文提出 AD2-Bench 评测基准与 EGVOR 推理框架，核心是复杂城市场景下的多模态推理，通过 Chain of Evidence (CoE) 结构化推理链诊断推理失败，并训练模型进行证据驱动的视觉推理。这属于多模态推理与思维链方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Solid benchmark+framework for MLLM reasoning robustness, but it is not aligned with the user's core continuous-model, autoregressive generation, or world-model priorities. Signals: multimodal reasoning, chain of evidence, reinforcement learning, urban scenes, benchmark

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate impressive performance in benign scenarios, their cognitive reliability deteriorates significantly in complex scenes under adverse conditions. In these settings, models often rely on implicit inference without sufficient visual evidence, leading to a disconnect between perception and reasoning. Meanwhile, existing outcome-oriented benchmarks evaluate only final predictions and fail to diagnose failures in the underlying reasoning process. To address this gap, the authors propose AD2-Bench, which introduces a Hierarchical Visual Diagnosis framework that decomposes reasoning into a structured Chain of Evidence (CoE). This fine-grained diagnosis reveals that robust multimodal reasoning fundamentally depends on accurate evidence acquisition. Building on this perspective, the authors formulate reasoning from a probabilistic viewpoint and identify two primary causes of reasoning failure: Spatial Ambiguity, where models fail to distinguish target objects from background clutter, resulting in localization errors; and Semantic Uncertainty, where degraded visual features lead to incorrect semantic interpretation, resulting in understanding errors. To overcome these evidence deficiencies, they further propose Evidence-grounded Visual Reasoning (EGVOR), which replaces implicit reasoning with the explicit generation of Evidence Atoms - structured spatial-semantic triplets that enforce tight alignment between localization and semantic understanding. The model is trained through a hierarchical curriculum that progresses from reflective supervision construction to reinforcement learning, where reducing reasoning variance is explicitly rewarded. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EGVOR substantially improves reasoning stability under adverse conditions, providing a more robust framework for trustworthy multimodal cognition.

</details>


### [66] [VERDICT: Training-Free Step-Wise Verification of Multimodal Reasoning via Disagreement-Aware Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10665)
*Rohit Sinha, Kunal Tilaganji, Tanuja Ganu, Nagarajan Natarajan, Amit Sharma, Vineeth Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.GT, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态推理与思维链

Importance: 高优先级 (82.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VERDICT 提出一种无需训练的逐步验证方法，通过多个冻结验证器之间的分歧感知共识来验证多模态推理链，将分歧建模为协调博弈并给出闭式均衡解，实现分歧感知过滤与稳定性排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型常生成包含细微错误的推理链导致错误答案。现有验证方法要么需要昂贵的标注监督且跨任务表现不一致，要么通过简单聚合多个来源的分数，忽略了分数分歧本身携带的重要信息——分歧恰恰能揭示推理步骤是否真正有效。

Method: 将多验证器逐步验证形式化为耦合打分问题，可解释为协调博弈并具有唯一闭式均衡解：一致表示有效步骤，分歧揭示不稳定。VERDICT 通过闭式解计算共识分数，实现分歧感知过滤和稳定性感知的推理步骤排序，全程无需训练且领域无关。

Result: 在六个基准上，VERDICT 相比基础模型最高提升 +5.95%，并与需要大量监督的领域特定批评器表现相当，证明跨模态一致性能提供稳健的验证信号而无需任务特定适配。

Conclusion: 跨模态验证器之间的分歧结构可以被显式建模并用于推理验证，提供无需训练、领域无关的稳健验证信号，为多模态推理可靠性提供新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文关注多模态大语言模型推理链的逐步验证与纠错，属于多模态推理与思维链范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Solid training-free verification method for multimodal reasoning, but it targets reasoning verification rather than the user's core generation/world-model priorities. Signals: multimodal reasoning verification, training-free, disagreement-aware consensus, closed-form equilibrium, multimodal large language model

Abstract: Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Existing approaches either require expensive labelled supervision with inconsistent cross-task performance or aggregate scores from multiple sources by simple aggregations, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We formalise this as a coupled scoring problem among disparate, frozen verifiers, interpretable as a coordination game with a unique closed-form equilibrium where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Towards this end, we propose a training-free domain-agnostic step-wise verification approach we call VERDICT: VERification via Disagreement-Informed Coupled Thresholding. To our knowledge, VERDICT is the first training-free verifier that makes the structure of cross-modal disagreement explicit and actionable. It computes consensus scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, \method consistently improves over the base model by up to +5.95%, and performs competitively with domain-specific critics that demand extensive supervision, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation and Training-Free Verification

</details>

### [67] [DistilVDR: A Compact End-to-End Visual Document Retriever via Dual-Student Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10636)
*Zhuchenyang Liu, Ziyi Wang, Yao Zhang, Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 值得关注 (79.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DistilVDR 是一个 524M 参数的端到端视觉文档检索系统，通过双学生蒸馏从单个 8B 视觉语言教师模型中学习，在 ViDoRe 基准上达到教师模型 86.9% 的性能，同时将索引体积缩小 15.6 倍、索引速度提升一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索（VDR）目前被数十亿参数的大模型主导，这些模型在全文语料规模下索引速度慢且服务成本高。已有的压缩方案要么从头训练较小的多向量编码器，要么只蒸馏查询侧，均无法端到端地得到紧凑的单向量检索器。

Method: DistilVDR 采用非对称编码器-仅学生结构，从单个 8B 视觉语言教师模型进行双侧蒸馏，使用逐点余弦对齐损失。所有监督信号来自冻结教师模型的 embedding 空间（该空间本身经过相关性监督训练），因此学生目标无需相关性标签、负采样或对比项。视觉能力集中在文档侧，查询侧仅保留 70M 参数。发布两个变体，共享相同编码器与训练，仅在文档编码器的视觉 tile 预算上不同：DistilVDR-HiRes 与 DistilVDR-Fast。

Result: DistilVDR-HiRes 在 ViDoRe v1+v2+v3 上达到 61.74 平均 NDCG@5（为 8B 教师模型的 86.9%），并在高分辨率敏感的 v3 基准上领先所有复现的 sub-1B 基线；DistilVDR-Fast 在视觉 token 预算缩小 3 倍的情况下达到 59.98。两个变体将一百万文档存储在比最强 sub-1B 多向量基线小 15.6 倍的索引中，索引语料库速度快一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过双侧蒸馏和逐点余弦对齐，可以在不依赖相关性标签的情况下训练出紧凑的端到端单向量视觉文档检索器，在保持接近教师模型性能的同时大幅降低存储与索引成本，验证了从大规模 VLM 蒸馏高效检索器的可行性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 DistilVDR，一个端到端视觉文档检索器（VDR），通过双学生蒸馏从 8B 视觉语言教师模型压缩而来，属于跨模态/文档检索范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态检索（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid efficiency-focused retrieval distillation work, but it targets document retrieval rather than the user's core generative/continuous-model priorities. Signals: efficient inference, model compression, distillation, visual document retrieval, sub-1B model

Abstract: Visual document retrieval (VDR) is dominated by multi-billion-parameter models that are slow to index at full corpus scale and expensive to serve. Prior compression routes either train a smaller multi-vector encoder from scratch or distil only the query side; neither yields a compact single-vector retriever end-to-end. We present DistilVDR, a 524M end-to-end VDR system distilled bilaterally from a single 8B vision-language teacher under a pointwise cosine alignment loss. All supervision comes from the frozen teacher's embedding space, which was itself trained with relevance supervision, so the student objective needs no relevance labels, negative sampling, or contrastive term. We match VDR's text-query and image-document input asymmetry with an asymmetric encoder-only student that concentrates visual capacity on the document side and keeps the query side at 70M parameters. We release two variants that share the same encoders and training and differ only in the document encoder's visual-tile budget: DistilVDR-HiRes attains 61.74 average NDCG@5 on ViDoRe v1+v2+v3 (86.9% of the 8B teacher) and leads every reproduced sub-1B baseline on the high-resolution-sensitive v3 benchmark, while DistilVDR-Fast attains 59.98 at a 3 times smaller visual-token budget. Both variants store one million documents in a 15.6 times smaller index than the strongest sub-1B multi-vector baseline and index the corpus an order of magnitude faster. The code is available at .

</details>

### [68] [MMArt A Multi-Perspective Multimodal Dataset for Visual Art Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10706)
*Shuai Wang, Wangyuan Ding, Yixian Shen, Jia-Hong Huang, Stevan Rudinac, Monika Kackovic, Nachoem Wijnberg, Marcel Worring*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MMArt 是一个包含 74,234 幅 WikiArt 画作的大规模多视角艺术理解数据集，每幅画配有叙事、形式、情感、历史四个独立视角标注及统一标题，并通过互补性分析证明多视角设计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型对艺术的理解停留在表面内容描述，缺乏形式分析、历史解读和情感刻画。作者认为这不仅是模型局限，也是数据集局限——现有艺术数据集都是单一视角资源，没有数据集同时为同一批画作提供叙事、形式、情感和历史四个视角。

Method: 构建 MMArt 数据集，包含 74,234 幅 WikiArt 画作，每幅由专门的视觉语言模型或人工标注生成四个独立视角的标注及一个统一标题，并通过互补性质量评估验证。采用生成分析（形式分析描述最能保留构图风格）和判别式检索分析（叙事描述驱动检索 R@1=44.0%，形式描述检索仅 7.8%）以及留一法分析来验证多视角设计的必要性。

Result: 互补性分析表明各视角编码了真正不同的信息；生成分析显示形式分析描述最能保留构图风格，历史描述在重建图像中携带强情感信号；判别式检索分析揭示任务不对称性：叙事描述驱动检索（R@1=44.0%），而形式描述在检索尺度上几乎无判别力（R@1=7.8%）；留一法分析确认历史描述是两个任务中最不可替代的视角。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一视角能胜任所有任务，这直接论证了 MMArt 多视角设计的必要性，为艺术理解研究提供了更全面的数据基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MMArt 多视角艺术理解数据集，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）。

Importance reason: Dataset paper for art understanding with limited direct alignment to your continuous multimodal or video generation priorities. Signals: multimodal dataset, art understanding, multi-perspective annotation, vision-language benchmark, no continuous tokens

Abstract: Recent vision-language models demonstrate impressive general visual understanding, yet their art interpretation remains shallow: they describe surface content but struggle with formal analysis, grounded historical interpretation, or affective characterization. We argue this is not only a model but also a dataset limitation. Existing art datasets are single perspective resources, where no dataset provides narrative, formal, emotional, and historical perspectives simultaneously for the same artworks. We introduce MMArt, a large-scale dataset of 74,234 WikiArt paintings, each annotated with four independently annotated perspectives plus a harmonized unified caption, produced by specialized vision-language models or human annotation and validated through complementary quality evaluations. Two complementarity analyses establish that perspectives encode genuinely distinct information. A generative analysis shows that formal analysis descriptions best preserve compositional style, and historical descriptions carry strong affective signal in reconstructed images. A discriminative retrieval analysis reveals task-asymmetry: narrative descriptions drive retrieval (R@1 = 44.0%), while formal descriptions, strongest for reconstruction, are nearly nondiscriminative at retrieval scale (R@1 = 7.8%). Leave-one-out analysis further confirms that historical descriptions are the least replaceable perspective across both tasks. Together, the two analyses establish that no single perspective suffices for all tasks, directly motivating MMArt multi-perspective design. The dataset, code, and additional information are available at .

</details>


### [69] [More Accurate, Less Human: Gestalt Grouping in Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10195)
*Sudhanva Manjunath Athreya, Sai Phani Kumar Malladi*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一个行为评测套件，基于人类感知研究数据对45个视觉模型（监督、自监督、对比视觉语言编码器、开源VLM和闭源基础模型）在四种格式塔分组任务（标记颜色odd-one-out、颜色序列计数、剪影识别、物体odd-one-out）上进行评分，发现与人类响应的对齐程度能捕捉传统性能指标无法区分的感知组织方面，且部分闭源模型的人类对齐度远低于其基准准确率所暗示的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉通过格式塔操作（如颜色分组、形状补全）组织视觉内容，可视化设计正是建立在这些操作之上。然而视觉模型是否以这种方式组织视觉内容尚未被系统测试，现有性能指标无法反映模型与人类感知组织方式的一致性。

Method: 作者构建了一个行为评测套件（behavioral battery），基于先前感知研究的人类数据，对45个模型在四个分组任务（标记颜色odd-one-out、颜色系列计数、剪影识别、物体odd-one-out）上进行评分，覆盖监督、自监督、对比视觉语言编码器、开源VLM和闭源基础模型五个训练家族。

Result: 评测发现，与人类响应的对齐程度能捕捉传统性能指标无法区分的感知组织方面，多个闭源模型的人类对齐度显著低于其基准准确率所暗示的水平。

Conclusion: 基于已发表感知数据评分可为可视化研究提供可复用的评测标尺，无需新的用户研究即可审计进入可视化管线的模型是否以人类受众的方式组织视觉内容。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个行为评测套件（behavioral battery），用人类感知数据对45个视觉模型在四种分组任务上进行评分，属于视觉模型评测基准与评估协议研究，映射到多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Evaluates Gestalt grouping in vision models but lacks direct connection to the user's priority topics of continuous/multimodal generation, video/image autoregression, or world models. Signals: vision model evaluation, gestalt grouping, human alignment, VLM benchmarking, no generative focus

Abstract: Human vision organizes what it sees into wholes: same-colored points group into series, similar marks cohere into categories, and shapes complete into recognizable objects. These are the Gestalt operations that visualization design builds on. Whether vision models organize visual content this way has not been systematically tested. We introduce a behavioral battery that scores models against human data from prior perception studies on four grouping tasks: mark-color odd-one-out, color-series counting, silhouette recognition, and object odd-one-out. We apply it to 45 models across five training families: supervised, self-supervised, and contrastive vision-language encoders, open-weight VLMs, and closed foundation models. The battery reveals that agreement with human responses captures aspects of perceptual organization that conventional performance metrics fail to distinguish, with several closed models exhibiting substantially lower alignment than their benchmark accuracy would suggest. Scoring against published perception data therefore gives visualization research a reusable yardstick, requiring no new user study, for auditing whether the models now entering visualization pipelines organize what they see the way their human audience does.

</details>

*Han Zhang, Yilin Zhao, Zaid Pervaiz Bhat, Zheng Tang, Varun Praveen, Vidya N. Murali, David C. Anastasiu, Tomasz Kornuta*

TL;DR: 本文提出 TAR（交通异常推理）与 TAR-Bench 数据集，用于训练和评测视频语言模型在异常检测之外的推理能力。TAR 包含 44,040 条思维链训练标注、覆盖 10 个任务、来自 8 个公开数据集的 3,670 段 CCTV 视频；TAR-Bench 包含 960 条人工精选测试标注。实验表明强问答准确率不能可靠预测时序或场景推理能力，多任务微调带来一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通异常检测数据集仅关注"是否异常"的二元检测，缺乏对异常事件进行多任务推理（如时序、场景、原因等）的细粒度理解能力评测，且缺少相应的训练数据。

Method: 构建 TAR 训练数据集，使用 MAVEN 方法将多尺度视频证据整合为结构化事件描述，再生成问答对与推理轨迹；构建 TAR-Bench 评测集，包含 80 段人工精选视频片段与 960 条标注；对 11 个视觉语言模型进行零样本与多任务微调评测。

Result: 11 个视觉语言模型在 TAR-Bench 上显示强问答准确率不能可靠预测时序或场景推理能力；多任务微调带来一致提升，完整 10 任务模型相比零样本基线聚合分数提升 21.4 分。

Conclusion: TAR 与 TAR-Bench 为视频语言模型在交通异常推理上的训练与评测提供了官方资源，并作为 AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3 的官方数据，推动从异常检测走向异常理解。

Classification reason: 论文提出 TAR-Bench 评测基准与 TAR 训练数据集，用于视频语言模型的多任务交通异常推理评测，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: Focuses on traffic anomaly reasoning benchmarks, which is outside your core continuous multimodal and video generation priorities. Signals: video-language model reasoning, chain-of-thought annotations, multi-task fine-tuning, traffic anomaly detection, benchmark dataset

Abstract: We present TAR (Traffic Anomaly Reasoning) and TAR-Bench datasets, resources for training and evaluating video-language models beyond anomaly detection. TAR contains 44,040 chain-of-thought training annotations across 10 tasks for 3,670 CCTV videos ($\sim$26 hours) from eight public datasets. Its evaluation component, TAR-Bench, contains 960 human-curated test annotations for 80 held-out clips trimmed from 17 public YouTube videos. TAR's training annotations are produced with MAVEN, which consolidates multi-scale video evidence into structured event descriptions before generating question-answer pairs and reasoning traces. On TAR-Bench, eleven vision-language models reveal that strong question-answering accuracy does not reliably predict temporal or scene reasoning ability. Multi-task fine-tuning on TAR yields consistent gains, with the full 10-task model improving aggregate score by 21.4 points over its zero-shot baseline. TAR and TAR-Bench provide the official training and in-domain evaluation data for AI City Challenge 2026 Track 3. The dataset is available at

</details>


### [71] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin, Zechuan Chen, Yongsen Zheng, Yuxiang Zhang, Jingyuan Yang, Bin Wang, Yubin Wang, Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: UserToolBench 是一个面向工具使用 LLM 的个性化决策评测基准，测试模型能否从交互历史中推断潜在用户偏好、识别何时需要澄清，并在信息不完整的情况下生成与用户对齐的工具调用轨迹。基准包含 10 个用户画像、36 个工具集、1065 轮对话和 170 个工具，实验表明当前强工具使用 LLM 在个性化委托上仍有困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用 LLM 基准大多聚焦于画像回忆、风格模仿、通用工具使用或响应级个性化，缺乏对"模型能否为用户做出正确决策"这一核心能力的评测。作者希望将个性化评测从"输出听起来是否像用户"转向"模型是否为用户做出正确决策"。

Method: 构建 UserToolBench 基准：基于隐私脱敏的真实交互轨迹，结合结构化人物画像、公开 API 风格工具生态和长时程多轮轨迹。包含 10 个用户画像、36 个工具集、1065 轮、170 个唯一工具，评测任务类型覆盖信息缺失、单工具和多工具设置，测试模型推断潜在偏好、识别澄清需求及生成用户对齐的工具调用轨迹的能力。

Result: 对强工具使用 LLM 的实验表明，当前模型在个性化委托上仍有困难。多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长时程行为一致性是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 个性化评测应超越"输出是否听起来像用户"，转而关注 LLM 是否为其所代表的用户做出正确决策。当前模型在个性化工具委托方面仍存在显著不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 UserToolBench，一个用于评测工具使用 LLM 个性化决策的新基准，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on tool-use personalization benchmarks, which is outside the user's core interests in continuous multimodal models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: tool-use LLM benchmark, personalized decision making, user profile inference, multi-tool coordination, no continuous/multimodal generation focus

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [72] [VisEditBench: Can Vision-Language Models Edit Visualization Code from Multimodal Feedback?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10408)
*Mizanur Rahman, Arshia Azimlu, Shadikur Rahman, Md Tahmid Rahman Laskar, Amran Bhuiyan, Shafiq Joty, Enamul Hoque Prince*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: VisEditBench 是一个包含 1,395 个人工标注可视化代码编辑任务的多模态基准，涵盖反馈引导修复与参考引导重样式两种场景，评估 20 个 SOTA VLM 后发现可视化代码编辑仍具挑战性，并提出了基于渲染反馈的 VisEditAgent 编辑框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的可视化创作本质上是迭代式的，用户经常需要修改已有可视化来修复有缺陷的图表或适配目标风格。然而现有基准主要评估从零生成，可视化代码编辑这一重要场景尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建 VisEditBench 基准，包含 1,395 个人工标注的可视化代码编辑任务，覆盖反馈引导修复（基于有缺陷/标记图表与文本反馈修改代码）和参考引导重样式（修改代码以匹配目标图表图像）两种设置。同时提出 VisEditAgent，一个基于渲染反馈的编辑框架，迭代地生成、执行、验证和精炼候选编辑。

Result: 评估 20 个 SOTA VLM，Claude-4.6-Sonnet 达到最佳整体通过率 74.46%，多数开源模型低于 50%；在视觉接地样式适配上表现尤其弱（Claude-4.6-Sonnet 仅 55.71%）。基于 GPT-4o 的 VisEditAgent 将整体通过率从 55.75% 提升到 67.99%。

Conclusion: 可视化代码编辑对现有 VLM 仍具挑战性，尤其是视觉接地样式适配；基于渲染反馈的迭代编辑框架能显著提升编辑忠实度，验证了渲染接地反馈的重要性。

Classification reason: 论文提出 VisEditBench，一个包含 1,395 个可视化代码编辑任务的多模态评测基准，评估 VLM 从多模态反馈中编辑可视化代码的能力，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark。

Importance reason: A solid but niche benchmark for visualization code editing, which is outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: multimodal benchmark, visualization code editing, VLM evaluation, render-grounded feedback, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong capabilities in generating visualization code from textual or visual specifications. However, real-world visualization authoring is inherently iterative: users frequently revise existing visualizations to repair flawed charts or adapt them to desired styles. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate generation from scratch, leaving visualization code editing from multimodal feedback largely unexplored. We introduce VisEditBench, a benchmark of 1,395 human-annotated visualization code-editing tasks grounded in realistic visualization workflows and failure cases. VisEditBench covers two practical settings: feedback-guided repair, where models revise visualization code using buggy or marked charts together with textual feedback, and reference-guided restyling, where models modify code to match a target chart image. Evaluating 20 state-of-the-art VLMs reveals that visualization code editing remains challenging: Claude-4.6-Sonnet achieves the best overall pass rate of 74.46%, while most open-source models remain below 50%. Performance is particularly weak on visually grounded style adaptation, where Claude-4.6-Sonnet achieves only 55.71%. To establish a strong baseline, we further propose VisEditAgent, a render-grounded editing framework that iteratively generates, executes, validates, and refines candidate edits. Built on GPT-4o, VisEditAgent improves overall pass rate from 55.75% to 67.99%, demonstrating the importance of render-grounded feedback for faithful visualization editing. We will release VisEditBench at .

</details>


### [73] [Chartography: A Benchmark for Professional Chart Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10677)
*Suhaas Garre, Chris Mutty, Sushant Mehta, Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (56.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Chartography 是一个包含 100 个任务的图表理解基准，图表来自医学、工程、金融等专业实践中的领域特定格式，问题由专业从业者撰写并经三位专家独立验证。在 30 个前沿模型配置的评测中，最佳配置仅达 45.0% 的 pass@1，其余在 9.0-39.5% 之间，失败集中在视觉感知方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表基准主要由柱状图、折线图和饼图主导，推理链较短，且前沿模型已接近饱和（80-90% 得分），无法充分衡量专业从业者在实际工作中阅读复杂领域图表的能力。

Method: 构建包含 100 个任务的基准，任务配对来自专业实践中的领域特定格式图表与专业从业者撰写的问题，并由三位额外专家独立验证。对 30 个前沿模型配置进行评测，每个任务进行 20 次评分试验。

Result: 最佳模型配置仅达到 45.0% 的平均 pass@1，其余配置得分在 9.0-39.5% 之间。失败集中在视觉感知：模型会遗漏细微特征、误读稀疏标注轴上的数值、错误处理投影 3D 几何，并违反图表中编码的领域约定。

Conclusion: 专业图表理解仍是前沿模型尚未解决的重要挑战，现有模型在视觉感知层面存在系统性不足。作者公开了所有任务、图像、来源元数据和评测代码，以推动该方向的研究。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Chartography，一个面向专业图表理解的多模态评测基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: A solid benchmark paper, but it targets chart understanding, which is outside the user's core focus on continuous/multimodal generation and world models. Signals: benchmark, chart understanding, multimodal understanding, 30 models evaluated, perception failures

Abstract: Professionals across medicine, engineering, finance, manufacturing, and the sciences often make consequential decisions from charts. Existing chart benchmarks do not sufficiently measure this ability: they are dominated by bar, line, and pie formats, rely on shorter reasoning chains, and are nearing saturation, with frontier models already scoring 80-90%. We introduce Chartography, a benchmark of 100 tasks that pair charts drawn from professional practice, in domain-specific formats that standard chart benchmarks rarely include, with questions written by professionals who read these charts for a living and independently verified by three additional experts. In an evaluation of 30 frontier-model configurations (20 scored trials per task), the best configuration reaches only 45.0% mean pass@1; the remainder span 9.0-39.5%. Failures concentrate in visual perception: models can miss nuanced features, misread values along sparsely labeled axes, mishandle projected 3D geometry, and violate domain conventions encoded in the chart. We release all tasks, images, provenance metadata, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [74] [FaithformBench: Benchmarking Faithfulness of Mathematical Chain-of-Thought Autoformalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10916)
*Rob Cornish, Iacopo Ghinassi, Po-Hung Yeh, Shuqi Liu, Qiyuan Xu, Haoxuan Yin, Dominik Wagner, Wenda Li, Yee Whye Teh, Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.LO, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FaithformBench 是一个用于评测数学思维链自动形式化系统忠实度的新基准。它通过自动生成扰动（无效）推理步骤，同时测量未扰动步骤的有效性保持与扰动步骤的无效性保持，无需昂贵的人工标注。在八个 AF 系统、四个数学数据集上评测发现普遍存在"谄媚"现象：许多系统将无效输入"静默纠正"为可证明语句。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化（AF）忠实度评估方法依赖昂贵的人工标注真值，或依赖 LLM 裁判与嵌入模型，准确性保证有限；且通常只考虑已知正确的输入，无法评估 AF 对错误输入的忠实翻译。作者希望提出一种廉价、在弱假设下可靠、且同时评估正负样本的忠实度基准。

Method: 方法基于自动生成扰动推理步骤（设计为无效），然后测量未扰动步骤的有效性保持（validity preservation）与扰动步骤的无效性保持（invalidity preservation）。通过这两个指标综合评估 AF 系统的忠实度，无需人工标注。

Result: 在八个 AF 系统、四个数学数据集上评测，观察到普遍的谄媚现象：许多 AF 系统将无效输入"静默纠正"为可证明语句。最保持有效性的微调 AF 系统同时也是最谄媚的，表明当前 AF 系统在有效性保持与无效性保持之间存在张力。

Conclusion: 当前自动形式化系统在忠实度上存在系统性缺陷，尤其是"静默纠正"无效输入的问题。有效性保持与无效性保持之间的张力提示需要新的训练目标或评估协议来平衡两者，FaithformBench 为这一方向提供了廉价且可靠的评测工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FaithformBench，一个用于评测数学思维链自动形式化（autoformalisation）忠实度的新基准，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on math autoformalisation faithfulness, which is outside the user's core continuous multimodal, video/image generation, and world model priorities. Signals: benchmark for AF faithfulness, sycophancy in AF systems, no human annotation needed, math reasoning focus, cs.AI/cs.LO/cs.CL

Abstract: Autoformalisation (AF) systems map natural language reasoning steps into formal statements in a proof assistant such as Lean. We consider how to assess the faithfulness of these systems. Existing approaches require expensive human-annotated ground truth, or rely on LLM judges or embedding models, which come with limited guarantees of accuracy. In addition, these methods typically only consider inputs that are known to be correct, and therefore do not assess whether the AF translates incorrect inputs faithfully. To address these limitations, we propose a new benchmark for AF faithfulness that is cheap to apply, sound under weak assumptions, and assesses both positive and negative examples. Our method is based on automatically generating perturbed reasoning steps that are designed to be invalid, and then measuring validity preservation on unperturbed steps and invalidity preservation on perturbed steps. We apply our method to eight AF systems across four mathematical datasets, and observe pervasive sycophancy: many AFs "silently correct" invalid inputs into provable statements. The most validity-preserving fine-tuned AFs are also the most sycophantic, suggesting a tension between validity and invalidity preservation in current AF systems.

</details>


### [75] [E$^3$mo-Bench: A Scalable Benchmark for Multimodal Evoked and Expressed Emotion Understanding via Bayesian Pairwise Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10796)
*Lancheng Gao, Ziheng Jia, Shengyan Li, Zixuan Xing, Jiarui Wang, Huiyu Duan, Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: E³mo-Bench 是一个可扩展的多模态情感理解基准，包含 12,314 个问答对、2,524 个视频，通过情感感知、开放词汇识别和 VAD 评估三个互补任务评测 MLLM 的诱发情感与表达情感理解能力，并提出 Bayesian Pairwise Alignment 方法实现可扩展的连续标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感理解基准通常孤立地评测表达情感或诱发情感，且局限于粗粒度、不完整的情感刻画，无法全面评估 MLLM 在情感感知交互中的能力。

Method: 提出 E³mo-Bench 基准，包含 12,314 个问答对和 2,524 个视频，从预定义情感视角评测诱发与表达情感理解，涵盖情感感知、开放词汇识别和 VAD 评估三个任务。提出 Bayesian Pairwise Alignment 方法，将稀疏低负担的成对判断聚合为锚点参照的 VAD 估计，实现可扩展的连续标注。同时开发 E³mo-Score，一个免训练智能体，聚合五模型委员会的互补判断以改进 VAD 估计。

Result: 大量实验验证了框架的有效性，并揭示了 MLLM 在诱发情感与表达情感范式之间存在显著性能偏差，同时在细粒度识别和维度评估方面存在持续缺陷。

Conclusion: E³mo-Bench 为推进多模态情感智能提供了清晰的评测方向，揭示了当前 MLLM 在情感理解上的不足，为后续研究指明了改进路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 E³mo-Bench，一个用于多模态大模型（MLLM）情感理解评测的新基准，包含 12,314 个问答对和 2,524 个视频，评估诱发情感与表达情感理解。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_benchmark 子主题。

Importance reason: Emotion benchmark is outside your core directions and priority subtopics, with no strong methodological overlap. Signals: multimodal benchmark, emotion understanding, Bayesian pairwise alignment, MLLM evaluation, no autoregressive/diffusion/world-model focus

Abstract: Understanding both expressed and evoked emotions is critical for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to achieve comprehensive affect-aware interactions. However, existing benchmarks typically examine expressed and evoked emotions in isolation or are constrained to coarse-grained and incomplete affective characterizations. To bridge this gap, we introduce E$^3$mo-Bench, a scalable benchmark comprising $12{,}314$ question-answer pairs across $2{,}524$ videos with predefined affective perspectives. It evaluates evoked and expressed emotion understanding via $3$ complementary tasks: emotion perception, open-vocabulary recognition, and valence-arousal-dominance (VAD) assessment. To efficiently scale reliable continuous annotations, we propose Bayesian Pairwise Alignment, which aggregates sparse, low-burden pairwise judgments into anchor-referenced VAD estimates. Furthermore, we develop E$^3$mo-Score, a training-free agent that aggregates complementary judgments from a five-model committee to improve VAD estimation. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of our framework and expose a pronounced performance skew between evoked and expressed emotion paradigms. These findings, coupled with MLLMs' persistent deficits in fine-grained recognition and dimensional assessment, chart a clear course for advancing multimodal emotional intelligence.

</details>


### [76] [DashArena: Benchmarking LLMs on Interactive Analytic Dashboard Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10567)
*Xiaotong Wang, Dazhen Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DashArena 是首个面向开放式、任务驱动的交互式分析仪表盘生成的评测基准。它要求系统同时生成仪表盘和可回放的交互轨迹，通过浏览器执行器将分析工作流转化为可复现的视觉与执行证据，再由 VLM 裁判结合证据进行对比评分，并用 Bradley-Terry 聚合生成排行榜。作者还蒸馏出开源权重裁判 DashJudge-8B，实验表明交互证据能提升裁判与人类判断的一致性，并揭示前沿模型在渲染、分析与交互方面仍存在持续失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析仪表盘生成是开放式任务，静态外观或成功执行都无法充分反映分析支持与交互质量，现有评测难以评估其实际有用性。

Method: 提出 DashArena 基准，要求系统生成仪表盘与可回放交互轨迹；浏览器执行器回放轨迹生成可复现的视觉与执行证据；VLM 裁判基于证据对比候选，经 Bradley-Terry 聚合生成排行榜；并蒸馏出开源权重裁判 DashJudge-8B。

Result: 人类评估显示 DashJudge-8B 能有效复现人类判断；消融实验表明交互证据可提升裁判与人类判断的一致性；对前沿模型的实验揭示了持续的渲染、分析与交互失败。

Conclusion: 真实仪表盘生成仍具挑战性，交互感知的评测能捕捉静态或仅执行检查所遗漏的失败。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DashArena，一个用于评测 LLM 生成交互式分析仪表盘的基准，属于多模态/生成式评测基准范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for interactive dashboard generation, but far outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, and world models. Signals: benchmark, dashboard generation, VLM judge, interaction trajectory, cs.AI

Abstract: Analytic dashboards combine coordinated views and interactions for data exploration and decision-making. Recent models can generate them from data and natural-language goals, but evaluating their usefulness remains difficult. Dashboard generation is open-ended, and neither static appearance nor successful execution alone captures analytical support and interaction quality. We introduce DashArena, to our knowledge the first benchmark for open-ended, task-grounded generation of interactive analytic dashboards. Its key innovation is to require each system to generate both a dashboard and a replayable interaction trajectory. A browser executor replays the trajectory and turns the system's intended analytical workflow into reproducible visual and execution evidence. A VLM judge compares candidates using this evidence, and Bradley--Terry aggregation produces the leaderboard. We further distill the judge into the open-weight DashJudge-8B. Human evaluations show that DashJudge-8B effectively reproduces human judgments and ablations show that interaction evidence improves judge agreement. Experiments with frontier models reveal persistent rendering, analytical, and interaction failures. Together, these results show that realistic dashboard generation remains challenging and that interaction-aware evaluation captures failures missed by static or execution-only checks.

</details>


### [77] [Order Matters: LVLMs as Judges for Temporal Reasoning in Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10908)
*Martina Ianaro, Guilherme Fernandes, Maurizio Gabbrielli, Joao Magalhaes*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (58.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）作为图像序列时序推理评测器时的结构性缺陷：模型在孤立点式评分上表现尚可，但在成对时序顺序判别上出现灾难性崩溃。通过诊断探针发现系统性的位置不对称（首因与近因效应），模型对故事判断受帧位置影响大于语义一致性，根源可能在于因果掩码与旋转嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式多媒体从静态图像合成演进到复杂的交错视觉叙事，自动评测系统对时序连续性"失明"，难以区分连贯叙事与语义打乱或矛盾的序列。现有依赖 LVLMs 作为评测器的范式存在架构性偏见，作者希望揭示这一结构性缺陷。

Method: 通过一系列诊断探针实验，对比模型在孤立点式评分与成对时序顺序判别上的表现，揭示性能二分现象。通过系统性操纵帧位置，检测首因效应与近因效应等位置不对称，并分析其与因果掩码和旋转嵌入的潜在关联。

Result: 发现模型在点式评分上看似胜任，但在成对时序判别上性能灾难性崩溃；存在系统性的位置不对称（primacy 和 recency 效应），帧位置对判断的影响大于语义一致性。

Conclusion: 当前基于 transformer 的评测器本质上不适合长形式视觉推理，需要超越快照中心式指标，开创"时序感知评测"（Temporally-Aware Evaluation）范式，将视觉序列视为统一逻辑结构而非无序帧集合。

Classification reason: 论文研究 LVLMs 作为图像序列时序推理评测器的缺陷，属于多模态评测与评估协议范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）。

Importance reason: Exposes structural temporal-judgment flaws in LVLMs, but focuses on evaluation rather than the user's priority of continuous/multimodal generation or world models. Signals: LVLM judge bias, temporal reasoning, primacy/recency effect, multimodal evaluation, image sequence understanding

Abstract: As generative multimedia evolves from static image synthesis to complex, interleaved visual narratives, a foundational bottleneck has emerged: the judgment crisis. While human perception naturally synthesizes the temporal and logical flow of a story, automated evaluation systems remain largely "blind" to sequential continuity, often failing to distinguish between a coherent narrative and a semantically shuffled or contradictory sequence. This work identifies a critical structural gap in current multimodal evaluation paradigms, arguing that the reliance on Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) as judges is fundamentally limited by architectural biases. Our analysis reveals a profound performance dichotomy: while models may appear competent in isolated pointwise scoring, they suffer a catastrophic collapse when required to perform pairwise discrimination of temporal order. We demonstrate that this is not merely a data-scarcity issue but a structural one. Through a series of diagnostic probes, we uncover systematic positional asymmetries, specifically primacy and recency effects, where a model's judgment of a story is significantly influenced by the placement of a frame, often more than by its semantic consistency. These biases, potentially rooted in causal masking and rotary embeddings, suggest that current transformer-based judges are inherently ill-equipped for long-form visual reasoning. By exposing these blind spots, we challenge the multimedia community to move beyond snapshot-centric metrics and instead pioneer Temporally-Aware Evaluation paradigms that treat visual sequences as unified logical structures rather than unordered collections of frames.

</details>


### [78] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani, Abdelghani Chibani, Yacine Amirat, Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出一个从扁平单标签动作语料合成四层分层意图基准（动作、活动、低层意图、高层意图）的框架，通过转移模型在主体一致性约束下组装片段，并用覆盖感知采样器降低主体使用Gini系数。通过将序列生成规则与评估用一阶逻辑规则分离来规避循环监督风险，生成15,002个片段，并验证了基准的结构性难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语料仅提供孤立的原子级标注片段，缺乏时间组合；而录制好的复合活动语料层次浅、领域窄、层次固定。需要一个沿语义层次标注、覆盖从原子动作到长时意图的基准来评估分层人类行为识别。

Method: 提出基准生成与评测框架：从扁平单标签动作语料合成四层分层意图基准，保留动作层真实预提取特征；用转移模型在主体一致性约束下组装片段；覆盖感知采样器降低主体使用Gini系数（0.566→0.248）；通过将序列生成规则与评估用一阶逻辑规则分离来规避循环监督风险。

Result: 生成15,002个片段。四种参考基线在所有模型族上均出现0.13-0.17 macro-F1的组合式留出差距，包括识别最佳但未缩小差距的图感知模型，表明这是基准的结构性属性而非模型伪影。无逻辑基线仍违反留出语义规则，顺序破坏控制作为生成器一致性检查。

Conclusion: 该基准具有结构性难度，组合式留出差距是基准固有属性。框架通过规则分离规避循环监督风险，并发布本体、转移模型和生成器以便基准可再生成和扩展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一个用于分层人类动作识别的基准生成与评测框架，属于新基准构建与评测协议设计，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Benchmark synthesis for hierarchical action recognition is methodologically sound but far from the user's continuous multimodal and video generation focus. Signals: hierarchical action benchmark, benchmark synthesis, subject-consistency, coverage-aware sampling, first-order logic evaluation

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [79] [ENTLORE: A Graph-Grounded Benchmark for Latent Organizational Reasoning in Enterprise Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10679)
*Akrin Zheng, Alexander Wu, Alaia Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.IR, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (51.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ENTLORE 是一个图基企业问答基准，通过从日常文档、组织表和运营记录重建可审计的企业世界，构造显式查找、跨源组合和潜在组织推理三类共 907 个问题，评测 56 种模型与访问配置，揭示潜在组织推理仍是企业 QA 的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业问答基准往往物化预定义的答案路径，只测试对已陈述事实的组合，而非从语料中恢复缺失的目标关系（即潜在组织推理）。作者希望构建一个能真实评测模型恢复隐含组织关系能力的基准。

Method: 提出 ENTLORE 基准构建框架：从日常文档、权威组织表和运营记录重建可审计的企业世界，通过版本化组织约定在真值图中认证派生关系，生成完整黄金答案与证明证书；对齐匿名化发布仅暴露文档语料而隐藏私有结构与目标关系。包含 2,341 篇文档、907 个问题，覆盖显式查找、跨源组合和潜在组织推理三类。

Result: 在 56 种模型与访问配置上评测：将发布世界结构化为诱导实体图或可导航知识库可获得最强的可部署结果；但即使提供黄金文档，仍有 30.4% 的潜在问题无法回答，而显式和组合问题分别为 12.6% 和 6.2%。

Conclusion: 企业问答不仅依赖文档召回，还取决于隐含组织关系是否变得可用。潜在组织推理是当前模型的主要短板，需要新的方法使隐式关系可被利用。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ENTLORE，一个面向企业问答中潜在组织推理的图基基准，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Enterprise QA benchmark with latent graph reasoning is solid but far from the user's continuous multimodal and video generation focus. Signals: enterprise QA benchmark, latent organizational reasoning, graph-grounded, 907 questions, 56 models

Abstract: Enterprise question answering is framed as retrieving internal documents and generating grounded answers. Routine enterprise records, however, are work by-products in which required organizational relations remain implicit across heterogeneous sources. Existing benchmarks provide realistic multi-source evidence, but often materialize a predefined answer path and therefore test the composition of stated facts rather than recovery of a target relation absent from the corpus. We call the latter capability latent organizational reasoning. We introduce ENTLORE, a graph-grounded benchmark construction framework that reconstructs an audited enterprise world from routine documents, authoritative organizational tables, and operational records. Versioned organizational conventions certify derived relations in a truth graph, enabling complete golden answers and proof certificates. The aligned anonymized release exposes only the document corpus while withholding private structure and target relations. ENTLORE contains 2,341 documents from three source types and 907 questions spanning explicit lookup, cross-source composition, and latent organizational reasoning, evaluated across 56 model and access configurations. Structuring the released world as an induced entity graph or navigable knowledge base gives the strongest deployable results. Yet supplying gold documents still leaves 30.4% of latent questions unanswered, versus 12.6% and 6.2% for explicit and compositional questions. Enterprise QA therefore depends not only on document recall, but also on whether implicit organizational relations become usable. The benchmark, data, and code are publicly available at ENTLORE.

</details>


### [80] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen, Victoire Laurent, Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari, Lara Turgut, Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CE, cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (44.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: V-FiLLM 是一个从可执行计算树自动生成金融推理评测基准的框架，通过符号化求值获得 ground truth，无需人工标注即可任意规模生成题目，并暴露计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度与上下文大小四个可控难度轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在 STEM 领域对 LLM 评测已有较大进展，但针对结构化数据上的金融推理评测仍相对不足。同时，传统基准依赖人工标注或生成模型，存在标注成本高、可能继承生成器错误率的问题。

Method: V-FiLLM 从真实表格出发构建可执行计算树，通过符号化求值获得 ground truth，再将计算树渲染为自然语言问题，完全移除模型参与标注的环节。框架提供四个独立可控的难度轴：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度与上下文大小。评测了开源模型，并验证了基于验证过的思维链轨迹进行轻量 LoRA 微调的效果。

Result: 随着推理深度增加，准确率最高下降 51%；在对抗性数值扰动下准确率下降最多 47 个百分点。轻量 LoRA 微调将留出集准确率从 81.1% 提升到 85.6%，并在 FinQA 上比基础模型高出 5 个百分点。

Conclusion: V-FiLLM 提供了一种可扩展、无标注成本的金融推理评测框架，揭示了现有模型在深层组合推理与数值鲁棒性上的不足，并表明低成本 LoRA 微调是提升金融组合推理能力的有效方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 V-FiLLM，一个基于可执行计算树自动生成金融推理评测基准的框架，属于评测基准类工作，映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。

Importance reason: Financial reasoning benchmark with synthetic generation, but far outside your core continuous/multimodal/world-model focus. Signals: synthetic benchmark generation, financial reasoning, LoRA fine-tuning, adversarial numerical perturbations, no multimodal or continuous token focus

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [81] [CapProbe: Evaluating Detailed Image Captions via Full-Scene Dense Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11074)
*Mouxiao Huang, Qiangyu Yan, Borui Jiang, Han Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (58.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CapProbe 是一个全场景密集 QA 基准，将详细图像描述评估转化为区域对齐的事实核查。每张图像分解为覆盖前景和背景的粗粒度语义区域，为每个区域生成覆盖 10 个语义类别的多项选择题，形成密集的视觉事实清单。基准包含 346 张图像、1,868 个区域和 25,650 个问题，平均每张图像 74 个 QA 对。语言裁判仅根据描述作答，通过 Uncertain 选项和 Effective Accuracy 区分未回答与错误回答的探针，密度指标惩罚冗长但无信息的描述。在 13 个 VLM 上的实验显示模型间 Coverage 差距大、能力-效率权衡明显，以及稀疏或重叠评估常遗漏的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有详细图像描述评估方法存在局限：基于参考的指标（如 CIDEr、SPICE）和 LLM-as-scorer 协议难以验证密集事实性声明，而现有基于 QA 的替代方案探针密度较低、领域覆盖较窄，或缺乏问题与图像区域之间的显式对齐。需要一种更严格、更细粒度的评估协议。

Method: CapProbe 将每张图像分解为覆盖前景和背景的粗粒度语义区域，为每个保留区域生成覆盖 10 个语义类别的多项选择题，形成密集的视觉事实清单。采用两级分类法（37 个 L1 领域和 219 个 L2 子领域）指导问题生成。语言裁判仅根据描述回答，Uncertain 选项和 Effective Accuracy 提供区分未回答与错误回答的代理指标，密度指标惩罚冗长但无信息的描述。

Result: 在 13 个 VLM 上的实验显示：模型间 Coverage 差距大，存在明显的能力-效率权衡，以及稀疏或重叠评估方法常遗漏的失败模式。该协议成本效益高，通过将无约束标量评分转换为结构化 MCQ 阅读，减少了开放式评分偏差，同时在固定裁判下产生相对稳定的模型排名。

Conclusion: CapProbe 提供了一种更严格、更细粒度的详细图像描述评估方法，通过区域对齐的密集 QA 核查实现事实性验证，弥补了现有评估协议在探针密度、领域覆盖和区域对齐方面的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CapProbe，一个用于评估 VLM 详细图像描述的全场景密集 QA 基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）。

Importance reason: A solid but niche evaluation benchmark for detailed image captioning, with limited direct relevance to the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: benchmark for detailed captions, dense QA evaluation, 13 VLMs evaluated, region-aligned factual checking, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Evaluating detailed image captions from Vision-Language Models (VLMs) requires going beyond surface-level semantic similarity. Reference-based metrics (e.g., CIDEr and SPICE) and LLM-as-scorer protocols struggle to verify dense factual claims, while existing QA-based alternatives generally offer lower probe density, narrower domain coverage, or no explicit alignment between individual questions and segmented image regions. We introduce CapProbe, a full-scene dense QA benchmark that turns detailed caption evaluation into region-aligned factual checking. Each image is decomposed into coarse semantic regions covering both foreground and background elements; for every retained region, we generate multiple-choice questions spanning 10 semantic categories, forming a dense checklist of probed visual facts. Guided by a two-tier taxonomy of 37 L1 domains and 219 L2 sub-domains, CapProbe comprises 346 images, 1,868 regions, and 25,650 questions, averaging 74 QA pairs per image. A language judge answers from the caption alone; an Uncertain option and Effective Accuracy provide a judge-dependent proxy for distinguishing unanswered probes from incorrectly resolved ones, while density-based metrics penalize verbose yet uninformative captions. The protocol is cost-effective: by converting unconstrained scalar scoring into structured MCQ reading, it reduces open-ended scoring bias while remaining judge-conditioned and yields relatively stable model rankings under a fixed reader. Experiments on 13 VLMs show large Coverage gaps across models, a clear competency-efficiency trade-off, and failure modes that sparse or overlap-based evaluation often misses. The benchmark data, annotations, and evaluation code will be released soon.

</details>

### [82] [When Vision Becomes Text: Visual Token Pruning via Cross-Modal Residual Guidance in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10489)
*Congyang Ou, Ruike Song, Yang Zhou, Libo Sun, Haokui Zhang, Zhenbo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (94.22) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文提出 SIEVE，一种免训练的视觉 token 压缩方法。通过跨模态吸收（CMA）量化文本对视觉信息的吸收程度，并基于 Tikhonov 正则最小二乘将视觉 token 投影到文本子空间，利用重建残差（CMR）衡量文本无法解释的视觉信息，结合文本注意力相关性与残差空间多样性保留任务相关且互补的 token。在 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 上仅保留 11.1% 视觉 token 即保持 97.5% 性能，实现 3.62× prefill 加速与 6.02× KV 缓存缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 中大量视觉 token 带来高昂推理成本，现有视觉 token 剪枝方法依赖基于相似度的引导，仅捕获局部层级的信号，忽略了 VLM 整体推理过程中文本 token 会通过自注意力逐步吸收视觉信息这一现象。

Method: 提出跨模态吸收（CMA）从几何表征视角量化文本对视觉信息的吸收程度；提出跨模态残差（CMR），通过 Tikhonov 正则最小二乘将视觉 token 投影到文本子空间并利用重建残差量化文本无法解释的视觉信息；最终 SIEVE 结合 CMR、文本注意力相关性与残差空间多样性进行免训练的视觉 token 压缩。

Result: 在多种 VLM 架构上验证有效性。在 LLaVA-NeXT-7B 上仅保留 11.1% 视觉 token 即保持 97.5% 平均性能，实现 3.62× prefill 加速、2.49× 端到端加速和 6.02× KV 缓存缩减。

Conclusion: 通过利用 LLM 深层中文本对视觉信息的吸收现象，基于跨模态残差引导的视觉 token 剪枝能在保持性能的同时显著降低 VLM 推理成本，为高效 VLM 推理提供了新的几何视角方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SIEVE 视觉 token 剪枝压缩方法，通过跨模态残差引导减少 VLM 推理中的视觉 token 数量，属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）这一 CANONICAL 子主题，聚焦 token 压缩与稀疏推理。

Importance reason: Directly targets efficient multimodal inference via training-free visual token pruning, aligning with the user's model efficiency and continuous multimodal priorities. Signals: visual token pruning, training-free, KV cache reduction, prefill speedup, cross-modal residual

Abstract: Abundant visual information strengthens vision-language model (VLM) perception, yet massive visual tokens raise inference costs. Existing visual token pruning methods rely on similarity-based guidance, which exploits pairwise text-vision and vision-vision token correlations for compression. However, such methods only capture local layer-level signals and overlook the whole inference process in VLM. In this paper, we revisit VLM inference and present a new efficient guidance scheme that complements similarity-based guidance. In particular, we identify a key observation: as LLM layers deepen, text tokens continuously aggregate visual information via self-attention and progressively absorb partial visual content into textual representations. To quantify this phenomenon, we propose Cross Modal Absorption (CMA) from a geometric representation perspective to measure how much visual information is absorbed by text, revealing that more visual tokens in deeper layers can be approximately explained by the text subspace. We accordingly propose Cross Modal Residual (CMR). It projects visual tokens onto the text subspace via Tikhonov regularized least squares and exploits reconstruction residuals to quantify visual information that cannot be explained by text. Finally, based on CMR, we present SIEVE, a training-free visual token compression method that combines CMR, text-attention relevance, and residual-space diversity to retain task-relevant and complementary tokens. Experiments on diverse VLM architectures verify the effectiveness of SIEVE. For instance, on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, SIEVE keeps only $11.1\%$ of visual tokens while preserving $97.5\%$ of the original average performance, achieving $3.62\times$ prefill speedup, $2.49\times$ end-to-end speedup, and a $6.02\times$ KV-cache reduction.

</details>


### [83] [Dynamic Context Adapters: Efficiently Infusing History into Vision-and-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10525)
*Yuhang Song, Bor-Jiun Lin, Jiaxu Liu, Te-Chuan Chiu, Anh Nguyen, Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (85.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Dynamic Context Adapter (DCA)，一种为预训练 VLM 注入历史上下文的高效方法，采用固定大小动态压缩记忆保留历史语义，无需帧拼接，在长时序决策任务上降低 25%+ 注意力 FLOPs 和 13% 内存，同时提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在顺序决策任务中独立处理视觉输入，缺乏时序理解能力。直接将历史帧拼接到 Transformer 输入会产生二次注意力复杂度和过高内存消耗，而现有方法要么计算膨胀要么因时序压缩导致信息丢失。需要一种高效的历史上下文注入方法。

Method: 提出 Dynamic Context Adapter (DCA)，一种针对预训练 VLM 的上下文注入方法。采用固定大小、动态压缩的记忆机制来保留历史语义，避免帧拼接。DCA 在静态 VLM 与循环策略之间建立桥梁，使预训练模型具备记忆能力同时保持计算效率。

Result: DCA 在长时序任务上实现超过 25% 的注意力 FLOPs 减少和 13% 的内存节省，同时提升任务性能。

Conclusion: DCA 通过动态压缩记忆高效地为预训练 VLM 注入历史上下文，在降低计算与内存开销的同时提升长时序决策任务性能，为静态 VLM 赋予记忆能力提供了高效可行的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Dynamic Context Adapter (DCA)，通过固定大小动态压缩记忆为预训练 VLM 注入历史上下文，显著降低注意力 FLOPs 和内存开销，属于高效 MLLM 与 token/上下文压缩范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] efficient_multimodal（多模态高效化与轻量化）。

Importance reason: Efficient VLM context injection with modest gains, but tangential to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model priorities. Signals: efficient inference, VLM memory, attention FLOPs reduction, long-horizon tasks, no autoregressive/video/world-model focus

Abstract: Historical context integration presents a fundamental challenge for Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in sequential decision-making tasks. Current VLMs process visual inputs independently, which creates critical limitations for downstream applications that require temporal understanding. Direct incorporation of historical frames into Transformer inputs produces quadratic attention complexity and excessive memory consumption. Existing approaches suffer from significant drawbacks: computational inflation or substantial information loss through temporal compression. To address these challenges, we introduce Dynamic Context Adapter (DCA), a novel context injection approach for pretrained VLMs. Our method employs fixed-size, dynamically compressed memory to preserve historical semantics without frame concatenation. DCA bridges static VLMs and recurrent policies and enables memory capabilities in pretrained models while maintaining computational efficiency. DCA achieves over $25\%$ reduction in attention FLOPs and $13\%$ memory savings while improving performance on long-horizon tasks.

</details>
