### [84] [Chain of Spatial Thoughts: Modality-Agnostic Spatial Grounding for Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10278)
*Hunter Schofield, Mohammed Elmahgiubi, Mohammad Mahdavian, Richard Shi, Jinjun Shan, Amir Rasouli, Dongfeng Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (66.03) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 Space Tokens，一种轻量、架构无关的框架，通过将场景级 3D 几何与物体级空间属性蒸馏为连续潜 token，使 VLM 无需额外推理模块即可获得显式连续空间表征，并融入思维链推理以提升空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间理解是具身智能的基础，但现有 VLM 在空间推理基准上依赖额外的空间编码器或架构修改，推理计算成本高。作者希望提供一种轻量、无需额外推理模块的空间表征方案。

Method: 提出 Space Tokens 框架：将场景级 3D 几何和物体级空间属性蒸馏为连续潜 token，这些 token 可直接融入 VLM 的思维链推理过程。学习到的表征可显式解码以验证其编码了有意义的几何信息，且统一 token 接口可扩展到更多模态。

Result: 在 VSI-Bench 上，Qwen3-VL-8B 提升 4.3%，SenseNova-SI-1.3 提升 1.3%；在物体尺寸估计（79.2%）和房间尺寸估计（75.7%）上达到 SOTA。

Conclusion: 连续空间 token 为将几何推理集成到大视觉语言模型中提供了一种有效、可解释且计算高效的方式，无需额外推理模块。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于 VLM 的空间推理能力提升，通过连续空间 token 蒸馏 3D 几何信息并融入思维链推理，属于空间与 3D 多模态推理方向。同时其连续空间 token 表征与连续语言/多模态方向相关，故匹配两个方向。

Importance reason: Introduces continuous spatial tokens for VLM spatial reasoning, aligning with continuous multimodal representation and efficient inference priorities. Signals: continuous latent tokens, chain-of-thought spatial reasoning, architecture-agnostic, efficient inference, VSI-Bench gains

Abstract: Spatial understanding is fundamental to embodied intelligence, underpinning applications such as robotic manipulation, embodied navigation, and autonomous driving. Although recent vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive performance on spatial reasoning benchmarks, state-of-the-art approaches typically rely on additional spatial encoders or architectural modifications during inference, increasing computational cost. We introduce Space Tokens, a lightweight, architecture-agnostic framework that equips VLMs with explicit continuous spatial representations without requiring additional inference-time modules. By distilling scene-level 3D geometry and object-centric spatial attributes into continuous latent tokens, our method enables these modalities to be directly incorporated into a chain-of-thought reasoning process, thereby improving the VLM's spatial reasoning capabilities. At the same time, the learned representations can be explicitly decoded to verify that they encode meaningful geometric information, while the unified token interface remains extensible to additional modalities. Experiments on VSI-Bench improve Qwen3-VL-8B by 4.3% and SenseNova-SI-1.3 by 1.3%, while achieving state-of-the-art performance on object size (79.2%) and room size estimation (75.7%). These results demonstrate that continuous spatial tokens provide an effective, interpretable, and computationally efficient mechanism for integrating geometric reasoning into large vision-language models.

</details>


### [85] [Self-Geometry: GT-Free and Plug-and-Play Test-Time Adaptation for Geometrically Consistent 3D Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10708)
*Seokhyun Youn, Dahyeon Kye, Sung-Ho Bae, Jihyong Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 值得关注 (78.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Self-Geometry 是一种即插即用的测试时自适应方法，通过显式多视图几何约束（多视图一致性与对极一致性损失）结合梯度解耦、SO(3) 视角采样和 LoRA 轻量适配，提升 3D 视觉基础模型（VGGT、π³、DA3 等）在深度、相机位姿和 pointmap 估计上的几何一致性，无需 GT 标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 视觉基础模型（VFM）单次前向即可预测深度、相机位姿和 pointmap，泛化能力强，但预训练时未施加显式多视图几何一致性约束（如 bundle adjustment 计算代价过高），导致输出存在几何不一致。先前工作仅利用模型输出的隐式自一致性信号，在预训练模型高度不准确的场景下性能提升有限。

Method: Self-Geometry 包含三个组件：(1) Geometric Disentanglement Optimization，结合 Multi-View Consistency 与 Epipolar Consistency 损失，并通过 Gradient Disentanglement 防止梯度冲突；(2) Frame Angular-Neighbor，基于 SO(3) 测地距离的视角采样器，以轻量方式施加约束；(3) Lightweight TTA，通过 LoRA 对 VFM 进行轻量测试时适配。该方法以 2D 像素对应作为伪真值，直接施加显式多视图几何约束。

Result: 在六个 VFM（VGGT、π³、DA3-Giant/Large/Base/Small）和四个基准（7Scenes、ETH3D、ScanNet++、HiRoom）上，位姿估计与几何估计均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: Self-Geometry 通过显式几何约束替代隐式自一致性信号，实现了无需 GT 标注、即插即用的测试时自适应，有效提升 3D 视觉基础模型的几何一致性，且对多种 VFM 与基准具有通用性。

Classification reason: 论文关注 3D 视觉基础模型的测试时自适应，通过多视图几何约束提升深度、相机位姿与 pointmap 估计的几何一致性，属于空间与 3D 多模态推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] spatial_3d_reasoning。

Importance reason: Solid test-time adaptation for 3D vision, but far outside the user's continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: 3D vision foundation models, test-time adaptation, geometric consistency, LoRA, no GT

Abstract: Recent Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) predict depth, camera pose, and pointmap in a single forward pass without per-scene optimization, achieving strong generalization. However, enforcing explicit multi-view geometric consistency, e.g., through bundle adjustment, is computationally costly and is thus not imposed during VFM pretraining, so such inconsistency can arise. To address this, implicit self-consistency derived from model outputs (e.g., pointmaps, features), though enforced at test-time in prior work, delivers inherently limited performance gain, especially on scenes where the pretrained VFM is highly inaccurate. In contrast to this implicit signal, we propose Self-Geometry, a plug-and-play test-time adaptation pipeline that directly imposes explicit multi-view geometric constraints using 2D pixel correspondences as pseudo ground-truth. Our proposed Self-Geometry consists of Geometric Disentanglement Optimization, which combines Multi-View Consistency and Epipolar Consistency losses with Gradient Disentanglement to prevent gradient conflict; Frame Angular-Neighbor, a view sampler based on SO(3) geodesic distances for lightly imposing these constraints; and Lightweight TTA, which adapts VFMs via LoRA. Our method achieves consistent improvements in both pose and geometry estimation across six VFMs (VGGT, $\pi^3$, DA3-Giant/Large/Base/Small) and four benchmarks (7Scenes, ETH3D, ScanNet++, HiRoom).

</details>


### [86] [Multi-View Relational Distillation for Spatial Reasoning with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10864)
*Kiet T. Nguyen, Hanbo Shim, Jinwoo Kim, Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (58.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出多视角关系蒸馏（MVRD），通过蒸馏跨视角的 patch-wise 余弦相似度而非教师特征本身，在保持 VLM 预训练视觉-语言对齐的同时增强其视觉空间推理能力，并泛化到 3D 场景理解任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 的视觉空间表征在几何上脆弱，导致具身 AI、机器人、自动驾驶等场景的空间推理失败。现有方法要么在空间问答上微调 VLM（可能固化虚假视觉表征），要么融合大规模几何基础视觉模型的特征（显著增加推理时模型规模）。知识蒸馏虽可替代，但直接匹配多视图教师特征会破坏视觉与文本表征的预训练对齐，损害物体与语言语义能力。

Method: 提出多视角关系蒸馏（MVRD）：不直接蒸馏教师特征，而是蒸馏跨视图的 patch-wise 余弦相似度。这些关系编码了几何对应关系，足以支撑空间理解，同时使学生表征保持欠定（underdetermined），从而能贴近其预训练的视觉-语言空间，避免破坏语义对齐。

Result: 在多个代表性 VLM 上，MVRD 提升了视觉空间推理能力，优于监督微调和特征蒸馏，接近特征融合方法但参数增加更少、延迟更低。同时使视觉表征更几何化而保持语言对齐，并泛化到 3D 场景理解任务（物体定位、密集描述、问答）。

Conclusion: MVRD 通过蒸馏关系而非特征，在增强 VLM 空间几何能力的同时保持其视觉-语言语义对齐，是一种高效且可泛化的空间推理增强方法。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦于提升 VLM 的视觉空间推理能力，通过多视角关系蒸馏增强几何表征，属于空间推理与几何推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 spatial_3d_reasoning（空间与 3D 多模态推理）。

Importance reason: Solid distillation method for spatial reasoning, but only tangentially touches continuous multimodal representations and lacks direct alignment with top-priority generative or world-model directions. Signals: knowledge distillation, spatial reasoning, vision-language model, relational distillation, 3D scene understanding

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong image and video understanding, yet their visual-spatial representations remain geometrically fragile, leading to failures in spatial reasoning needed for embodied AI, robotics, and autonomous driving. Prior approaches to geometry grounding either fine-tune VLMs on spatial question answering, which can perpetuate spurious visual representations, or fuse features from large geometry-grounded vision models, which substantially increases model size at inference. Knowledge distillation from geometry-grounded vision models offers an alternative, but directly matching multi-view teacher features can disrupt the pretrained alignment between visual and textual representations, degrading object- and language-semantic capabilities. We propose multi-view relational distillation (MVRD), which distills patch-wise cosine similarities across views instead of the teacher features themselves. These relations encode geometric correspondences adequate for spatial understanding, while leaving the student representation underdetermined, allowing it to remain close to its pretrained vision- language space. Across representative VLMs, MVRD improves visual-spatial reasoning, outperforming supervised fine-tuning and feature distillation while approaching feature fusion methods with considerably fewer added parameters and lower latency. We show that MVRD makes visual representations more geometric while retaining language alignment, and generalizes to 3D scene understanding tasks such as object grounding, dense captioning, and question answering.

</details>


### [87] [ThinkAfford: Affordance-Centric Reasoning for Fine-Grained 3D Grounding in Cluttered Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10981)
*Xinrui Lin, Sha Zhang, Shumin Wang, Zenghuan Zhu, Jiajun Deng, Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ThinkAfford 提出一种以功能属性为中心的推理方法，用于杂乱场景中的细粒度 3D 定位。它将高召回的功能属性候选生成与指令接地推理解耦，通过可学习功能属性提示生成交互条件热图，并利用视觉提示推理模块结合完整指令进行"先思考后回答"的结构化推理，最后用 Group Relative Policy Optimization 对齐候选选择与最终 3D 定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 功能属性定位方法存在两个相互交织的失败模式：预测或选择的区域可能遗漏目标交互区域或粒度不合适，且语言接地在关系型指令下可能混淆视觉上相似的备选区域。需要一种解耦的方法来同时解决候选生成质量与指令接地推理问题。

Method: ThinkAfford 包含两个模块：(1) Affordance Proposal Generation (APG) 使用可学习功能属性提示和多层级视觉特征预测交互条件热图，无需解析物体或部件名称作为分割提示即可提取可变数量的细粒度候选；(2) Visual-Prompted Affordance Reasoning (VPAR) 基于标注的候选叠加图结合完整指令进行推理，以"先思考后回答"的结构化响应返回标识符。此外使用 Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，利用提升的 3D 重叠作为候选级奖励来对齐 VPAR 选择与最终 3D 定位。

Result: 在 SceneFun3D 验证集上，ThinkAfford 在官方评估器下达到 10.69% AP50 和 25.46% AP25，优于可比较的 3D 开放词汇和基于 VLM 的 2D-to-3D 基线。模块级诊断显示 APG 在 25% IoU 下达到 77.5% 召回率，GRPO 训练的 VPAR 在 APG 覆盖的查询上达到 72.1% 选择准确率，而监督微调仅为 63.4%。

Conclusion: ThinkAfford 通过将高召回候选生成与指令接地推理解耦，并引入 GRPO 对齐机制，有效提升了杂乱场景中细粒度 3D 功能属性定位的性能，验证了功能属性中心推理范式的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文研究杂乱场景下的 3D 功能区域定位（3D affordance grounding），属于空间与 3D 多模态推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题 spatial_3d_reasoning。

Importance reason: 3D affordance grounding is a niche spatial reasoning task with no direct overlap with the user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models, autoregressive/diffusion video generation, or world models. Signals: 3D grounding, affordance reasoning, GRPO, SceneFun3D, no continuous token/video/world-model keywords

Abstract: Task-driven 3D affordance grounding aims to localize the functional region in a cluttered 3D scene that enables an action specified by a natural-language instruction. Existing methods either predict 3D masks directly or construct them by selecting and fusing intermediate 2D/3D regions. However, they remain vulnerable to two intertwined failure modes: the predicted or selected regions may miss the target interaction area or have unsuitable granularity, while language grounding may confuse visually similar alternatives under relational instructions. To this end, we introduce ThinkAfford, which decouples high-recall affordance proposal generation from instruction-grounded reasoning. Specifically, the Affordance Proposal Generation module first uses learnable affordance prompts and multi-level visual features to predict interaction-conditioned heatmaps, extracting a variable number of fine-grained proposals without parsed object or part names as segmentation prompts. Visual-Prompted Affordance Reasoning then reasons over labeled proposal overlays using the full instruction, returning identifiers in a structured "think-then-answer" response. Moreover, Group Relative Policy Optimization uses proposal-level rewards from lifted 3D overlap to align VPAR selection with final 3D grounding. On the SceneFun3D validation split, ThinkAfford achieves 10.69% AP50 and 25.46% AP25 under the official evaluator, outperforming comparable 3D open-vocabulary and vision-language-model-based 2D-to-3D baselines. Module-level diagnostics further show that APG attains 77.5% recall at 25% intersection-over-union, while GRPO-trained VPAR achieves 72.1% selection accuracy on APG-covered queries, compared with 63.4% under supervised fine-tuning.

</details>


### [88] [CausalSplat: Towards Comprehensive Hierarchical Reasoning in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11150)
*Jiayu Ding, Meilu Song, Yun Chen, Wei Gao, Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 空间与 3D 多模态推理

Importance: 普通相关 (58.68) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CausalSplat 提出"推理式 3D 高斯分割"任务，构建 Causal-LERF 与 Causal-ScanNet 两个基准，系统评估常识、空间、affordance 与反事实推理，并设计将视觉语言模型与 3D 场景图结合的框架，在推理基准上达到 SOTA 且对标准指代与开放词汇分割任务具有强泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3DGS 开放词汇场景理解方法局限于显式查询，难以解释隐式意图、复杂空间约束与具身交互所需的常识推理，缺乏对推理式分割的系统评测与建模。

Method: 提出推理式 3D 高斯分割任务，构建两个基准 Causal-LERF 与 Causal-ScanNet 评估常识、空间、affordance 与反事实推理；提出 CausalSplat 框架，将视觉语言模型与 3D 场景图结合，解耦显式结构感知与隐式逻辑推理。

Result: 实验表明现有 SOTA 方法在推理基准上表现不佳，而 CausalSplat 在推理基准上达到 SOTA，并在标准指代与开放词汇 3D 分割任务上展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将 VLM 与 3D 场景图结合可有效解耦结构感知与逻辑推理，显著提升 3D 场景中的推理式分割能力，为具身交互场景理解提供新范式。

Classification reason: 论文提出 3D 高斯泼溅场景中的推理式分割任务，涉及空间推理、常识推理、affordance 推理与反事实推理，属于空间与 3D 多模态推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] spatial_3d_reasoning。

Importance reason: Solid 3D reasoning benchmark work, but it is outside your core continuous/multimodal generation and world-model focus. Signals: 3D Gaussian Splatting, reasoning segmentation, vision-language model, 3D scene graph, benchmark

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has advanced open vocabulary scene understanding, existing methods remain confined to explicit queries. They struggle to interpret implicit intents, complex spatial constraints, and commonsense reasoning required for practical embodied interactions. To address this gap, we introduce the task of reasoning 3D Gaussian segmentation and construct two benchmarks, Causal-LERF and Causal-ScanNet. These benchmarks systematically evaluate commonsense, spatial, affordance, and counterfactual reasoning. Evaluations reveal that current state of the art methods perform poorly on these reasoning challenges. Therefore, we propose CausalSplat, a framework that integrates vision-language models with 3D scene graphs to disentangle explicit structural perception from implicit logical inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalSplat achieves state of the art performance on our reasoning benchmarks while showing strong generalizability on standard referring and open vocabulary 3D segmentation tasks. Project Page:

</details>

## 统一多模态模型 [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Ex-Omni-2D: Expressive Omni-Modal Dialogue Models with Native Visual Presence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10720)
*Haoyu Zhang, Zhipeng Li, Xiaoying Tang, Tianshu Yu, Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 值得关注 (74.3) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Ex-Omni-2D 是一个全模态对话框架，通过 Visual Thought Plan (VTP) 和共享声学-时序接口，生成文本、个性化语音与参考条件视频的协调响应，并采用全序列视频生成器作为教师、块因果流式学生进行高效增量生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态对话模型虽能理解多模态输入并合成语音回复，但回复缺乏视觉呈现（视觉具身化）。作者希望让对话模型不仅"说话"，还能生成与语音同步的、参考条件化的视频化身，实现真正具有视觉临场感的全模态对话。

Method: 提出 Ex-Omni-2D 框架：1) 预测结构化 Visual Thought Plan (VTP) 描述场景、情感与动作；2) 生成响应文本与原生多码本语音单元；3) 语音单元构成共享声学-时序接口，解码为语音并与视频帧在线对齐；4) 全序列视频生成器作为 Teacher，蒸馏为少步块因果 Streaming Student，其 Prefix Streaming 机制在连续块间传递干净 latent 以减少累积退化。

Result: 四步推理下，完整四 GPU 流水线在 400×720/720×400 分辨率下实现端到端 RTF 1.293，提供了质量与效率的实用平衡点。

Conclusion: Ex-Omni-2D 通过共享声学-时序接口解耦语音与视频学习，避免大规模 query-text-speech-video 监督需求，并通过教师-学生蒸馏实现高效流式视频生成，为具有视觉临场感的全模态对话提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Ex-Omni-2D，一个全模态对话框架，能同时生成文本、个性化语音和参考条件视频，属于理解与生成一体化的统一多模态模型，映射到 [CANONICAL] unified_multimodal_model。

Importance reason: Novel omni-modal dialogue with visual presence, but tangential to core continuous-model and video-generation priorities. Signals: omni-modal dialogue, visual thought plan, streaming video generation, block-causal distillation, personalized speech

Abstract: Omni-modal dialogue models can understand multimodal inputs and synthesize spoken replies, yet their responses remain visually disembodied. We introduce \textbf{Ex-Omni-2D}, an omni-modal dialogue framework that generates a coordinated response comprising text, personalized speech, and reference-conditioned video. Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-vlm_mllm_general'></div>

### [90] [MedUP: Awakening Unified Understanding and Perception in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10635)
*Yuan Wang, Hualiang Wang, Yixin Chen, Songtao Jiang, Shujian Gao, Jiaming Lin, Siming Fu, Jian Wu, Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MedUP 是一个医学视觉语言模型，通过 UniMedTok 区域 tokenizer 将掩码编码为 LLM 词汇表中的离散 token，在共享 token 空间中原生统一感知与理解，并配套 1.84M 实例的 UniMed-Train 语料与 UniMed-Bench 评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Med-VLM 在精确视觉感知、分割与定位上存在不足，要么将区域表示为坐标字符串，要么依赖外部模块将感知与理解解耦，造成区域-语言对齐的表征鸿沟。

Method: 提出 MedUP 模型，核心是 UniMedTok 区域 tokenizer，将掩码编码为 LLM 词汇表中的离散 token，使模型能在文本中无缝交织掩码 token。同时构建 UniMed-Train 语料（1.84M 实例，涵盖文本引导分割、区域接地理解、医学 VQA 与 CoT 分割）和 UniMed-Bench 统一评测基准。

Result: MedUP 在全部任务上优于原生、智能体式与双解码器 Med-VLM，同时与专业分割器保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 统一理解与感知建模在医学视觉语言模型中具有强大潜力，共享 token 空间能有效弥合区域-语言对齐的表征鸿沟。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MedUP，一个统一理解与感知的医学视觉语言模型，属于通用 MLLM 架构与多模态指令微调范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general 子主题。

Importance reason: Medical-specific VLM with region tokenization, but outside the user's core continuous-model, video-generation, and world-model focus. Signals: medical VLM, region tokenizer, unified perception, segmentation, grounding

Abstract: Medical Vision-Language Models (Med-VLMs) excel at verbalizing visual content, yet precise visual perception, segmentation, and grounding remain challenging. Existing approaches either verbalize regions as coordinate strings or rely on external modules that decouple perception from understanding, creating representation gaps for region-language alignment. We present MedUP, a Med-VLM that natively unifies perception and understanding within a shared token space. At its core lies UniMedTok, a region tokenizer that encodes masks as discrete tokens in the LLM vocabulary, enabling the model to seamlessly interleave mask tokens with text. We curate UniMed-Train, a 1.84M-instance corpus spanning text-guided segmentation, region-grounded understanding, medical VQA and CoT-based segmentation, and introduce UniMed-Bench for unified evaluation. Extensive experiments show that MedUP outperforms native, agentic, and dual-decoder Med-VLMs across all tasks while remaining competitive with specialist segmentors, demonstrating the strong potential of unified understanding and perception modeling.

</details>


### [91] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu, Ligang Huang, Zeli Su, Zewei Pan, Zhaoyang Han, Xing Chen, Yuanfeng Song, Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (73.05) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SkillLens 提出 Visual Skill Cards (VSCs)，一种状态条件化的视觉程序记忆表征，将可复用的 GUI 操作流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定。通过 Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card 从异构交互经验构建 VSC，推理时检索相关卡片并选择性展开证据供冻结的 VLM 执行器进行 GUI 动作预测；同时支持 CardDistill 将 VSC 证据作为教师上下文蒸馏出无需检索的学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体虽能感知丰富的软件界面，但其决策缺乏视觉程序记忆：能识别单个控件，却难以判断当前处于哪个熟悉的工作流、下一步该操作哪个控件、以及什么证据能确认进展。原始交互轨迹冗长嘈杂难以直接条件化，而纯文本技能又常丢失使流程适用的视觉状态。

Method: 提出 Visual Skill Cards (VSCs) 状态条件化记忆表征，通过 Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card 从异构交互经验构建，绑定可复用流程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号。推理时检索相关卡片并选择性展开所需证据，供冻结的视觉语言模型执行器进行 grounded GUI 动作预测。CardDistill 利用 VSC 证据作为特权教师上下文，训练无需运行时卡片检索的学生模型。

Result: 在 Multimodal-Mind2Web 和 WebLINX-BrowserGym 上，SkillLens 使冻结的 GPT-5.4-mini 执行器分别提升 +11.6 点 Step SR 和 +2.9 点 Overall；CardDistill 进一步使仅学生模型的 Qwen3-VL-2B 分别提升 +12.0 和 +3.2 点。

Conclusion: 视觉程序记忆（VSC）能显著提升 GUI 动作预测的准确性与泛化能力，且通过 CardDistill 可将检索增强能力蒸馏到轻量学生模型中，实现无需运行时检索的高效部署。

Classification reason: 论文提出 Visual Skill Cards 记忆表征，用于检索增强的 GUI 动作预测，并基于视觉语言模型执行器（GPT-5.4-mini、Qwen3-VL）进行推理与蒸馏。核心是视觉语言模型在 GUI 交互场景中的应用与增强，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Focuses on GUI agent memory rather than core continuous/multimodal generation or world-model directions, with limited direct alignment to your priority subtopics. Signals: GUI action prediction, visual skill cards, retrieval-augmented, on-policy distillation, frozen VLM executor

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [92] [Watching Synthetic Videos: Aligning Cross-modal Representations with Visual Synthesis for Zero-shot Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11013)
*Liangyu Fu, Junbo Wang, Yuke Li, Ya Jing, Xuecheng Wu, Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (85.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 WSV 框架，通过两阶段训练实现零样本视频字幕生成：先用预训练文生视频模型生成合成视频潜表征，再用 polisher 弥合真实与合成视频分布差距，最后用 prompter 将精炼潜表征条件化 GPT-2 生成字幕。在 MSVD、MSR-VTT、VATEX 上取得 B@4=52、CIDEr=95.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本视频字幕生成采用纯文本训练范式，训练时模型无法接触视频分布，导致训练（纯文本）与推理（视频）之间存在跨模态鸿沟。以往方法仅用简单线性变换桥接该鸿沟，但文本与视频之间的固有差异使得跨模态表征空间对齐不充分，生成的字幕不准确。

Method: 提出 WSV（Watching Synthetic Videos）框架，包含两个训练阶段。第一阶段利用预训练文生视频模型生成对应的合成视频潜表征，并设计 polisher 弥合真实与合成视频分布之间的差距以增强潜表征保真度。第二阶段设计 prompter，将精炼后的潜表征作为条件输入 GPT-2 生成字幕。推理时，输入视频由预训练 3D Causal VAE 编码后直接送入 prompter，引导 GPT-2 生成最终字幕。

Result: 在 MSVD、MSR-VTT 和 VATEX 三个数据集上进行实验，方法在 B@4 指标上达到 52，在 CIDEr 指标上达到 95.7，优于以往零样本视频字幕生成方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用文生视频模型生成合成视频潜表征并设计 polisher 弥合分布差距，WSV 有效缓解了纯文本训练范式下的跨模态鸿沟，显著提升了零样本视频字幕生成的准确性。

Classification reason: 论文提出零样本视频字幕生成框架（WSV），利用文生视频模型生成合成视频潜表征并对齐跨模态表征，属于视觉语言模型与视频字幕生成范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。同时涉及文生视频模型作为中间工具，故也匹配 multimodal_generation 方向。

Importance reason: Uses synthetic video latents from text-to-video models to align cross-modal representations for zero-shot captioning, touching on continuous latent representations but not core continuous VLM or world-model directions. Signals: zero-shot video captioning, synthetic video latent, text-to-video generation, cross-modal alignment, GPT-2 conditioning

Abstract: Text-only training is a popular paradigm in zero-shot video captioning, where the video distribution is not available to the model during training, leading to a cross-modal gap between the training (text-only) and the inference (video-only). Previous works attempt to bridge the gap through simple linear transformations. However, the inherent gap between text and video makes cross-modal representation space alignment insufficient, resulting in inaccurate sentences. To address this issue, we propose a novel zero-shot video captioning framework (WSV) consisting of two training stages, which first generates corresponding synthetic video latent representations via a pretrained text-to-video generation model. To strengthen the fidelity of the latent representations, we propose a polisher capable of bridging the gap between real and synthetic video distributions. Subsequently, we design a prompter that conditions GPT-2 on the polished latent representations to generate the captions in the second training stage. During inference, an input video is encoded by a pretrained 3D Causal VAE and then fed directly into the prompter, which in turn guides GPT-2 to produce the final caption. Experimental results conducted on MSVD, MSR-VTT, and VATEX datasets demonstrate that our proposed method achieves scores of 52 and 95.7 on the B@4 and CIDEr metrics, respectively.

</details>


### [93] [MultiModal Code-Switching: Interleaving Visual Objects into Language for Explicit Object-Level Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11167)
*Changhao Xiang, Shangyu Xing, Zhen Wu, Jianbing Zhang, Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 提出 MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS)，一种新的 MLLM 预训练范式，通过将文本实体替换为对应视觉对象来交错视觉与语言，提供显式对象级对齐监督，缓解图像级对齐的指代歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 主要依赖图像-文本对进行模态对齐预训练，将全局图像表征映射到长文本描述。这种图像级对齐存在指代歧义：模型难以从全局表征推断多个视觉对象与文本实体之间的对应关系，导致数据效率低下和语义接地不充分。

Method: 受语言学中语码转换现象启发，MMCS 将文本实体替换为对应的视觉对象，交错视觉与语言，强制局部视觉-语言接地。同时开发了可扩展的数据合成流水线，生成包含 773K 样本、具有精确对象-实体对应关系的预训练数据集。

Result: MMCS 数据效率高：仅用 50K 样本即可匹配或超越在 600K 图像-文本对上训练的模型。此外，MMCS 在不同模型规模下持续提升视觉接地和感知能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式对象级对齐监督，MMCS 显著提升了 MLLM 的视觉接地能力与数据效率，为多模态预训练提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种新的 MLLM 预训练范式 MMCS，通过将视觉对象插入语言序列实现显式对象级对齐，属于通用视觉语言模型与 MLLM 架构/预训练方法范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] vlm_mllm_general。

Importance reason: Proposes a data-efficient object-level alignment pretraining paradigm for MLLMs, directly relevant to unified multimodal and continuous multimodal reasoning interests. Signals: MLLM pretraining, object-level alignment, data efficiency, visual grounding, code-switching

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) predominantly rely on image-text pairs for modality alignment pretraining, mapping global image representations to long textual descriptions. However, this image-level alignment suffers from referential ambiguity: models struggle to infer the correspondences between multiple visual objects and textual entities from the global representation, leading to data inefficiency and suboptimal semantic grounding. To address this, we propose MultiModal Code-Switching (MMCS), a novel pretraining paradigm that provides explicit object-level supervision. Inspired by the linguistic phenomenon of code-switching, MMCS interleaves vision and language by replacing textual entities with their corresponding visual objects, enforcing local vision-language grounding. We further develop a scalable data synthesis pipeline to generate a pretraining dataset of 773K samples with accurate object-entity correspondences. Experiments show that MMCS is highly data-efficient: with only 50K samples, it matches or surpasses models trained on 600K image-text pairs. Furthermore, MMCS consistently improves visual grounding and perception capabilities across varying model scales.

</details>


<div id='direction-multimodal_understanding_generation-multimodal_vqa_doc'></div>

## 视觉问答与文档理解 [[Back]](#toc)

VQA、文档/图表/表格理解、OCR-free 文档智能。

### [94] [FormStruct-Bench:A Hierarchical and Diagnostic Benchmark for Table-Form Document Structure Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10396)
*Lujie Ban, Jiangtao Zhu, Yuanheng Yu, Jiasheng Shi, Chenhao Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (54.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FormStruct-Bench 是一个层级化、诊断性的表格表单文档结构识别基准，通过 70 个可复用模板扩展为 7000 个验证实例，在文档级和组件级评估结构识别能力，揭示内容读取与层级结构恢复之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格文档基准要么评估整体文档输出，要么评估传统表格网格，聚合分数无法揭示结构失败发生在何处。作者希望构建一个能追溯具体结构失败模式的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建 70 个可复用模板，通过保留来源的 Director-Artist-Verifier 流水线扩展为 7000 个验证实例；测试集 1100 个实例经过人工审查且与模板不相交。评估协议使用五个主要指标和三个结构特定诊断，覆盖页面、模式、组件三个层级，并包含难度、结构约束和视觉退化切片。评测了 14 个 API 托管/本地部署系统及两个 SFT 变体。

Result: 最佳文档级分数达到 83.85%，而最佳细粒度结构分数低于 18%，揭示了读取文档内容与恢复层级和区域组织之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前系统在读取表格表单内容方面表现尚可，但在恢复文档层级结构和区域组织方面存在明显不足，说明可靠的表格表单理解仍需在结构识别能力上取得突破。

Classification reason: 该论文提出表格表单文档结构识别基准 FormStruct-Bench，属于文档/表格理解与评测范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题"视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）。虽然它同时涉及评测基准，但文档结构识别是核心主题，且该子主题明确涵盖表格理解。

Importance reason: A niche document-structure benchmark with limited overlap with the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models and generative world models. Signals: benchmark paper, document structure recognition, diagnostic evaluation, gap between content reading and hierarchy recovery, no generative or continuous-model focus

Abstract: Transforming table-form documents into machine-processable records requires recovering not only their visible content but also the multilevel structure that organizes it. However, existing benchmarks evaluate either holistic document outputs or conventional table grids, and their aggregate scores provide little insight into where structural failures occur. We introduce FormStruct-Bench, a hierarchical and diagnostic benchmark that evaluates table-form document structure recognition at both the document level and progressively finer component levels, allowing aggregate performance to be traced back to specific structural failure modes. To construct auditable ground truth at scale, we annotate 70 reusable templates and expand them into 7,000 verified instances through a provenance-preserving Director--Artist--Verifier pipeline; all 1,100 instances in the template-disjoint test set additionally receive human review. Our evaluation protocol uses five primary metrics and three structure-specific diagnostics across page, schema, and component levels, together with slices over difficulty, structural constraints, and visual degradation. Across 14 API-hosted and locally deployable systems plus two SFT variants, the best document-level score reaches 83.85%, whereas the best reported fine-grained structural score remains below 18%. These results reveal a pronounced gap between reading document content and recovering the hierarchy and regional organization required for reliable table-form understanding.

</details>


### [95] [GeoSeg-OV: Bridging Geospatial Gaps with Structural Guidance for Open-Vocabulary Remote Sensing Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10426)
*Ruizhong Liu, Tingzhang Luo, Zaiyan Zhang, Jundong Chen, Hongruixuan Chen, Shaoguang Huang, Hongyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (44.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: GeoSeg-OV 提出一种开放词汇遥感分割方法，将辅助视觉基础模型（VFM）的特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦，重新用作结构引导进行代价聚合与解码，以缓解地理空间域偏移并提升跨数据集泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇遥感分割面临地理空间域偏移（异构区域、空间分辨率、采集平台差异）导致的视觉-文本匹配弱化与跨数据集泛化受限问题。现有方法引入辅助视觉基础模型（VFM）特征作为额外匹配证据，但可能引入不一致的匹配信号，且未充分利用 VFM 的结构敏感表征。

Method: GeoSeg-OV 将辅助 VFM 特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦，重新用作结构引导。具体包括：从多旋转 CLIP 特征构建方向鲁棒的代价体；冻结的 VFM 并行提取多尺度结构敏感特征；提出 Structure-Guided Aggregation (SGA) 将代价 token 与 CLIP 语义引导结合 VFM 成对结构偏置进行空间传播，随后进行文本条件类别推理；提出 Cost-Aware Decoding (CAD) 根据当前解码器上下文自适应细化并融合多尺度语义与结构引导。

Result: 在全球 High-Resolution Land Cover (HRLC) 基准（覆盖六大洲七个数据集）上，GeoSeg-OV 在两种训练设置下分别以 +2.5 和 +2.7 平均 mIoU 超越现有最优方法。大规模零样本案例研究进一步展示了其跨地理域与类别系统的泛化能力，无需目标域标注或重训练。

Conclusion: 将 VFM 特征从视觉-文本匹配中解耦并作为结构引导使用，比直接耦合为匹配证据更有效，能够缓解地理空间域偏移并提升开放词汇遥感分割的跨数据集泛化性能。

Classification reason: 该论文研究开放词汇遥感图像分割，属于视觉语言模型在像素级语义理解任务上的应用，与多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答与文档理解（multimodal_vqa_doc）这一 CANONICAL 子主题相关，因其涉及视觉-文本匹配与跨模态语义理解。

Importance reason: Remote sensing segmentation is outside your core focus areas and lacks alignment with your priority subtopics. Signals: remote sensing, open-vocabulary segmentation, domain shift, vision foundation model, negative keyword match

Abstract: Open-vocabulary remote sensing segmentation has recently emerged as a promising paradigm that enables pixel-level recognition of arbitrary categories specified by natural language, including classes unseen during training. However, geospatial domain shifts caused by heterogeneous regions, spatial resolutions, and acquisition platforms weaken visual-text matching and limit cross-dataset generalization. Recent attempts have begun to incorporate auxiliary vision foundation models (VFMs), typically coupling their features with text embeddings as additional matching evidence. However, this strategy may introduce inconsistent matching signals while leaving the structure-sensitive representations of VFMs insufficiently exploited. We therefore propose GeoSeg-OV, which decouples auxiliary VFM features from visual-text matching and repurposes them as structural guidance for cost aggregation and decoding. GeoSeg-OV constructs an orientation-robust cost volume from multi-rotation CLIP features, while a frozen VFM extracts multi-scale structure-sensitive features in parallel. We propose Structure-Guided Aggregation (SGA), which integrates cost tokens and CLIP semantic guidance with VFM-derived pairwise structural biases for coherent spatial propagation, followed by text-conditioned class-wise reasoning. We further introduce Cost-Aware Decoding (CAD) to adaptively refine and fuse multi-scale semantic and structural guidance based on the current decoder context. On the global High-Resolution Land Cover (HRLC) benchmark spanning seven datasets across six continents, GeoSeg-OV outperforms the state-of-the-art by +2.5 and +2.7 average mIoU under two training settings. A large-scale zero-shot case study further demonstrates its generalization across geographic domains and category systems without target-domain annotations or retraining.

</details>


### [96] [InSight-doc: Agentic Visual Perception for Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10628)
*Kaican Li, Weiyan Xie, Lewei Yao, Jiannan Wu, Lanqing Hong, Yongxiang Huang, Nevin L. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (55.43) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: InSight-doc 是一个智能体式视觉感知框架，将视觉分辨率视为可自适应分配的推理时资源，从低分辨率开始选择性放大到高分辨率区域以获取更细粒度证据，无需外部检索器。通过 17.9K SFT 样本和 19.2K 硬 RL 样本训练，在文档 VQA 基准上提升 4.3-16.4 个准确率点，长文档上幻觉降低 40% 以上，推理延迟降低 41%-68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档理解需要对大量视觉丰富页面进行推理，导致推理成本高昂且易出现上下文退化（context rot）。现有方法通常以固定高分辨率处理所有页面，缺乏对视觉分辨率的自适应分配。

Method: 提出 InSight-doc 智能体式视觉感知框架，将视觉分辨率视为可自适应调整的推理时资源。框架从低分辨率开始，通过智能体决策选择性放大到高分辨率区域以获取更细粒度证据，无需外部检索器。构建了包含 17.9K 高质量 SFT 样本（含区域级放大轨迹）和 19.2K 硬 RL 样本的主动感知语料库，通过 SFT+RL 两阶段训练。

Result: InSight-doc-8B 在文档 VQA 基准上相比基线提升 4.3-16.4 个准确率点；在长文档上幻觉降低超过 40%，推理延迟降低 41%-68%，同时保持准确率优势。

Conclusion: 将视觉分辨率作为自适应推理时资源是提升长文档理解效率与准确率的有效途径，智能体式主动感知结合 SFT+RL 训练能够显著降低推理成本并减少幻觉。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦长文档视觉理解，通过智能体式视觉感知框架进行文档 VQA，属于文档理解方向，映射到 [CANONICAL] 子主题"视觉问答与文档理解"。

Importance reason: 文档理解智能体虽提升效率，但偏离用户核心的连续多模态与视频生成方向。 Signals: 长文档理解, 自适应分辨率, 推理效率, 幻觉降低, 无外部检索器

Abstract: Long-document understanding often requires reasoning over many visually rich pages, making inference costly and prone to context rot. In this work, we propose InSight-doc, an agentic visual perception framework that treats visual resolution as an adaptive reasoning-time resource. InSight-doc starts from low resolution and selectively zooms into high-resolution regions for finer evidence, without relying on any external retriever. To train such an agent, we construct an active-perception corpus of 17.9K high-quality SFT examples with region-level zoom-in trajectories, accompanied by 19.2K hard RL examples. Through SFT+RL, InSight-doc-8B improves the baseline by 4.3--16.4 accuracy points over document VQA benchmarks. On long documents, it reduces hallucination by more than 40% and inference latency by 41%--68% while maintaining an accuracy lead. Our code, datasets, and model are released at .

</details>


### [97] [RadFusion: Towards Threshold-Controllable Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10505)
*Ying Jin, Noel C. F. Codella, John Corring, Mu Wei, Dinei Florencio, Eric Horvitz*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (41.83) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: RadFusion 是一个面向放射学报告生成的框架，通过融合多标签分类器与 VQA 报告生成器，并利用 LLM 重写报告，使报告生成具备阈值可控性，从而支持 ROC 验证与临床场景自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成模型无法控制诊断内容的敏感度-特异度权衡，而不同临床场景（急诊分诊 vs 确诊解读）对敏感度和特异度有不同需求，且监管审批需要 ROC 验证。单一固定报告无法适应这些需求。

Method: RadFusion 融合多标签分类器（提供每种疾病的置信度分数）与基于 VQA 的报告生成器（详细描述医学发现），再由 LLM 根据所选阈值重写报告，使报告中的诊断结论遵循分类器在阈值下的决策，同时保持与生成器描述的忠实性。

Result: 在 MIMIC-CXR 上，RadFusion 的性能符合分类器的 ROC 曲线：扫描阈值并将报告映射回类别标签可复现分类器验证的 ROC 性能。相比无控制生成，敏感度在匹配特异度下提升 6.9%，特异度在匹配敏感度下提升 20.7%。

Conclusion: RadFusion 使报告生成在临床上可自适应、可量化验证且诊断上更可靠，通过阈值可控性支持 ROC 分析，为监管审批和临床操作点选择提供了基础。

Classification reason: 该论文面向医学影像报告生成，属于视觉问答/文档理解范畴（VQA-based report generator），映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 multimodal_vqa_doc 子主题。

Importance reason: Radiology report generation with threshold control is methodologically interesting but far outside the user's core focus on continuous multimodal models, video generation, and world models. Signals: medical domain, negative keyword clinical, no autoregressive/video/world-model keywords, VQA-based generation, LLM rewriting

Abstract: Automated radiology report generation is advancing rapidly in response to the shortage of radiologists, yet unlike a perception model, existing generation models offer no control over the sensitivity-specificity trade-off of their diagnostic content. Such control is essential because clinical scenarios diverge: emergency triage prioritizes sensitivity to reduce missed findings, whereas confirmatory interpretation emphasizes specificity to limit unnecessary interventions. A single fixed report can neither adapt to these scenarios nor support the ROC-based validation widely expected for regulatory clearance. We introduce RadFusion, a framework that equips report generation with threshold controllability. Our method fuses a multi-label classifier, which provides per-disease confidence scores, with a VQA-based report generator, which describes medical findings in detail; an LLM then rewrites the report so that its stated diagnoses follow the classifier's decisions at the selected threshold while staying grounded in the generator's descriptions. On MIMIC-CXR, the performance of RadFusion conforms to the classifier's ROC curve: sweeping the threshold and mapping the reports back to class labels reproduces the classifier's validated ROC performance. This conformance makes generated reports quantitatively evaluable through ROC analysis, strengthening the case for regulatory clearance, and enables operating-point selection that matches report behavior to clinical context. Moreover, combining the two model types improves diagnostic accuracy over uncontrolled generation: sensitivity increases by 6.9% at matched specificity, and specificity by 20.7% at matched sensitivity. These results show that RadFusion makes report generation clinically adaptable, quantitatively verifiable, and diagnostically more reliable.

</details>

### [98] [R4DSG: Relative 4D Scene Graph Memory for Object-Centric Question Answering in Long Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11017)
*Ke Ma, Yamin Mao, Weiming Li, Shuai Tan, Yijie Zhong, Hao Chen, Haofen Wang, Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.MM, cs.HC, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 值得关注 (77.53) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: R4DSG 提出一种相对 4D 场景图记忆方法，将长时程第一视角视频转换为按时间、地点、持久物体、锚点相对变化和局部交互上下文索引的可查询记忆条目，用于物体中心的长视频问答，在 EgoLifeQA 物体相关子集上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程第一视角视频是可穿戴 AI 助手的丰富数据源，但物体中心问题（如物品被移到哪里、何时改变状态、为何被重新放置）难以回答，因为基于字幕和转录的记忆难以保持持久物体身份或结构化空间变化。现有长视频 QA 方法主要强调时序定位和片段检索，而先前的 3D 场景图方法通常假设比自由运动可穿戴 RGB 视频更强的几何信息（点云、RGB-D、位姿视图、稀疏重建等）。

Method: R4DSG 将视频转换为紧凑的可查询记忆条目，按时间、地点、持久物体、锚点相对变化和局部交互上下文索引。核心思想是分离稳定锚点与动态物体，跨帧保持持久物体身份，并通过锚点相对转换而非全局对齐世界模型来表示物体状态。方法基于 RGB-only 的可提示视频分割、时序传播和相对 3D lifting 等近期进展，直接生成可用于长时程问答的检索就绪记忆。

Result: 在 EgoLifeQA 的 255 个物体相关问题子集上，question-only 检索设置下，整体比 EgoRAG-Text 提升 6.7 分，在 when 类问题上提升 12.5 分，凸显了时序组织的物体记忆的价值。

Conclusion: 相对 4D 场景图可作为可穿戴助手、AR 系统和具身多媒体代理的实用记忆基底，为长时程第一视角视频中的物体中心问答提供有效支持。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦长时程第一视角（egocentric）视频中的物体中心问答，属于长视频理解与视频问答范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 video_understanding_temporal（视频理解与时序推理）子主题。

Importance reason: Solid video-QA memory method but tangential to your core continuous-model and generation priorities. Signals: egocentric video QA, 4D scene graph memory, object-centric reasoning, long-video retrieval, EgoLifeQA

Abstract: Long-horizon egocentric video is a rich substrate for wearable AI assistants, but object-centric questions such as where an item was moved, when it last changed state, or why it was relocated remain difficult because caption- and transcript-based memories rarely preserve persistent object identity or structured spatial change. Existing long-video QA methods mainly emphasize temporal grounding and clip retrieval, while prior 3D scene-graph methods typically assume stronger geometry than free-motion wearable RGB video provides, including point clouds, RGB-D input, posed views, sparse reconstruction, or reconstructed scenes. R4DSG introduces a relative 4D scene graph memory for long egocentric video. Instead of storing raw graph sequences, R4DSG converts video into compact queryable memory entries indexed by time, place, persistent objects, anchor-relative change, and local interaction context. The main idea is to separate stable anchors from dynamic objects, maintain persistent object identity across frames, and represent object state through anchor-relative transitions rather than a globally aligned world model. Built on recent RGB-only advances in promptable video segmentation, temporal propagation, and relative 3D lifting, the method produces a retrieval-ready memory directly usable for long-horizon question answering. Evaluation on a 255-question object-related subset from EgoLifeQA shows, under question-only retrieval, a 6.7-point overall gain over EgoRAG-Text and a 12.5-point gain on when questions, which highlights the value of temporally organized object memory. These results position relative 4D scene graphs as a practical memory substrate for wearable assistants, AR systems, and embodied multimedia agents. GitHub Page: .

</details>


### [99] [Temporally Grounded Compositional Camera Motion Understanding via Geometric Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10932)
*Dazhao Du, Shiyan Du, Jian Liu, Yongjian Yu, Bohai Gu, Tao Han, Hualuo Liu, Eric Liu, Yujia Zhang, Xi Chen, Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (82.08) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CamChoreo 基准（4229 个真实单镜头视频片段，专家标注时间分段与 20 个方向感知标签的组合式相机运动），并提出 CamDistill 方法，通过知识蒸馏将冻结 3D 基础模型的几何知识注入轻量相机 token，在推理时无需运行昂贵的 3D 模型即可达到与直接特征注入（CamInject）相当的精度，将相机运动理解从片段级标注推进到时间定位的组合式识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 对相机运动的理解通常对整个片段分配一个或多个标签，忽略了真实相机运动的两大特性：运动可在镜头内变化、多种运动可同时发生。此外，当前 MLLM 的视觉编码器强调语义内容而非相机运动所依赖的几何证据，导致细粒度组合式运动识别困难。

Method: 提出 CamChoreo 基准，包含 4229 个真实单镜头视频片段，使用 20 个方向感知标签的紧凑词汇进行专家标注，近半片段包含复合相机运动。提出 CamDistill 方法：训练时将冻结 3D 基础模型的几何知识蒸馏进轻量相机 token，推理时移除 3D 模型。对比基线 CamInject 直接注入 3D 模型特征。

Result: CamDistill 在不于推理时运行 3D teacher 的情况下，达到了与直接特征注入（CamInject）相当的精度，验证了知识蒸馏的有效性。

Conclusion: CamChoreo 与 CamDistill 共同将相机运动理解从片段级标注推进到时间定位的组合式识别，为空间智能与可控视频生成提供了更细粒度的运动理解基础。

Classification reason: 论文研究相机运动理解，将其建模为时间定位（temporally grounded）与组合式识别任务，属于视频理解与时序推理范畴，映射到 [CANONICAL] video_understanding_temporal。

Importance reason: Solid benchmark+distillation work for camera motion understanding, but it is a niche video understanding task with limited direct connection to the user's core continuous/multimodal generation priorities. Signals: camera motion understanding, temporally grounded compositional recognition, knowledge distillation, 3D geometry model, MLLM video understanding

Abstract: Understanding camera motion is fundamental to video perception, with applications in spatial intelligence and controllable video generation. Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide a natural interface for this task, but existing work typically assigns one or more labels to an entire clip. Such clip-level recognition overlooks two defining properties of real camera motion: it can change within a shot, and multiple movements can occur simultaneously. We therefore formulate camera-motion understanding as temporally grounded, compositional recognition, which requires a model to localize motion-consistent intervals and identify every movement active within each interval. We introduce CamChoreo, a benchmark of 4,229 real single-shot clips with expert-annotated temporal segments. Its annotations use a compact vocabulary of 20 direction-aware labels, and nearly half of the segments contain compound camera motion, with multiple movement primitives active simultaneously. Recognizing such fine-grained, compositional motion is hard for current MLLMs, whose visual encoders emphasize semantic content rather than the geometric evidence on which camera motion depends. Directly injecting features from a frozen 3D foundation model addresses this gap, but requires running the expensive geometry model on every input; we refer to this baseline as CamInject. We instead propose CamDistill, which distills the same geometric knowledge into lightweight camera tokens during training and removes the 3D model at inference. CamDistill matches the accuracy of direct feature injection without running the 3D teacher at inference. Together, CamChoreo and CamDistill advance camera-motion understanding from clip-level labeling to temporally grounded, compositional recognition. Project page: .

</details>


### [100] [StreamFlow: Dynamic Memory Flows for Streaming Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10949)
*Muxin Fu, Yifan Zhang, Wentao Zhang, Fangming Guo, Qian Chen, Guibin Zhang, Shuicheng Yan, Bo An*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视频理解与时序推理

Importance: 高优先级 (84.23) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StreamFlow 提出一种高效的视觉记忆框架，通过动力学感知的中期记忆过滤时序冗余、潜在长期记忆整合历史视频内容，并利用注意力引导的检索机制在推理时动态注入相关视觉潜在表征，实现流式视频理解，在 StreamingBench 上达到 67.73% 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解要求 MLLM 在严格因果性和有限记忆约束下保留持续流中的相关证据。现有方法存在局限：基于模型的方法需要侵入式骨干网络更新，基于记忆的方法在时序冗余内容上耗费大量视觉编码计算，且对视觉历史的访问方式僵化。

Method: StreamFlow 结合两种记忆机制：一是轻量级、动力学感知的中期记忆，在视觉编码前过滤时序冗余；二是潜在长期记忆，将历史视频内容整合为可供后续推理访问的视觉潜在表征。生成阶段通过注意力引导的检索机制，在模型对视觉证据依赖减弱时注入相关视觉潜在表征。

Result: 在 StreamingBench 上达到 67.73% 的整体准确率，取得流式视频理解 SOTA；在离线长视频基准上也表现优异。相比 vanilla 设置，视觉注意力分数（VAS）提升 59.1%，端到端延迟和峰值内存分别降低 50.4% 和 21.1%。

Conclusion: StreamFlow 通过动态、按需访问历史视觉信息的记忆框架，在保持高效推理的同时显著提升流式视频理解的视觉接地能力与准确性，验证了记忆管理与检索机制在流式视频理解中的有效性。

Classification reason: 论文聚焦流式视频理解，属于视频 LLM 与长视频理解范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题"视频理解与时序推理"高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong efficiency-focused streaming video understanding framework with latent memory and retrieval, but not directly aligned with top-priority continuous/multimodal generation or world-model topics. Signals: streaming video understanding, efficient memory framework, latent long-term memory, attention-guided retrieval, StreamingBench SOTA

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to preserve relevant evidence from continuously evolving streams under strict causality and bounded memory. Yet existing paradigms remain limited: model-based methods require intrusive backbone updates, while memory-based methods expend substantial visual-encoding computation on temporally redundant content and rely on rigid access to visual history. To address these limitations, we introduce StreamFlow, an efficient visual memory framework that enables dynamic, on-demand access to historical visual information. StreamFlow combines a lightweight, dynamics-aware mid-term memory that filters temporal redundancy before visual encoding with a latent long-term memory that consolidates historical video content into visual latents accessible to subsequent reasoning. During generation, an attention-guided retrieval mechanism injects relevant visual latents when the model's reliance on visual evidence weakens. StreamFlow achieves state-of-the-art streaming video understanding performance, reaching 67.73% overall accuracy on StreamingBench, while also delivering strong performance on offline long-video benchmarks. Relative to the vanilla setting, it improves the visual attention score (VAS) by 59.1% while reducing end-to-end latency and peak memory by 50.4% and 21.1%, respectively, enabling more visually grounded and efficient reasoning.

</details>
